| <a id="api-a4467417a82bd085"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenGamepad(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23910) | Open a gamepad for use. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseGamepad @sa SDL_IsGamepad<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：a gamepad identifier or NULL if an error occurred; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-be60741ae07cc05d"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenHaptic(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21120) | Open a haptic device for use. The index passed as an argument refers to the N'th haptic device on this system. When opening a haptic device, its gain will be set to maximum and autocenter will be disabled. To modify these values use SDL_SetHapticGain() and SDL_SetHapticAutocenter(). @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseHaptic @sa SDL_GetHaptics @sa SDL_OpenHapticFromJoystick @sa SDL_OpenHapticFromMouse @sa SDL_SetHapticAutocenter @sa SDL_SetHapticGain<br>`instance_id`：the haptic device instance ID.<br>返回：the device identifier or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8e4e1fe2d1bc0748"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenHapticFromJoystick(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36360) | Open a haptic device for use from a joystick device. You must still close the haptic device separately. It will not be closed with the joystick. When opened from a joystick you should first close the haptic device before closing the joystick device. If not, on some implementations the haptic device will also get unallocated and you'll be unable to use force feedback on that device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseHaptic @sa SDL_IsJoystickHaptic<br>`joystick`：the SDL_Joystick to create a haptic device from.<br>返回：a valid haptic device identifier on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1b97ae2fc3098dea"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenHapticFromMouse()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24165) | Try to open a haptic device from the current mouse. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseHaptic @sa SDL_IsMouseHaptic<br>返回：the haptic device identifier or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6d26459970836bd1"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStreamInterface* iface, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41885) | Create a custom SDL_IOStream. Applications do not need to use this function unless they are providing their own SDL_IOStream implementation. If you just need an SDL_IOStream to read/write a common data source, you should use the built-in implementations in SDL, like SDL_IOFromFile() or SDL_IOFromMem(), etc. This function makes a copy of `iface` and the caller does not need to keep it around after this call. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseIO @sa SDL_INIT_INTERFACE @sa SDL_IOFromConstMem @sa SDL_IOFromFile @sa SDL_IOFromMem<br>`iface`：the interface that implements this SDL_IOStream, initialized using SDL_INIT_INTERFACE().<br>`userdata`：the pointer that will be passed to the interface functions.<br>返回：a pointer to the allocated memory on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a1115200fbde318f"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenJoystick(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31231) | Open a joystick for use. The joystick subsystem must be initialized before a joystick can be opened for use. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseJoystick<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>返回：a joystick identifier or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e2fcedb74418e31e"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensor* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenSensor(uint instance_id)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48897) | Open a sensor for use. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`instance_id`：the sensor instance ID.<br>返回：an SDL_Sensor object or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0f195718064b095c"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorage* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenStorage(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorageInterface* iface, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28149) | Opens up a container using a client-provided storage interface. Applications do not need to use this function unless they are providing their own SDL_Storage implementation. If you just need an SDL_Storage, you should use the built-in implementations in SDL, like SDL_OpenTitleStorage() or SDL_OpenUserStorage(). This function makes a copy of `iface` and the caller does not need to keep it around after this call. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseStorage @sa SDL_GetStorageFileSize @sa SDL_GetStorageSpaceRemaining @sa SDL_INIT_INTERFACE @sa SDL_ReadStorageFile @sa SDL_StorageReady @sa SDL_WriteStorageFile<br>`iface`：the interface that implements this storage, initialized using SDL_INIT_INTERFACE().<br>`userdata`：the pointer that will be passed to the interface functions.<br>返回：a storage container on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e2b3fb5589f6ba07"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorage* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenTitleStorage(sbyte* @override, uint props)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51832) | Opens up a read-only container for the application's filesystem. By default, SDL_OpenTitleStorage uses the generic storage implementation. When the path override is not provided, the generic implementation will use the output of SDL_GetBasePath as the base path. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseStorage @sa SDL_GetStorageFileSize @sa SDL_OpenUserStorage @sa SDL_ReadStorageFile<br>`override`：a path to override the backend's default title root.<br>`props`：a property list that may contain backend-specific information.<br>返回：a title storage container on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-24cf0a7d82eb342a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenUrl(sbyte* url)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37869) | Open a URL/URI in the browser or other appropriate external application. Open a URL in a separate, system-provided application. How this works will vary wildly depending on the platform. This will likely launch what makes sense to handle a specific URL's protocol (a web browser for `http://`, etc), but it might also be able to launch file managers for directories and other things. What happens when you open a URL varies wildly as well: your game window may lose focus (and may or may not lose focus if your game was fullscreen or grabbing input at the time). On mobile devices, your app will likely move to the background or your process might be paused. Any given platform may or may not handle a given URL. If this is unimplemented (or simply unavailable) for a platform, this will fail with an error. A successful result does not mean the URL loaded, just that we launched _something_ to handle it (or at least believe we did). All this to say: this function can be useful, but you should definitely test it on every platform you target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`url`：a valid URL/URI to open. Use `file:///full/path/to/file` for local files, if supported.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3b7fef1a935fa12e"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorage* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OpenUserStorage(sbyte* org, sbyte* app, uint props)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27992) | Opens up a container for a user's unique read/write filesystem. While title storage can generally be kept open throughout runtime, user storage should only be opened when the client is ready to read/write files. This allows the backend to properly batch file operations and flush them when the container has been closed; ensuring safe and optimal save I/O. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CloseStorage @sa SDL_GetStorageFileSize @sa SDL_GetStorageSpaceRemaining @sa SDL_OpenTitleStorage @sa SDL_ReadStorageFile @sa SDL_StorageReady @sa SDL_WriteStorageFile<br>`org`：the name of your organization.<br>`app`：the name of your application.<br>`props`：a property list that may contain backend-specific information.<br>返回：a user storage container on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-83acf8dcd6af5f3e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.OutOfMemory()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42623) | Set an error indicating that memory allocation failed. This function does not do any memory allocation. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>返回：false. |
| <a id="api-b4dd2516bd536edc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PauseAudioDevice(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35232) | Use this function to pause audio playback on a specified device. This function pauses audio processing for a given device. Any bound audio streams will not progress, and no audio will be generated. Pausing one device does not prevent other unpaused devices from running. Unlike in SDL2, audio devices start in an _unpaused_ state, since an app has to bind a stream before any audio will flow. Pausing a paused device is a legal no-op. Pausing a device can be useful to halt all audio without unbinding all the audio streams. This might be useful while a game is paused, or a level is loading, etc. Physical devices can not be paused or unpaused, only logical devices created through SDL_OpenAudioDevice() can be. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ResumeAudioDevice @sa SDL_AudioDevicePaused<br>`devid`：a device opened by SDL_OpenAudioDevice().<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f282964956231e2e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PauseAudioStreamDevice(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45308) | Use this function to pause audio playback on the audio device associated with an audio stream. This function pauses audio processing for a given device. Any bound audio streams will not progress, and no audio will be generated. Pausing one device does not prevent other unpaused devices from running. Pausing a device can be useful to halt all audio without unbinding all the audio streams. This might be useful while a game is paused, or a level is loading, etc. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ResumeAudioStreamDevice<br>`stream`：the audio stream associated with the audio device to pause.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c225ff2c1a4ce698"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PauseHaptic(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19065) | Pause a haptic device. Device must support the `SDL_HAPTIC_PAUSE` feature. Call SDL_ResumeHaptic() to resume playback. Do not modify the effects nor add new ones while the device is paused. That can cause all sorts of weird errors. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ResumeHaptic<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to pause.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8871c1a3ca8ef13f"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PeepEvents(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEvent* events, int numevents, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEventAction action, uint minType, uint maxType)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49297) | Check the event queue for messages and optionally return them. `action` may be any of the following: - `SDL_ADDEVENT`: up to `numevents` events will be added to the back of the event queue. - `SDL_PEEKEVENT`: `numevents` events at the front of the event queue, within the specified minimum and maximum type, will be returned to the caller and will _not_ be removed from the queue. If you pass NULL for `events`, then `numevents` is ignored and the total number of matching events will be returned. - `SDL_GETEVENT`: up to `numevents` events at the front of the event queue, within the specified minimum and maximum type, will be returned to the caller and will be removed from the queue. You may have to call SDL_PumpEvents() before calling this function. Otherwise, the events may not be ready to be filtered when you call SDL_PeepEvents(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PollEvent @sa SDL_PumpEvents @sa SDL_PushEvent<br>`events`：destination buffer for the retrieved events, may be NULL to leave the events in the queue and return the number of events that would have been stored.<br>`numevents`：if action is SDL_ADDEVENT, the number of events to add back to the event queue; if action is SDL_PEEKEVENT or SDL_GETEVENT, the maximum number of events to retrieve.<br>`action`：action to take; see Remarks for details.<br>`minType`：minimum value of the event type to be considered; SDL_EVENT_FIRST is a safe choice.<br>`maxType`：maximum value of the event type to be considered; SDL_EVENT_LAST is a safe choice.<br>返回：the number of events actually stored or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1ad73e14f63c17ca"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PlayHapticRumble(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic, float strength, uint length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39062) | Run a simple rumble effect on a haptic device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_InitHapticRumble @sa SDL_StopHapticRumble<br>`haptic`：the haptic device to play the rumble effect on.<br>`strength`：strength of the rumble to play as a 0-1 float value.<br>`length`：length of the rumble to play in milliseconds.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f46c511c177e072b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PollEvent(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEvent* @event)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34823) | Poll for currently pending events. If `event` is not NULL, the next event is removed from the queue and stored in the SDL_Event structure pointed to by `event`. If `event` is NULL, it simply returns true if there is an event in the queue, but will not remove it from the queue. As this function may implicitly call SDL_PumpEvents(), you can only call this function in the thread that initialized the video subsystem. SDL_PollEvent() is the favored way of receiving system events since it can be done from the main loop and does not suspend the main loop while waiting on an event to be posted. The common practice is to fully process the event queue once every frame, usually as a first step before updating the game's state: ```c while (game_is_still_running) { SDL_Event event; while (SDL_PollEvent( &event )) { // poll until all events are handled! // decide what to do with this event. } // update game state, draw the current frame } ``` Note that Windows (and possibly other platforms) has a quirk about how it handles events while dragging/resizing a window, which can cause this function to block for significant amounts of time. Technical explanations and solutions are discussed on the wiki: https://wiki.libsdl.org/SDL3/AppFreezeDuringDrag @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PushEvent @sa SDL_WaitEvent @sa SDL_WaitEventTimeout<br>`event`：the SDL_Event structure to be filled with the next event from the queue, or NULL.<br>返回：true if this got an event or false if there are none available. |
| <a id="api-075e608980551fcc"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PopGpuDebugGroup(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCommandBuffer* command_buffer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35748) | Ends the most-recently pushed debug group. On Direct3D 12, using SDL_PopGPUDebugGroup requires WinPixEventRuntime.dll to be in your PATH or in the same directory as your executable. See [here](https://devblogs.microsoft.com/pix/winpixeventruntime/) for instructions on how to obtain it. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PushGPUDebugGroup<br>`command_buffer`：a command buffer. |
| <a id="api-a35e701d8a4cc3e9"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Pow(double x, double y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39214) | Raise `x` to the power `y` Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF` Range: `-INF < = z < = INF` If `y` is the base of the natural logarithm (e), consider using SDL_exp instead. This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_powf for single-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_powf @sa SDL_exp @sa SDL_log<br>`x`：the base.<br>`y`：the exponent.<br>返回：`x` raised to the power `y`. |
| <a id="api-3fece54030a35a51"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Powf(float x, float y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28245) | Raise `x` to the power `y` Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `-INF < = y < = INF` Range: `-INF < = z < = INF` If `y` is the base of the natural logarithm (e), consider using SDL_exp instead. This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_pow for double-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_pow @sa SDL_expf @sa SDL_logf<br>`x`：the base.<br>`y`：the exponent.<br>返回：`x` raised to the power `y`. |
| <a id="api-c4324a4731a51cfc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PremultiplyAlpha(int width, int height, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat src_format, void* src, int src_pitch, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPixelFormat dst_format, void* dst, int dst_pitch, byte linear)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43962) | Premultiply the alpha on a block of pixels. This is safe to use with src == dst, but not for other overlapping areas. @threadsafety The same destination pixels should not be used from two threads at once. It is safe to use the same source pixels from multiple threads. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`width`：the width of the block to convert, in pixels.<br>`height`：the height of the block to convert, in pixels.<br>`src_format`：an SDL_PixelFormat value of the `src` pixels format.<br>`src`：a pointer to the source pixels.<br>`src_pitch`：the pitch of the source pixels, in bytes.<br>`dst_format`：an SDL_PixelFormat value of the `dst` pixels format.<br>`dst`：a pointer to be filled in with premultiplied pixel data.<br>`dst_pitch`：the pitch of the destination pixels, in bytes.<br>`linear`：true to convert from sRGB to linear space for the alpha multiplication, false to do multiplication in sRGB space.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ab8f458d16c49b7f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PremultiplySurfaceAlpha(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, byte linear)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35418) | Premultiply the alpha in a surface. This is safe to use with src == dst, but not for other overlapping areas. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`surface`：the surface to modify.<br>`linear`：true to convert from sRGB to linear space for the alpha multiplication, false to do multiplication in sRGB space.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3bc375344ad37880"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PumpEvents()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38156) | Pump the event loop, gathering events from the input devices. This function updates the event queue and internal input device state. SDL_PumpEvents() gathers all the pending input information from devices and places it in the event queue. Without calls to SDL_PumpEvents() no events would ever be placed on the queue. Often the need for calls to SDL_PumpEvents() is hidden from the user since SDL_PollEvent() and SDL_WaitEvent() implicitly call SDL_PumpEvents(). However, if you are not polling or waiting for events (e.g. you are filtering them), then you must call SDL_PumpEvents() to force an event queue update. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PollEvent @sa SDL_WaitEvent |
| <a id="api-7d3bdaab7fca036f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PushEvent(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEvent* @event)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47405) | Add an event to the event queue. The event queue can actually be used as a two way communication channel. Not only can events be read from the queue, but the user can also push their own events onto it. `event` is a pointer to the event structure you wish to push onto the queue. The event is copied into the queue, and the caller may dispose of the memory pointed to after SDL_PushEvent() returns. Note: Pushing device input events onto the queue doesn't modify the state of the device within SDL. Note: Events pushed onto the queue with SDL_PushEvent() get passed through the event filter but events added with SDL_PeepEvents() do not. For pushing application-specific events, please use SDL_RegisterEvents() to get an event type that does not conflict with other code that also wants its own custom event types. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PeepEvents @sa SDL_PollEvent @sa SDL_RegisterEvents<br>`event`：the SDL_Event to be added to the queue.<br>返回：true on success, false if the event was filtered or on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. A common reason for error is the event queue being full. |
| <a id="api-5f10af0448de1419"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PushGpuComputeUniformData(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCommandBuffer* command_buffer, uint slot_index, void* data, uint length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25904) | Pushes data to a uniform slot on the command buffer. Subsequent draw calls in this command buffer will use this uniform data. The data being pushed must respect std140 layout conventions. In practical terms this means you must ensure that vec3 and vec4 fields are 16-byte aligned. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`command_buffer`：a command buffer.<br>`slot_index`：the uniform slot to push data to.<br>`data`：client data to write.<br>`length`：the length of the data to write. |
| <a id="api-8f2f1697c40142d4"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PushGpuDebugGroup(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCommandBuffer* command_buffer, sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41990) | Begins a debug group with an arbitrary name. Used for denoting groups of calls when viewing the command buffer callstream in a graphics debugging tool. Each call to SDL_PushGPUDebugGroup must have a corresponding call to SDL_PopGPUDebugGroup. On Direct3D 12, using SDL_PushGPUDebugGroup requires WinPixEventRuntime.dll to be in your PATH or in the same directory as your executable. See [here](https://devblogs.microsoft.com/pix/winpixeventruntime/) for instructions on how to obtain it. On some backends (e.g. Metal), pushing a debug group during a render/blit/compute pass will create a group that is scoped to the native pass rather than the command buffer. For best results, if you push a debug group during a pass, always pop it in the same pass. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PopGPUDebugGroup<br>`command_buffer`：a command buffer.<br>`name`：a UTF-8 string constant that names the group. |
| <a id="api-1dcefa99bb2bdd9d"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PushGpuFragmentUniformData(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCommandBuffer* command_buffer, uint slot_index, void* data, uint length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21914) | Pushes data to a fragment uniform slot on the command buffer. Subsequent draw calls in this command buffer will use this uniform data. The data being pushed must respect std140 layout conventions. In practical terms this means you must ensure that vec3 and vec4 fields are 16-byte aligned. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`command_buffer`：a command buffer.<br>`slot_index`：the fragment uniform slot to push data to.<br>`data`：client data to write.<br>`length`：the length of the data to write. |
| <a id="api-f2409ea1e338d8cf"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PushGpuVertexUniformData(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCommandBuffer* command_buffer, uint slot_index, void* data, uint length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39836) | Pushes data to a vertex uniform slot on the command buffer. Subsequent draw calls in this command buffer will use this uniform data. The data being pushed must respect std140 layout conventions. In practical terms this means you must ensure that vec3 and vec4 fields are 16-byte aligned. For detailed information about accessing uniform data from a shader, please refer to SDL_CreateGPUShader. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`command_buffer`：a command buffer.<br>`slot_index`：the vertex uniform slot to push data to.<br>`data`：client data to write.<br>`length`：the length of the data to write. |
| <a id="api-aa663b1a79ddd1d5"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PutAudioStreamData(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, void* buf, int len)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42685) | Add data to the stream. This data must match the format/channels/samplerate specified in the latest call to SDL_SetAudioStreamFormat, or the format specified when creating the stream if it hasn't been changed. Note that this call simply copies the unconverted data for later. This is different than SDL2, where data was converted during the Put call and the Get call would just dequeue the previously-converted data. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, but if the stream has a callback set, the caller might need to manage extra locking. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ClearAudioStream @sa SDL_FlushAudioStream @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamData @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamQueued<br>`stream`：the stream the audio data is being added to.<br>`buf`：a pointer to the audio data to add.<br>`len`：the number of bytes to write to the stream.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ea65c53691959fe3"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PutAudioStreamDataNoCopy(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, void* buf, int len, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, void*, int, void> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42442) | Add external data to an audio stream without copying it. Unlike SDL_PutAudioStreamData(), this function does not make a copy of the provided data, instead storing the provided pointer. This means that the put operation does not need to allocate and copy the data, but the original data must remain available until the stream is done with it, either by being read from the stream in its entirety, or a call to SDL_ClearAudioStream() or SDL_DestroyAudioStream(). The data must match the format/channels/samplerate specified in the latest call to SDL_SetAudioStreamFormat, or the format specified when creating the stream if it hasn't been changed. An optional callback may be provided, which is called when the stream no longer needs the data. Once this callback fires, the stream will not access the data again. This callback will fire for any reason the data is no longer needed, including clearing or destroying the stream. Note that there is still an allocation to store tracking information, so this function is more efficient for larger blocks of data. If you're planning to put a few samples at a time, it will be more efficient to use SDL_PutAudioStreamData(), which allocates and buffers in blocks. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, but if the stream has a callback set, the caller might need to manage extra locking. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_ClearAudioStream @sa SDL_FlushAudioStream @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamData @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamQueued<br>`stream`：the stream the audio data is being added to.<br>`buf`：a pointer to the audio data to add.<br>`len`：the number of bytes to add to the stream.<br>`callback`：the callback function to call when the data is no longer needed by the stream. May be NULL.<br>`userdata`：an opaque pointer provided to the callback for its own personal use.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1047c813c03fcdd8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.PutAudioStreamPlanarData(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, void** channel_buffers, int num_channels, int num_samples)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35705) | Add data to the stream with each channel in a separate array. This data must match the format/channels/samplerate specified in the latest call to SDL_SetAudioStreamFormat, or the format specified when creating the stream if it hasn't been changed. The data will be interleaved and queued. Note that SDL_AudioStream only operates on interleaved data, so this is simply a convenience function for easily queueing data from sources that provide separate arrays. There is no equivalent function to retrieve planar data. The arrays in `channel_buffers` are ordered as they are to be interleaved; the first array will be the first sample in the interleaved data. Any individual array may be NULL; in this case, silence will be interleaved for that channel. `num_channels` specifies how many arrays are in `channel_buffers`. This can be used as a safety to prevent overflow, in case the stream format has changed elsewhere. If more channels are specified than the current input spec, they are ignored. If less channels are specified, the missing arrays are treated as if they are NULL (silence is written to those channels). If the count is -1, SDL will assume the array count matches the current input spec. Note that `num_samples` is the number of _samples per array_. This can also be thought of as the number of _sample frames_ to be queued. A value of 1 with stereo arrays will queue two samples to the stream. This is different than SDL_PutAudioStreamData, which wants the size of a single array in bytes. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, but if the stream has a callback set, the caller might need to manage extra locking. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_ClearAudioStream @sa SDL_FlushAudioStream @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamData @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamQueued<br>`stream`：the stream the audio data is being added to.<br>`channel_buffers`：a pointer to an array of arrays, one array per channel.<br>`num_channels`：the number of arrays in `channel_buffers` or -1.<br>`num_samples`：the number of _samples_ per array to write to the stream.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-210a6f72808d8656"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Qsort(void* @base, ulong nmemb, ulong size, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, void*, int> compare)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46332) | Sort an array. For example: ```c typedef struct { int key; const char *string; } data; int SDLCALL compare(const void *a, const void *b) { const data *A = (const data *)a; const data *B = (const data *)b; if (A->n < B->n) { return -1; } else if (B->n < A->n) { return 1; } else { return 0; } } data values[] = { { 3, "third" }, { 1, "first" }, { 2, "second" } }; SDL_qsort(values, SDL_arraysize(values), sizeof(values[0]), compare); ``` @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_bsearch @sa SDL_qsort_r<br>`base`：a pointer to the start of the array.<br>`nmemb`：the number of elements in the array.<br>`size`：the size of the elements in the array.<br>`compare`：a function used to compare elements in the array. |
| <a id="api-9da9d4edcca73ec6"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.QsortR(void* @base, ulong nmemb, ulong size, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, void*, void*, int> compare, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37665) | Sort an array, passing a userdata pointer to the compare function. For example: ```c typedef enum { sort_increasing, sort_decreasing, } sort_method; typedef struct { int key; const char *string; } data; int SDLCALL compare(const void *userdata, const void *a, const void *b) { sort_method method = (sort_method)(uintptr_t)userdata; const data *A = (const data *)a; const data *B = (const data *)b; if (A->key < B->key) { return (method == sort_increasing) ? -1 : 1; } else if (B->key < A->key) { return (method == sort_increasing) ? 1 : -1; } else { return 0; } } data values[] = { { 3, "third" }, { 1, "first" }, { 2, "second" } }; SDL_qsort_r(values, SDL_arraysize(values), sizeof(values[0]), compare, (const void *)(uintptr_t)sort_increasing); ``` @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_bsearch_r @sa SDL_qsort<br>`base`：a pointer to the start of the array.<br>`nmemb`：the number of elements in the array.<br>`size`：the size of the elements in the array.<br>`compare`：a function used to compare elements in the array.<br>`userdata`：a pointer to pass to the compare function. |
| <a id="api-a8bce8254f7095fe"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.QueryGpuFence(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUFence* fence)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29055) | Checks the status of a fence. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SubmitGPUCommandBufferAndAcquireFence<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`fence`：a fence.<br>返回：true if the fence is signaled, false if it is not. |
| <a id="api-b3f48bafe4cb36cc"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Quit()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32398) | Clean up all initialized subsystems. You should call this function even if you have already shutdown each initialized subsystem with SDL_QuitSubSystem(). It is safe to call this function even in the case of errors in initialization. You can use this function with atexit() to ensure that it is run when your application is shutdown, but it is not wise to do this from a library or other dynamically loaded code. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_Init @sa SDL_QuitSubSystem |
| <a id="api-959d16a81e36030c"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.QuitSubSystem(uint flags)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26597) | Shut down specific SDL subsystems. You still need to call SDL_Quit() even if you close all open subsystems with SDL_QuitSubSystem(). @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_InitSubSystem @sa SDL_Quit<br>`flags`：any of the flags used by SDL_Init(); see SDL_Init for details. |
| <a id="api-c445bafb584183f6"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RaiseWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24597) | Request that a window be raised above other windows and gain the input focus. The result of this request is subject to desktop window manager policy, particularly if raising the requested window would result in stealing focus from another application. If the window is successfully raised and gains input focus, an SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_FOCUS_GAINED event will be emitted, and the window will have the SDL_WINDOW_INPUT_FOCUS flag set. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to raise.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9fa256ae5b562819"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Rand(int n)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47369) | Generate a pseudo-random number less than n for positive n The method used is faster and of better quality than `rand() % n`. Odds are roughly 99.9% even for n = 1 million. Evenness is better for smaller n, and much worse as n gets bigger. Example: to simulate a d6 use `SDL_rand(6) + 1` The +1 converts 0..5 to 1..6 If you want to generate a pseudo-random number in the full range of Sint32, you should use: (Sint32)SDL_rand_bits() If you want reproducible output, be sure to initialize with SDL_srand() first. There are no guarantees as to the quality of the random sequence produced, and this should not be used for security (cryptography, passwords) or where money is on the line (loot-boxes, casinos). There are many random number libraries available with different characteristics and you should pick one of those to meet any serious needs. @threadsafety All calls should be made from a single thread @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_srand @sa SDL_randf<br>`n`：the number of possible outcomes. n must be positive.<br>返回：a random value in the range of [0 .. n-1]. |
| <a id="api-16ecb8dc9c6de279"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RandBits()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50610) | Generate 32 pseudo-random bits. You likely want to use SDL_rand() to get a pseudo-random number instead. There are no guarantees as to the quality of the random sequence produced, and this should not be used for security (cryptography, passwords) or where money is on the line (loot-boxes, casinos). There are many random number libraries available with different characteristics and you should pick one of those to meet any serious needs. @threadsafety All calls should be made from a single thread @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_rand @sa SDL_randf @sa SDL_srand<br>返回：a random value in the range of [0-SDL_MAX_UINT32]. |
| <a id="api-c461eb4f40d22c65"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RandBitsR(ulong* state)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17485) | Generate 32 pseudo-random bits. You likely want to use SDL_rand_r() to get a pseudo-random number instead. There are no guarantees as to the quality of the random sequence produced, and this should not be used for security (cryptography, passwords) or where money is on the line (loot-boxes, casinos). There are many random number libraries available with different characteristics and you should pick one of those to meet any serious needs. @threadsafety This function is thread-safe, as long as the state pointer isn't shared between threads. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_rand_r @sa SDL_randf_r<br>`state`：a pointer to the current random number state, this may not be NULL.<br>返回：a random value in the range of [0-SDL_MAX_UINT32]. |
| <a id="api-12dc165977115a1f"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RandR(ulong* state, int n)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36660) | Generate a pseudo-random number less than n for positive n The method used is faster and of better quality than `rand() % n`. Odds are roughly 99.9% even for n = 1 million. Evenness is better for smaller n, and much worse as n gets bigger. Example: to simulate a d6 use `SDL_rand_r(state, 6) + 1` The +1 converts 0..5 to 1..6 If you want to generate a pseudo-random number in the full range of Sint32, you should use: (Sint32)SDL_rand_bits_r(state) There are no guarantees as to the quality of the random sequence produced, and this should not be used for security (cryptography, passwords) or where money is on the line (loot-boxes, casinos). There are many random number libraries available with different characteristics and you should pick one of those to meet any serious needs. @threadsafety This function is thread-safe, as long as the state pointer isn't shared between threads. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_rand @sa SDL_rand_bits_r @sa SDL_randf_r<br>`state`：a pointer to the current random number state, this may not be NULL.<br>`n`：the number of possible outcomes. n must be positive.<br>返回：a random value in the range of [0 .. n-1]. |

There are many random number libraries available with different characteristics and you should pick one of those to meet any serious needs. @threadsafety All calls should be made from a single thread @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_srand @sa SDL_rand<br>返回：a random value in the range of [0.0, 1.0). |
| <a id="api-f26a5314e11f81c0"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RandfR(ulong* state)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25679) | Generate a uniform pseudo-random floating point number less than 1.0 If you want reproducible output, be sure to initialize with SDL_srand() first. There are no guarantees as to the quality of the random sequence produced, and this should not be used for security (cryptography, passwords) or where money is on the line (loot-boxes, casinos). There are many random number libraries available with different characteristics and you should pick one of those to meet any serious needs. @threadsafety This function is thread-safe, as long as the state pointer isn't shared between threads. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_rand_bits_r @sa SDL_rand_r @sa SDL_randf<br>`state`：a pointer to the current random number state, this may not be NULL.<br>返回：a random value in the range of [0.0, 1.0). |
| <a id="api-68a90978cb7a3b73"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ReadAsyncIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIO* asyncio, void* ptr, ulong offset, ulong size, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIOQueue* queue, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43647) | Start an async read. This function reads up to `size` bytes from `offset` position in the data source to the area pointed at by `ptr`. This function may read less bytes than requested. This function returns as quickly as possible; it does not wait for the read to complete. On a successful return, this work will continue in the background. If the work begins, even failure is asynchronous: a failing return value from this function only means the work couldn't start at all. `ptr` must remain available until the work is done, and may be accessed by the system at any time until then. Do not allocate it on the stack, as this might take longer than the life of the calling function to complete! An SDL_AsyncIOQueue must be specified. The newly-created task will be added to it when it completes its work. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_WriteAsyncIO @sa SDL_CreateAsyncIOQueue<br>`asyncio`：a pointer to an SDL_AsyncIO structure.<br>`ptr`：a pointer to a buffer to read data into.<br>`offset`：the position to start reading in the data source.<br>`size`：the number of bytes to read from the data source.<br>`queue`：a queue to add the new SDL_AsyncIO to.<br>`userdata`：an app-defined pointer that will be provided with the task results.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-41548bd8a3f87726"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ReadIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* context, void* ptr, ulong size)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21766) | Read from a data source. This function reads up `size` bytes from the data source to the area pointed at by `ptr`. This function may read less bytes than requested. This function will return zero when the data stream is completely read, and SDL_GetIOStatus() will return SDL_IO_STATUS_EOF. If zero is returned and the stream is not at EOF, SDL_GetIOStatus() will return a different error value and SDL_GetError() will offer a human-readable message. A request for zero bytes on a valid stream will return zero immediately without accessing the stream, so the stream status (EOF, err, etc) will not change. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_WriteIO @sa SDL_GetIOStatus<br>`context`：a pointer to an SDL_IOStream structure.<br>`ptr`：a pointer to a buffer to read data into.<br>`size`：the number of bytes to read from the data source.<br>返回：the number of bytes read, or 0 on end of file or other failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a0c4fc38b686cfe3"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ReadProcess(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLProcess* process, ulong* datasize, int* exitcode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38907) | Read all the output from a process. If a process was created with I/O enabled, you can use this function to read the output. This function blocks until the process is complete, capturing all output, and providing the process exit code. The data is allocated with a zero byte at the end (null terminated) for convenience. This extra byte is not included in the value reported via `datasize`. The data should be freed with SDL_free(). @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateProcess @sa SDL_CreateProcessWithProperties @sa SDL_DestroyProcess<br>`process`：The process to read.<br>`datasize`：a pointer filled in with the number of bytes read, may be NULL.<br>`exitcode`：a pointer filled in with the process exit code if the process has exited, may be NULL.<br>返回：the data or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-cea53ae88b2ecbcc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ReadS16Be(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* src, short* value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40205) | Use this function to read 16 bits of big-endian data from an SDL_IOStream and return in native format. SDL byteswaps the data only if necessary, so the data returned will be in the native byte order. This function will return false when the data stream is completely read, and SDL_GetIOStatus() will return SDL_IO_STATUS_EOF. If false is returned and the stream is not at EOF, SDL_GetIOStatus() will return a different error value and SDL_GetError() will offer a human-readable message. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`src`：the stream from which to read data.<br>`value`：a pointer filled in with the data read.<br>返回：true on successful read or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |

This function prioritizes correctness over speed: it is suitable for unit tests, but is not intended for use in a game engine. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`surface`：the surface to read.<br>`x`：the horizontal coordinate, 0 < = x < width.<br>`y`：the vertical coordinate, 0 < = y < height.<br>`r`：a pointer filled in with the red channel, normally in the range 0-1, or NULL to ignore this channel.<br>`g`：a pointer filled in with the green channel, normally in the range 0-1, or NULL to ignore this channel.<br>`b`：a pointer filled in with the blue channel, normally in the range 0-1, or NULL to ignore this channel.<br>`a`：a pointer filled in with the alpha channel, normally in the range 0-1, or NULL to ignore this channel.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f61b5e0034503ede"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ReadU16Be(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* src, ushort* value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28943) | Use this function to read 16 bits of big-endian data from an SDL_IOStream and return in native format. SDL byteswaps the data only if necessary, so the data returned will be in the native byte order. This function will return false when the data stream is completely read, and SDL_GetIOStatus() will return SDL_IO_STATUS_EOF. If false is returned and the stream is not at EOF, SDL_GetIOStatus() will return a different error value and SDL_GetError() will offer a human-readable message. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`src`：the stream from which to read data.<br>`value`：a pointer filled in with the data read.<br>返回：true on successful read or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |

| <a id="api-1495096a76b2f285"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Realloc(void* mem, ulong size)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20955) | Change the size of allocated memory. The memory returned by this function must be freed with SDL_free(). If `size` is 0, it will be set to 1. Note that this is unlike some other C runtime `realloc` implementations, which may treat `realloc(mem, 0)` the same way as `free(mem)`. If `mem` is NULL, the behavior of this function is equivalent to SDL_malloc(). Otherwise, the function can have one of three possible outcomes: - If it returns the same pointer as `mem`, it means that `mem` was resized in place without freeing. - If it returns a different non-NULL pointer, it means that `mem` was freed and cannot be dereferenced anymore. - If it returns NULL (indicating failure), then `mem` will remain valid and must still be freed with SDL_free(). If the allocation is successfully resized, the returned pointer is guaranteed to be aligned to either the *fundamental alignment* (`alignof(max_align_t)` in C11 and later) or `2 * sizeof(void *)`, whichever is smaller. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_free @sa SDL_malloc @sa SDL_calloc<br>`mem`：a pointer to allocated memory to reallocate, or NULL.<br>`size`：the new size of the memory.<br>返回：a pointer to the newly allocated memory, or NULL if allocation failed. |
| <a id="api-c7d738e16fb63f21"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RegisterEvents(int numevents)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30333) | Allocate a set of user-defined events, and return the beginning event number for that set of events. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PushEvent<br>`numevents`：the number of events to be allocated.<br>返回：the beginning event number, or 0 if numevents is invalid or if there are not enough user-defined events left. |
| <a id="api-78982c7d1b792710"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ReleaseCameraFrame(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCamera* camera, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* frame)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30363) | Release a frame of video acquired from a camera. Let the back-end reuse the internal buffer for camera. This function _must_ be called only on surface objects returned by SDL_AcquireCameraFrame(). This function should be called as quickly as possible after acquisition, as SDL keeps a small FIFO queue of surfaces for video frames; if surfaces aren't released in a timely manner, SDL may drop upcoming video frames from the camera. If the app needs to keep the surface for a significant time, they should make a copy of it and release the original. The app should not use the surface again after calling this function; assume the surface is freed and the pointer is invalid. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AcquireCameraFrame<br>`camera`：opened camera device.<br>`frame`：the video frame surface to release. |

| <a id="api-9fe738860fe1a869"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenamePath(sbyte* oldpath, sbyte* newpath)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42896) | Rename a file or directory. If the file at `newpath` already exists, it will be replaced. Note that this will not copy files across filesystems/drives/volumes, as that is a much more complicated (and possibly time-consuming) operation. Which is to say, if this function fails, SDL_CopyFile() to a temporary file in the same directory as `newpath`, then SDL_RenamePath() from the temporary file to `newpath` and SDL_RemovePath() on `oldpath` might work for files. Renaming a non-empty directory across filesystems is dramatically more complex, however. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`oldpath`：the old path.<br>`newpath`：the new path.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7a3c0bcba1aa8db4"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenameStoragePath(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorage* storage, sbyte* oldpath, sbyte* newpath)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50752) | Rename a file or directory in a writable storage container. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_StorageReady<br>`storage`：a storage container.<br>`oldpath`：the old path.<br>`newpath`：the new path.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ef8794a09530b117"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderClear(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31054) | Clear the current rendering target with the drawing color. This function clears the entire rendering target, ignoring the viewport and the clip rectangle. Note, that clearing will also set/fill all pixels of the rendering target to current renderer draw color, so make sure to invoke SDL_SetRenderDrawColor() when needed. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderDrawColor<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |

Each render target has its own clip rectangle. This function checks the cliprect for the current render target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderClipRect @sa SDL_SetRenderClipRect<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>返回：true if clipping is enabled or false if not; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-90a100d5e8459801"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderCoordinatesFromWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float window_x, float window_y, float* x, float* y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38869) | Get a point in render coordinates when given a point in window coordinates. This takes into account several states: - The window dimensions. - The logical presentation settings (SDL_SetRenderLogicalPresentation) - The scale (SDL_SetRenderScale) - The viewport (SDL_SetRenderViewport) @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderLogicalPresentation @sa SDL_SetRenderScale<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`window_x`：the x coordinate in window coordinates.<br>`window_y`：the y coordinate in window coordinates.<br>`x`：a pointer filled with the x coordinate in render coordinates.<br>`y`：a pointer filled with the y coordinate in render coordinates.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4e0affe99f91c3b3"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderCoordinatesToWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float x, float y, float* window_x, float* window_y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40886) | Get a point in window coordinates when given a point in render coordinates. This takes into account several states: - The window dimensions. - The logical presentation settings (SDL_SetRenderLogicalPresentation) - The scale (SDL_SetRenderScale) - The viewport (SDL_SetRenderViewport) @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetRenderLogicalPresentation @sa SDL_SetRenderScale @sa SDL_SetRenderViewport<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`x`：the x coordinate in render coordinates.<br>`y`：the y coordinate in render coordinates.<br>`window_x`：a pointer filled with the x coordinate in window coordinates.<br>`window_y`：a pointer filled with the y coordinate in window coordinates.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f5f237711d2b3618"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderDebugText(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float x, float y, sbyte* str)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48701) | Draw debug text to an SDL_Renderer. This function will render a string of text to an SDL_Renderer. Note that this is a convenience function for debugging, with severe limitations, and not intended to be used for production apps and games. Among these limitations: - It accepts UTF-8 strings, but will only renders ASCII characters. - It has a single, tiny size (8x8 pixels). You can use logical presentation or SDL_SetRenderScale() to adjust it. - It uses a simple, hardcoded bitmap font. It does not allow different font selections and it does not support truetype, for proper scaling. - It does no word-wrapping and does not treat newline characters as a line break. If the text goes out of the window, it's gone. For serious text rendering, there are several good options, such as SDL_ttf, stb_truetype, or other external libraries. On first use, this will create an internal texture for rendering glyphs. This texture will live until the renderer is destroyed. The text is drawn in the color specified by SDL_SetRenderDrawColor(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderDebugTextFormat @sa SDL_DEBUG_TEXT_FONT_CHARACTER_SIZE<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should draw a line of text.<br>`x`：the x coordinate where the top-left corner of the text will draw.<br>`y`：the y coordinate where the top-left corner of the text will draw.<br>`str`：the string to render.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1fcafc7cf08aa7ff"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderFillRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44212) | Fill a rectangle on the current rendering target with the drawing color at subpixel precision. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderFillRects<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should fill a rectangle.<br>`rect`：a pointer to the destination rectangle, or NULL for the entire rendering target.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6b3a2943da5786dc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderFillRects(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* rects, int count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17778) | Fill some number of rectangles on the current rendering target with the drawing color at subpixel precision. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderFillRect<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should fill multiple rectangles.<br>`rects`：a pointer to an array of destination rectangles.<br>`count`：the number of rectangles.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7501be9920d744be"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderGeometry(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLVertex* vertices, int num_vertices, int* indices, int num_indices)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46102) | Render a list of triangles, optionally using a texture and indices into the vertex array. Color and alpha modulation is done per vertex (SDL_SetTextureColorMod and SDL_SetTextureAlphaMod are ignored). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderGeometryRaw @sa SDL_SetRenderTextureAddressMode<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`texture`：(optional) The SDL texture to use.<br>`vertices`：vertices.<br>`num_vertices`：number of vertices.<br>`indices`：(optional) An array of integer indices into the 'vertices' array, if NULL all vertices will be rendered in sequential order.<br>`num_indices`：number of indices.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4650f188b0541e59"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderGeometryRaw(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, float* xy, int xy_stride, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFColor* color, int color_stride, float* uv, int uv_stride, int num_vertices, void* indices, int num_indices, int size_indices)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40753) | Render a list of triangles, optionally using a texture and indices into the vertex arrays. Color and alpha modulation is done per vertex (SDL_SetTextureColorMod and SDL_SetTextureAlphaMod are ignored). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderGeometry @sa SDL_SetRenderTextureAddressMode<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`texture`：(optional) The SDL texture to use.<br>`xy`：vertex positions.<br>`xy_stride`：byte size to move from one element to the next element.<br>`color`：vertex colors (as SDL_FColor).<br>`color_stride`：byte size to move from one element to the next element.<br>`uv`：vertex normalized texture coordinates.<br>`uv_stride`：byte size to move from one element to the next element.<br>`num_vertices`：number of vertices.<br>`indices`：(optional) An array of indices into the 'vertices' arrays, if NULL all vertices will be rendered in sequential order.<br>`num_indices`：number of indices.<br>`size_indices`：index size: 1 (byte), 2 (short), 4 (int).<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0331106bf3de8b82"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderLine(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float x1, float y1, float x2, float y2)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37902) | Draw a line on the current rendering target at subpixel precision. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderLines<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should draw a line.<br>`x1`：the x coordinate of the start point.<br>`y1`：the y coordinate of the start point.<br>`x2`：the x coordinate of the end point.<br>`y2`：the y coordinate of the end point.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5e80453954832cb6"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderLines(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFPoint* points, int count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24193) | Draw a series of connected lines on the current rendering target at subpixel precision. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderLine<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should draw multiple lines.<br>`points`：the points along the lines.<br>`count`：the number of points, drawing count-1 lines.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e0cbb14e4d82aa58"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderPoint(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float x, float y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45209) | Draw a point on the current rendering target at subpixel precision. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderPoints<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should draw a point.<br>`x`：the x coordinate of the point.<br>`y`：the y coordinate of the point.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-20357b2fb7558c07"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderPoints(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFPoint* points, int count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49161) | Draw multiple points on the current rendering target at subpixel precision. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderPoint<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should draw multiple points.<br>`points`：the points to draw.<br>`count`：the number of points to draw.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c879224f15601503"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderPresent(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32024) | Update the screen with any rendering performed since the previous call. SDL's rendering functions operate on a backbuffer; that is, calling a rendering function such as SDL_RenderLine() does not directly put a line on the screen, but rather updates the backbuffer. As such, you compose your entire scene and *present* the composed backbuffer to the screen as a complete picture. Therefore, when using SDL's rendering API, one does all drawing intended for the frame, and then calls this function once per frame to present the final drawing to the user. The backbuffer should be considered invalidated after each present; do not assume that previous contents will exist between frames. You are strongly encouraged to call SDL_RenderClear() to initialize the backbuffer before starting each new frame's drawing, even if you plan to overwrite every pixel. Please note, that in case of rendering to a texture - there is **no need** to call `SDL_RenderPresent` after drawing needed objects to a texture, and should not be done; you are only required to change back the rendering target to default via `SDL_SetRenderTarget(renderer, NULL)` afterwards, as textures by themselves do not have a concept of backbuffers. Calling SDL_RenderPresent while rendering to a texture will fail. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateRenderer @sa SDL_RenderClear @sa SDL_RenderFillRect @sa SDL_RenderFillRects @sa SDL_RenderLine @sa SDL_RenderLines @sa SDL_RenderPoint @sa SDL_RenderPoints @sa SDL_RenderRect @sa SDL_RenderRects @sa SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode @sa SDL_SetRenderDrawColor<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a283ec0ffc3af38d"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderReadPixels(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17417) | Read pixels from the current rendering target. The returned surface contains pixels inside the desired area clipped to the current viewport, and should be freed with SDL_DestroySurface(). Note that this returns the actual pixels on the screen, so if you are using logical presentation you should use SDL_GetRenderLogicalPresentationRect() to get the area containing your content. **WARNING**: This is a very slow operation, and should not be used frequently. If you're using this on the main rendering target, it should be called after rendering and before SDL_RenderPresent(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`rect`：an SDL_Rect structure representing the area to read, which will be clipped to the current viewport, or NULL for the entire viewport.<br>返回：a new SDL_Surface on success or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c87d4a3b46c66d57"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41517) | Draw a rectangle on the current rendering target at subpixel precision. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderRects<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should draw a rectangle.<br>`rect`：a pointer to the destination rectangle, or NULL to outline the entire rendering target.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6e4ba1ed17726151"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderRects(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* rects, int count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30169) | Draw some number of rectangles on the current rendering target at subpixel precision. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderRect<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should draw multiple rectangles.<br>`rects`：a pointer to an array of destination rectangles.<br>`count`：the number of rectangles.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d71363e7bb2870a1"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderTexture(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* srcrect, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* dstrect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42385) | Copy a portion of the texture to the current rendering target at subpixel precision. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderTextureRotated @sa SDL_RenderTextureTiled<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should copy parts of a texture.<br>`texture`：the source texture.<br>`srcrect`：a pointer to the source rectangle, or NULL for the entire texture.<br>`dstrect`：a pointer to the destination rectangle, or NULL for the entire rendering target.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9750f2e8c09fd899"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderTexture9Grid(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* srcrect, float left_width, float right_width, float top_height, float bottom_height, float scale, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* dstrect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31139) | Perform a scaled copy using the 9-grid algorithm to the current rendering target at subpixel precision. The pixels in the texture are split into a 3x3 grid, using the different corner sizes for each corner, and the sides and center making up the remaining pixels. The corners are then scaled using `scale` and fit into the corners of the destination rectangle. The sides and center are then stretched into place to cover the remaining destination rectangle. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderTexture @sa SDL_RenderTexture9GridTiled<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should copy parts of a texture.<br>`texture`：the source texture.<br>`srcrect`：the SDL_Rect structure representing the rectangle to be used for the 9-grid, or NULL to use the entire texture.<br>`left_width`：the width, in pixels, of the left corners in `srcrect`.<br>`right_width`：the width, in pixels, of the right corners in `srcrect`.<br>`top_height`：the height, in pixels, of the top corners in `srcrect`.<br>`bottom_height`：the height, in pixels, of the bottom corners in `srcrect`.<br>`scale`：the scale used to transform the corner of `srcrect` into the corner of `dstrect`, or 0.0f for an unscaled copy.<br>`dstrect`：a pointer to the destination rectangle, or NULL for the entire rendering target.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-664a3386949d3a6e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderTexture9GridTiled(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* srcrect, float left_width, float right_width, float top_height, float bottom_height, float scale, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* dstrect, float tileScale)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51413) | Perform a scaled copy using the 9-grid algorithm to the current rendering target at subpixel precision. The pixels in the texture are split into a 3x3 grid, using the different corner sizes for each corner, and the sides and center making up the remaining pixels. The corners are then scaled using `scale` and fit into the corners of the destination rectangle. The sides and center are then tiled into place to cover the remaining destination rectangle. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_RenderTexture @sa SDL_RenderTexture9Grid<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should copy parts of a texture.<br>`texture`：the source texture.<br>`srcrect`：the SDL_Rect structure representing the rectangle to be used for the 9-grid, or NULL to use the entire texture.<br>`left_width`：the width, in pixels, of the left corners in `srcrect`.<br>`right_width`：the width, in pixels, of the right corners in `srcrect`.<br>`top_height`：the height, in pixels, of the top corners in `srcrect`.<br>`bottom_height`：the height, in pixels, of the bottom corners in `srcrect`.<br>`scale`：the scale used to transform the corner of `srcrect` into the corner of `dstrect`, or 0.0f for an unscaled copy.<br>`dstrect`：a pointer to the destination rectangle, or NULL for the entire rendering target.<br>`tileScale`：the scale used to transform the borders and center of `srcrect` into the borders and middle of `dstrect`, or 1.0f for an unscaled copy.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
