| <a id="api-95b811b438e50d43"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderTextureAffine(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* srcrect, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFPoint* origin, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFPoint* right, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFPoint* down)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39283) | Copy a portion of the source texture to the current rendering target, with affine transform, at subpixel precision. @threadsafety You may only call this function from the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderTexture<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should copy parts of a texture.<br>`texture`：the source texture.<br>`srcrect`：a pointer to the source rectangle, or NULL for the entire texture.<br>`origin`：a pointer to a point indicating where the top-left corner of srcrect should be mapped to, or NULL for the rendering target's origin.<br>`right`：a pointer to a point indicating where the top-right corner of srcrect should be mapped to, or NULL for the rendering target's top-right corner.<br>`down`：a pointer to a point indicating where the bottom-left corner of srcrect should be mapped to, or NULL for the rendering target's bottom-left corner.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-226372272208b605"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderTextureRotated(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* srcrect, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* dstrect, double angle, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFPoint* center, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFlipMode flip)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20428) | Copy a portion of the source texture to the current rendering target, with rotation and flipping, at subpixel precision. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderTexture<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should copy parts of a texture.<br>`texture`：the source texture.<br>`srcrect`：a pointer to the source rectangle, or NULL for the entire texture.<br>`dstrect`：a pointer to the destination rectangle, or NULL for the entire rendering target.<br>`angle`：an angle in degrees that indicates the rotation that will be applied to dstrect, rotating it in a clockwise direction.<br>`center`：a pointer to a point indicating the point around which dstrect will be rotated (if NULL, rotation will be done around dstrect.w/2, dstrect.h/2).<br>`flip`：an SDL_FlipMode value stating which flipping actions should be performed on the texture.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b447fb0916a12eea"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderTextureTiled(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* srcrect, float scale, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFRect* dstrect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35458) | Tile a portion of the texture to the current rendering target at subpixel precision. The pixels in `srcrect` will be repeated as many times as needed to completely fill `dstrect`. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RenderTexture<br>`renderer`：the renderer which should copy parts of a texture.<br>`texture`：the source texture.<br>`srcrect`：a pointer to the source rectangle, or NULL for the entire texture.<br>`scale`：the scale used to transform srcrect into the destination rectangle, e.g. a 32x32 texture with a scale of 2 would fill 64x64 tiles.<br>`dstrect`：a pointer to the destination rectangle, or NULL for the entire rendering target.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-23ef6ee9a944330d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RenderViewportSet(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28198) | Return whether an explicit rectangle was set as the viewport. This is useful if you're saving and restoring the viewport and want to know whether you should restore a specific rectangle or NULL. Each render target has its own viewport. This function checks the viewport for the current render target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderViewport @sa SDL_SetRenderViewport<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>返回：true if the viewport was set to a specific rectangle, or false if it was set to NULL (the entire target). |
| <a id="api-9e1768aa396ae478"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ResetHint(sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52239) | Reset a hint to the default value. This will reset a hint to the value of the environment variable, or NULL if the environment isn't set. Callbacks will be called normally with this change. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetHint @sa SDL_ResetHints<br>`name`：the hint to set.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-cf276307f08388ab"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ResetHints()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37002) | Reset all hints to the default values. This will reset all hints to the value of the associated environment variable, or NULL if the environment isn't set. Callbacks will be called normally with this change. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ResetHint |
| <a id="api-9adb63e8fcabe0fe"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ResetKeyboard()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34232) | Clear the state of the keyboard. This function will generate key up events for all pressed keys. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetKeyboardState |
| <a id="api-fd9a7a691d3d32fb"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ResetLogPriorities()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31428) | Reset all priorities to default. This is called by SDL_Quit(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetLogPriorities @sa SDL_SetLogPriority |
| <a id="api-4f17e5773dfc99ea"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RestoreWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23615) | Request that the size and position of a minimized or maximized window be restored. If the window is in a fullscreen state, this request has no direct effect. It may alter the state the window is returned to when leaving fullscreen. On some windowing systems this request is asynchronous and the new window state may not have have been applied immediately upon the return of this function. If an immediate change is required, call SDL_SyncWindow() to block until the changes have taken effect. When the window state changes, an SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_RESTORED event will be emitted. Note that, as this is just a request, the windowing system can deny the state change. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_MaximizeWindow @sa SDL_MinimizeWindow @sa SDL_SyncWindow<br>`window`：the window to restore.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d660bd3e0204b7ab"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ResumeAudioDevice(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26628) | Use this function to unpause audio playback on a specified device. This function unpauses audio processing for a given device that has previously been paused with SDL_PauseAudioDevice(). Once unpaused, any bound audio streams will begin to progress again, and audio can be generated. Unlike in SDL2, audio devices start in an _unpaused_ state, since an app has to bind a stream before any audio will flow. Unpausing an unpaused device is a legal no-op. Physical devices can not be paused or unpaused, only logical devices created through SDL_OpenAudioDevice() can be. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AudioDevicePaused @sa SDL_PauseAudioDevice<br>`devid`：a device opened by SDL_OpenAudioDevice().<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-58414f6e44fe9e2d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ResumeAudioStreamDevice(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21530) | Use this function to unpause audio playback on the audio device associated with an audio stream. This function unpauses audio processing for a given device that has previously been paused. Once unpaused, any bound audio streams will begin to progress again, and audio can be generated. SDL_OpenAudioDeviceStream opens audio devices in a paused state, so this function call is required for audio playback to begin on such devices. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PauseAudioStreamDevice<br>`stream`：the audio stream associated with the audio device to resume.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-38cd706190c605c0"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ResumeHaptic(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48878) | Resume a haptic device. Call to unpause after SDL_PauseHaptic(). @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PauseHaptic<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to unpause.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3738a21474bb835f"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RotateSurface(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, float angle)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51232) | Return a copy of a surface rotated clockwise a number of degrees. The angle of rotation can be negative for counter-clockwise rotation. When the rotation isn't a multiple of 90 degrees, the resulting surface is larger than the original, with the background filled in with the colorkey, if available, or RGBA 255/255/255/0 if not. If `surface` has the SDL_PROP_SURFACE_ROTATION_FLOAT property set on it, the new copy will have the adjusted value set: if the rotation property is 90 and `angle` was 30, the new surface will have a property value of 60 (that is: to be upright vs gravity, this surface needs to rotate 60 more degrees). However, note that further rotations on the new surface in this example will produce unexpected results, since the image will have resized and padded to accommodate the not-90 degree angle. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`surface`：the surface to rotate.<br>`angle`：the rotation angle, in degrees.<br>返回：a rotated copy of the surface or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3ee03c8d943ce7c7"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Round(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34508) | Round `x` to the nearest integer. Rounds `x` to the nearest integer. Values halfway between integers will be rounded away from zero. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_roundf for single-precision floats. To get the result as an integer type, use SDL_lround. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lround @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_trunc<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：the nearest integer to `x`. |
| <a id="api-5bc466bf75cc712f"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Roundf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18330) | Round `x` to the nearest integer. Rounds `x` to the nearest integer. Values halfway between integers will be rounded away from zero. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_round for double-precision floats. To get the result as an integer type, use SDL_lroundf. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lroundf @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_truncf<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：the nearest integer to `x`. |
| <a id="api-e8ee1de96e2b81a3"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RumbleGamepad(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, ushort low_frequency_rumble, ushort high_frequency_rumble, uint duration_ms)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41190) | Start a rumble effect on a gamepad. Each call to this function cancels any previous rumble effect, and calling it with 0 intensity stops any rumbling. This function requires you to process SDL events or call SDL_UpdateJoysticks() to update rumble state. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad to vibrate.<br>`low_frequency_rumble`：the intensity of the low frequency (left) rumble motor, from 0 to 0xFFFF.<br>`high_frequency_rumble`：the intensity of the high frequency (right) rumble motor, from 0 to 0xFFFF.<br>`duration_ms`：the duration of the rumble effect, in milliseconds.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9b2b9dd8f44349c8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RumbleGamepadTriggers(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, ushort left_rumble, ushort right_rumble, uint duration_ms)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39322) | Start a rumble effect in the gamepad's triggers. Each call to this function cancels any previous trigger rumble effect, and calling it with 0 intensity stops any rumbling. Note that this is rumbling of the _triggers_ and not the gamepad as a whole. This is currently only supported on Xbox One gamepads. If you want the (more common) whole-gamepad rumble, use SDL_RumbleGamepad() instead. This function requires you to process SDL events or call SDL_UpdateJoysticks() to update rumble state. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RumbleGamepad<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad to vibrate.<br>`left_rumble`：the intensity of the left trigger rumble motor, from 0 to 0xFFFF.<br>`right_rumble`：the intensity of the right trigger rumble motor, from 0 to 0xFFFF.<br>`duration_ms`：the duration of the rumble effect, in milliseconds.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-790ac19022e589ac"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RumbleJoystick(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, ushort low_frequency_rumble, ushort high_frequency_rumble, uint duration_ms)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48990) | Start a rumble effect. Each call to this function cancels any previous rumble effect, and calling it with 0 intensity stops any rumbling. This function requires you to process SDL events or call SDL_UpdateJoysticks() to update rumble state. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`joystick`：the joystick to vibrate.<br>`low_frequency_rumble`：the intensity of the low frequency (left) rumble motor, from 0 to 0xFFFF.<br>`high_frequency_rumble`：the intensity of the high frequency (right) rumble motor, from 0 to 0xFFFF.<br>`duration_ms`：the duration of the rumble effect, in milliseconds.<br>返回：true, or false if rumble isn't supported on this joystick. |
| <a id="api-14e973caa6917088"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RumbleJoystickTriggers(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, ushort left_rumble, ushort right_rumble, uint duration_ms)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23672) | Start a rumble effect in the joystick's triggers. Each call to this function cancels any previous trigger rumble effect, and calling it with 0 intensity stops any rumbling. Note that this is rumbling of the _triggers_ and not the game controller as a whole. This is currently only supported on Xbox One controllers. If you want the (more common) whole-controller rumble, use SDL_RumbleJoystick() instead. This function requires you to process SDL events or call SDL_UpdateJoysticks() to update rumble state. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RumbleJoystick<br>`joystick`：the joystick to vibrate.<br>`left_rumble`：the intensity of the left trigger rumble motor, from 0 to 0xFFFF.<br>`right_rumble`：the intensity of the right trigger rumble motor, from 0 to 0xFFFF.<br>`duration_ms`：the duration of the rumble effect, in milliseconds.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3cefe5351fa47f75"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RunHapticEffect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic, int effect, uint iterations)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28882) | Run the haptic effect on its associated haptic device. To repeat the effect over and over indefinitely, set `iterations` to `SDL_HAPTIC_INFINITY`. (Repeats the envelope - attack and fade.) To make one instance of the effect last indefinitely (so the effect does not fade), set the effect's `length` in its structure/union to `SDL_HAPTIC_INFINITY` instead. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetHapticEffectStatus @sa SDL_StopHapticEffect @sa SDL_StopHapticEffects<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to run the effect on.<br>`effect`：the ID of the haptic effect to run.<br>`iterations`：the number of iterations to run the effect; use `SDL_HAPTIC_INFINITY` to repeat forever.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a202f720680938c3"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.RunOnMainThread(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, void> callback, void* userdata, byte wait_complete)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17519) | Call a function on the main thread during event processing. If this is called on the main thread, the callback is executed immediately. If this is called on another thread, this callback is queued for execution on the main thread during event processing. Be careful of deadlocks when using this functionality. You should not have the main thread wait for the current thread while this function is being called with `wait_complete` true. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_IsMainThread<br>`callback`：the callback to call on the main thread.<br>`userdata`：a pointer that is passed to `callback`.<br>`wait_complete`：true to wait for the callback to complete, false to return immediately.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9e25ff1e63e22463"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SaveBmp(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, sbyte* file)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32918) | Save a surface to a file in BMP format. Surfaces with a 24-bit, 32-bit and paletted 8-bit format get saved in the BMP directly. Other RGB formats with 8-bit or higher get converted to a 24-bit surface or, if they have an alpha mask or a colorkey, to a 32-bit surface before they are saved. YUV and paletted 1-bit and 4-bit formats are not supported. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LoadBMP @sa SDL_SaveBMP_IO<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure containing the image to be saved.<br>`file`：a file to save to.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b152750be1ac2352"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SaveBmpIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* dst, byte closeio)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29517) | Save a surface to a seekable SDL data stream in BMP format. Surfaces with a 24-bit, 32-bit and paletted 8-bit format get saved in the BMP directly. Other RGB formats with 8-bit or higher get converted to a 24-bit surface or, if they have an alpha mask or a colorkey, to a 32-bit surface before they are saved. YUV and paletted 1-bit and 4-bit formats are not supported. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LoadBMP_IO @sa SDL_SaveBMP<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure containing the image to be saved.<br>`dst`：a data stream to save to.<br>`closeio`：if true, calls SDL_CloseIO() on `dst` before returning, even in the case of an error.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e5ec27483b405037"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SaveFile(sbyte* file, void* data, ulong datasize)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18106) | Save all the data into a file path. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SaveFile_IO @sa SDL_LoadFile<br>`file`：the path to write all available data into.<br>`data`：the data to be written. If datasize is 0, may be NULL or a invalid pointer.<br>`datasize`：the number of bytes to be written.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-959fe158f66d6dfc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SaveFileIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* src, void* data, ulong datasize, byte closeio)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42580) | Save all the data into an SDL data stream. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SaveFile @sa SDL_LoadFile_IO<br>`src`：the SDL_IOStream to write all data to.<br>`data`：the data to be written. If datasize is 0, may be NULL or a invalid pointer.<br>`datasize`：the number of bytes to be written.<br>`closeio`：if true, calls SDL_CloseIO() on `src` before returning, even in the case of an error.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-858475d076df22dd"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SavePng(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, sbyte* file)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32099) | Save a surface to a file in PNG format. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_LoadPNG @sa SDL_SavePNG_IO<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure containing the image to be saved.<br>`file`：a file to save to.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-90e4c2511be35b70"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SavePngIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* dst, byte closeio)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36854) | Save a surface to a seekable SDL data stream in PNG format. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_LoadPNG_IO @sa SDL_SavePNG<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure containing the image to be saved.<br>`dst`：a data stream to save to.<br>`closeio`：if true, calls SDL_CloseIO() on `dst` before returning, even in the case of an error.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-65cfb3a70c205f10"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Scalbn(double x, int n)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46976) | Scale `x` by an integer power of two. Multiplies `x` by the `n`th power of the floating point radix (always 2). Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `n` integer Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_scalbnf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_scalbnf @sa SDL_pow<br>`x`：floating point value to be scaled.<br>`n`：integer exponent.<br>返回：`x * 2^n`. |
| <a id="api-bc7f891996290e2d"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Scalbnf(float x, int n)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36949) | Scale `x` by an integer power of two. Multiplies `x` by the `n`th power of the floating point radix (always 2). Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `n` integer Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_scalbn for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_scalbn @sa SDL_powf<br>`x`：floating point value to be scaled.<br>`n`：integer exponent.<br>返回：`x * 2^n`. |
| <a id="api-1bb7b79d96a792a1"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ScaleSurface(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, int width, int height, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScaleMode scaleMode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35076) | Creates a new surface identical to the existing surface, scaled to the desired size. The returned surface should be freed with SDL_DestroySurface(). @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_DestroySurface<br>`surface`：the surface to duplicate and scale.<br>`width`：the width of the new surface.<br>`height`：the height of the new surface.<br>`scaleMode`：the SDL_ScaleMode to be used.<br>返回：a copy of the surface or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8809366cb9f69835"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ScreenKeyboardShown(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20630) | Check whether the screen keyboard is shown for given window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HasScreenKeyboardSupport<br>`window`：the window for which screen keyboard should be queried.<br>返回：true if screen keyboard is shown or false if not. |
| <a id="api-c791048646e4d94d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ScreenSaverEnabled()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42160) | Check whether the screensaver is currently enabled. The screensaver is disabled by default. The default can also be changed using `SDL_HINT_VIDEO_ALLOW_SCREENSAVER`. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_DisableScreenSaver @sa SDL_EnableScreenSaver<br>返回：true if the screensaver is enabled, false if it is disabled. |
| <a id="api-0cad493789e5121e"></a>[`public long Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SeekIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* context, long offset, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOWhence whence)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35910) | Seek within an SDL_IOStream data stream. This function seeks to byte `offset`, relative to `whence`. `whence` may be any of the following values: - `SDL_IO_SEEK_SET`: seek from the beginning of data - `SDL_IO_SEEK_CUR`: seek relative to current read point - `SDL_IO_SEEK_END`: seek relative to the end of data If this stream can not seek, it will return -1. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_TellIO<br>`context`：a pointer to an SDL_IOStream structure.<br>`offset`：an offset in bytes, relative to `whence` location; can be negative.<br>`whence`：any of `SDL_IO_SEEK_SET`, `SDL_IO_SEEK_CUR`, `SDL_IO_SEEK_END`.<br>返回：the final offset in the data stream after the seek or -1 on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4d2d51817b565cdc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SendGamepadEffect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, void* data, int size)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28680) | Send a gamepad specific effect packet. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad to affect.<br>`data`：the data to send to the gamepad.<br>`size`：the size of the data to send to the gamepad.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1e7567b6666d102f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SendJoystickEffect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, void* data, int size)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18682) | Send a joystick specific effect packet. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`joystick`：the joystick to affect.<br>`data`：the data to send to the joystick.<br>`size`：the size of the data to send to the joystick.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d281eab039f0c2f9"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SendJoystickVirtualSensorData(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensorType type, ulong sensor_timestamp, float* data, int num_values)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49977) | Send a sensor update for an opened virtual joystick. Please note that values set here will not be applied until the next call to SDL_UpdateJoysticks, which can either be called directly, or can be called indirectly through various other SDL APIs, including, but not limited to the following: SDL_PollEvent, SDL_PumpEvents, SDL_WaitEventTimeout, SDL_WaitEvent. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualAxis @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualButton @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualBall @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualHat @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualTouchpad<br>`joystick`：the virtual joystick on which to set state.<br>`type`：the type of the sensor on the virtual joystick to update.<br>`sensor_timestamp`：a 64-bit timestamp in nanoseconds associated with the sensor reading.<br>`data`：the data associated with the sensor reading.<br>`num_values`：the number of values pointed to by `data`.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5e7a40ff16214a30"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAppMetadata(sbyte* appname, sbyte* appversion, sbyte* appidentifier)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34194) | Specify basic metadata about your app. You can optionally provide metadata about your app to SDL. This is not required, but strongly encouraged. There are several locations where SDL can make use of metadata (an "About" box in the macOS menu bar, the name of the app can be shown on some audio mixers, etc). Any piece of metadata can be left as NULL, if a specific detail doesn't make sense for the app. This function should be called as early as possible, before SDL_Init. Multiple calls to this function are allowed, but various state might not change once it has been set up with a previous call to this function. Passing a NULL removes any previous metadata. This is a simplified interface for the most important information. You can supply significantly more detailed metadata with SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAppMetadataProperty<br>`appname`：The name of the application ("My Game 2: Bad Guy's Revenge!").<br>`appversion`：The version of the application ("1.0.0beta5" or a git hash, or whatever makes sense).<br>`appidentifier`：A unique string in reverse-domain format that identifies this app ("com.example.mygame2").<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1011c6cb02e354fb"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAppMetadataProperty(sbyte* name, sbyte* value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32536) | Specify metadata about your app through a set of properties. You can optionally provide metadata about your app to SDL. This is not required, but strongly encouraged. There are several locations where SDL can make use of metadata (an "About" box in the macOS menu bar, the name of the app can be shown on some audio mixers, etc). Any piece of metadata can be left out, if a specific detail doesn't make sense for the app. This function should be called as early as possible, before SDL_Init. Multiple calls to this function are allowed, but various state might not change once it has been set up with a previous call to this function. Once set, this metadata can be read using SDL_GetAppMetadataProperty(). These are the supported properties: - `SDL_PROP_APP_METADATA_NAME_STRING`: The human-readable name of the application, like "My Game 2: Bad Guy's Revenge!". This will show up anywhere the OS shows the name of the application separately from window titles, such as volume control applets, etc. This defaults to "SDL Application". - `SDL_PROP_APP_METADATA_VERSION_STRING`: The version of the app that is running; there are no rules on format, so "1.0.3beta2" and "April 22nd, 2024" and a git hash are all valid options. This has no default. - `SDL_PROP_APP_METADATA_IDENTIFIER_STRING`: A unique string that identifies this app. This must be in reverse-domain format, like "com.example.mygame2". This string is used by desktop compositors to identify and group windows together, as well as match applications with associated desktop settings and icons. If you plan to package your application in a container such as Flatpak, the app ID should match the name of your Flatpak container as well. This has no default. - `SDL_PROP_APP_METADATA_CREATOR_STRING`: The human-readable name of the creator/developer/maker of this app, like "MojoWorkshop, LLC" - `SDL_PROP_APP_METADATA_COPYRIGHT_STRING`: The human-readable copyright notice, like "Copyright (c) 2024 MojoWorkshop, LLC" or whatnot. Keep this to one line, don't paste a copy of a whole software license in here. This has no default. - `SDL_PROP_APP_METADATA_URL_STRING`: A URL to the app on the web. Maybe a product page, or a storefront, or even a GitHub repository, for user's further information This has no default. - `SDL_PROP_APP_METADATA_TYPE_STRING`: The type of application this is. Currently this string can be "game" for a video game, "mediaplayer" for a media player, or generically "application" if nothing else applies. Future versions of SDL might add new types. This defaults to "application". @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAppMetadataProperty @sa SDL_SetAppMetadata<br>`name`：the name of the metadata property to set.<br>`value`：the value of the property, or NULL to remove that property.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f5ed3a23f7f622e7"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAtomicInt(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAtomicInt* a, int v)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20981) | Set an atomic variable to a value. This function also acts as a full memory barrier. ***Note: If you don't know what this function is for, you shouldn't use it!*** @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAtomicInt<br>`a`：a pointer to an SDL_AtomicInt variable to be modified.<br>`v`：the desired value.<br>返回：the previous value of the atomic variable. |
| <a id="api-eac8341309e301da"></a>[`public void* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAtomicPointer(void** a, void* v)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36080) | Set a pointer to a value atomically. ***Note: If you don't know what this function is for, you shouldn't use it!*** @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CompareAndSwapAtomicPointer @sa SDL_GetAtomicPointer<br>`a`：a pointer to a pointer.<br>`v`：the desired pointer value.<br>返回：the previous value of the pointer. |
| <a id="api-a20aa1feba52c043"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAtomicU32(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAtomicU32* a, uint v)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49378) | Set an atomic variable to a value. This function also acts as a full memory barrier. ***Note: If you don't know what this function is for, you shouldn't use it!*** @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAtomicU32<br>`a`：a pointer to an SDL_AtomicU32 variable to be modified.<br>`v`：the desired value.<br>返回：the previous value of the atomic variable. |
| <a id="api-f52fd45b46e75060"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAudioDeviceGain(uint devid, float gain)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35392) | Change the gain of an audio device. The gain of a device is its volume; a larger gain means a louder output, with a gain of zero being silence. Audio devices default to a gain of 1.0f (no change in output). Physical devices may not have their gain changed, only logical devices, and this function will always return false when used on physical devices. While it might seem attractive to adjust several logical devices at once in this way, it would allow an app or library to interfere with another portion of the program's otherwise-isolated devices. This is applied, along with any per-audiostream gain, during playback to the hardware, and can be continuously changed to create various effects. On recording devices, this will adjust the gain before passing the data into an audiostream; that recording audiostream can then adjust its gain further when outputting the data elsewhere, if it likes, but that second gain is not applied until the data leaves the audiostream again. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAudioDeviceGain<br>`devid`：the audio device on which to change gain.<br>`gain`：the gain. 1.0f is no change, 0.0f is silence.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e144d7352e9601b1"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAudioPostmixCallback(uint devid, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioSpec*, float*, int, void> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19296) | Set a callback that fires when data is about to be fed to an audio device. This is useful for accessing the final mix, perhaps for writing a visualizer or applying a final effect to the audio data before playback. The buffer is the final mix of all bound audio streams on an opened device; this callback will fire regularly for any device that is both opened and unpaused. If there is no new data to mix, either because no streams are bound to the device or all the streams are empty, this callback will still fire with the entire buffer set to silence. This callback is allowed to make changes to the data; the contents of the buffer after this call is what is ultimately passed along to the hardware. The callback is always provided the data in float format (values from -1.0f to 1.0f), but the number of channels or sample rate may be different than the format the app requested when opening the device; SDL might have had to manage a conversion behind the scenes, or the playback might have jumped to new physical hardware when a system default changed, etc. These details may change between calls. Accordingly, the size of the buffer might change between calls as well. This callback can run at any time, and from any thread; if you need to serialize access to your app's data, you should provide and use a mutex or other synchronization device. All of this to say: there are specific needs this callback can fulfill, but it is not the simplest interface. Apps should generally provide audio in their preferred format through an SDL_AudioStream and let SDL handle the difference. This function is extremely time-sensitive; the callback should do the least amount of work possible and return as quickly as it can. The longer the callback runs, the higher the risk of audio dropouts or other problems. This function will block until the audio device is in between iterations, so any existing callback that might be running will finish before this function sets the new callback and returns. Setting a NULL callback function disables any previously-set callback. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`devid`：the ID of an opened audio device.<br>`callback`：a callback function to be called. Can be NULL.<br>`userdata`：app-controlled pointer passed to callback. Can be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-2788ee7699508f91"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAudioStreamFormat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioSpec* src_spec, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioSpec* dst_spec)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33360) | Change the input and output formats of an audio stream. Future calls to and SDL_GetAudioStreamAvailable and SDL_GetAudioStreamData will reflect the new format, and future calls to SDL_PutAudioStreamData must provide data in the new input formats. Data that was previously queued in the stream will still be operated on in the format that was current when it was added, which is to say you can put the end of a sound file in one format to a stream, change formats for the next sound file, and start putting that new data while the previous sound file is still queued, and everything will still play back correctly. If a stream is bound to a device, then the format of the side of the stream bound to a device cannot be changed (src_spec for recording devices, dst_spec for playback devices). Attempts to make a change to this side will be ignored, but this will not report an error. The other side's format can be changed. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamFormat @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamFrequencyRatio<br>`stream`：the stream the format is being changed.<br>`src_spec`：the new format of the audio input; if NULL, it is not changed.<br>`dst_spec`：the new format of the audio output; if NULL, it is not changed.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-582b23d4237d9c20"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAudioStreamFrequencyRatio(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, float ratio)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26959) | Change the frequency ratio of an audio stream. The frequency ratio is used to adjust the rate at which input data is consumed. Changing this effectively modifies the speed and pitch of the audio. A value greater than 1.0f will play the audio faster, and at a higher pitch. A value less than 1.0f will play the audio slower, and at a lower pitch. 1.0f means play at normal speed. This is applied during SDL_GetAudioStreamData, and can be continuously changed to create various effects. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamFrequencyRatio @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamFormat<br>`stream`：the stream on which the frequency ratio is being changed.<br>`ratio`：the frequency ratio. 1.0 is normal speed. Must be between 0.01 and 100.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-42e6aefcadf8dd44"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAudioStreamGain(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, float gain)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45280) | Change the gain of an audio stream. The gain of a stream is its volume; a larger gain means a louder output, with a gain of zero being silence. Audio streams default to a gain of 1.0f (no change in output). This is applied during SDL_GetAudioStreamData, and can be continuously changed to create various effects. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetAudioStreamGain<br>`stream`：the stream on which the gain is being changed.<br>`gain`：the gain. 1.0f is no change, 0.0f is silence.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b08334d920144e85"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAudioStreamGetCallback(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream*, int, int, void> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19177) | Set a callback that runs when data is requested from an audio stream. This callback is called _before_ data is obtained from the stream, giving the callback the chance to add more on-demand. The callback can (optionally) call SDL_PutAudioStreamData() to add more audio to the stream during this call; if needed, the request that triggered this callback will obtain the new data immediately. The callback's `additional_amount` argument is roughly how many bytes of _unconverted_ data (in the stream's input format) is needed by the caller, although this may overestimate a little for safety. This takes into account how much is already in the stream and only asks for any extra necessary to resolve the request, which means the callback may be asked for zero bytes, and a different amount on each call. The callback is not required to supply exact amounts; it is allowed to supply too much or too little or none at all. The caller will get what's available, up to the amount they requested, regardless of this callback's outcome. Clearing or flushing an audio stream does not call this callback. This function obtains the stream's lock, which means any existing callback (get or put) in progress will finish running before setting the new callback. Setting a NULL function turns off the callback. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamPutCallback<br>`stream`：the audio stream to set the new callback on.<br>`callback`：the new callback function to call when data is requested from the stream.<br>`userdata`：an opaque pointer provided to the callback for its own personal use.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This only fails if `stream` is NULL. |
| <a id="api-669a461141d5a8b8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAudioStreamInputChannelMap(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, int* chmap, int count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36442) | Set the current input channel map of an audio stream. Channel maps are optional; most things do not need them, instead passing data in the order that SDL expects. The input channel map reorders data that is added to a stream via SDL_PutAudioStreamData. Future calls to SDL_PutAudioStreamData must provide data in the new channel order. Each item in the array represents an input channel, and its value is the channel that it should be remapped to. To reverse a stereo signal's left and right values, you'd have an array of `{ 1, 0 }`. It is legal to remap multiple channels to the same thing, so `{ 1, 1 }` would duplicate the right channel to both channels of a stereo signal. An element in the channel map set to -1 instead of a valid channel will mute that channel, setting it to a silence value. You cannot change the number of channels through a channel map, just reorder/mute them. Data that was previously queued in the stream will still be operated on in the order that was current when it was added, which is to say you can put the end of a sound file in one order to a stream, change orders for the next sound file, and start putting that new data while the previous sound file is still queued, and everything will still play back correctly. Audio streams default to no remapping applied. Passing a NULL channel map is legal, and turns off remapping. SDL will copy the channel map; the caller does not have to save this array after this call. If `count` is not equal to the current number of channels in the audio stream's format, this will fail. This is a safety measure to make sure a race condition hasn't changed the format while this call is setting the channel map. Unlike attempting to change the stream's format, the input channel map on a stream bound to a recording device is permitted to change at any time; any data added to the stream from the device after this call will have the new mapping, but previously-added data will still have the prior mapping. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. Don't change the stream's format to have a different number of channels from a different thread at the same time, though! @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamOutputChannelMap<br>`stream`：the SDL_AudioStream to change.<br>`chmap`：the new channel map, NULL to reset to default.<br>`count`：The number of channels in the map.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-324be50f3b137df7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAudioStreamOutputChannelMap(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, int* chmap, int count)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27417) | Set the current output channel map of an audio stream. Channel maps are optional; most things do not need them, instead passing data in the order that SDL expects. The output channel map reorders data that is leaving a stream via SDL_GetAudioStreamData. Each item in the array represents an input channel, and its value is the channel that it should be remapped to. To reverse a stereo signal's left and right values, you'd have an array of `{ 1, 0 }`. It is legal to remap multiple channels to the same thing, so `{ 1, 1 }` would duplicate the right channel to both channels of a stereo signal. An element in the channel map set to -1 instead of a valid channel will mute that channel, setting it to a silence value. You cannot change the number of channels through a channel map, just reorder/mute them. The output channel map can be changed at any time, as output remapping is applied during SDL_GetAudioStreamData. Audio streams default to no remapping applied. Passing a NULL channel map is legal, and turns off remapping. SDL will copy the channel map; the caller does not have to save this array after this call. If `count` is not equal to the current number of channels in the audio stream's format, this will fail. This is a safety measure to make sure a race condition hasn't changed the format while this call is setting the channel map. Unlike attempting to change the stream's format, the output channel map on a stream bound to a recording device is permitted to change at any time; any data added to the stream after this call will have the new mapping, but previously-added data will still have the prior mapping. When the channel map doesn't match the hardware's channel layout, SDL will convert the data before feeding it to the device for playback. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as it holds a stream-specific mutex while running. Don't change the stream's format to have a different number of channels from a a different thread at the same time, though! @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamInputChannelMap<br>`stream`：the SDL_AudioStream to change.<br>`chmap`：the new channel map, NULL to reset to default.<br>`count`：The number of channels in the map.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-97d821e99d282677"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetAudioStreamPutCallback(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream*, int, int, void> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45673) | Set a callback that runs when data is added to an audio stream. This callback is called _after_ the data is added to the stream, giving the callback the chance to obtain it immediately. The callback can (optionally) call SDL_GetAudioStreamData() to obtain audio from the stream during this call. The callback's `additional_amount` argument is how many bytes of _converted_ data (in the stream's output format) was provided by the caller, although this may underestimate a little for safety. This value might be less than what is currently available in the stream, if data was already there, and might be less than the caller provided if the stream needs to keep a buffer to aid in resampling. Which means the callback may be provided with zero bytes, and a different amount on each call. The callback may call SDL_GetAudioStreamAvailable to see the total amount currently available to read from the stream, instead of the total provided by the current call. The callback is not required to obtain all data. It is allowed to read less or none at all. Anything not read now simply remains in the stream for later access. Clearing or flushing an audio stream does not call this callback. This function obtains the stream's lock, which means any existing callback (get or put) in progress will finish running before setting the new callback. Setting a NULL function turns off the callback. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetAudioStreamGetCallback<br>`stream`：the audio stream to set the new callback on.<br>`callback`：the new callback function to call when data is added to the stream.<br>`userdata`：an opaque pointer provided to the callback for its own personal use.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. This only fails if `stream` is NULL. |
| <a id="api-5b70fe51e83f7798"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetBooleanProperty(uint props, sbyte* name, byte value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50101) | Set a boolean property in a group of properties. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetBooleanProperty<br>`props`：the properties to modify.<br>`name`：the name of the property to modify.<br>`value`：the new value of the property.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-56f9ef0b1a74d119"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetClipboardData(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, sbyte*, ulong*, void*> callback, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, void> cleanup, void* userdata, sbyte** mime_types, ulong num_mime_types)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24295) | Offer clipboard data to the OS. Tell the operating system that the application is offering clipboard data for each of the provided mime-types. Once another application requests the data the callback function will be called, allowing it to generate and respond with the data for the requested mime-type. The size of text data does not include any terminator, and the text does not need to be null-terminated (e.g., you can directly copy a portion of a document). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ClearClipboardData @sa SDL_GetClipboardData @sa SDL_HasClipboardData<br>`callback`：a function pointer to the function that provides the clipboard data.<br>`cleanup`：a function pointer to the function that cleans up the clipboard data.<br>`userdata`：an opaque pointer that will be forwarded to the callbacks.<br>`mime_types`：a list of mime-types that are being offered. SDL copies the given list.<br>`num_mime_types`：the number of mime-types in the mime_types list.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7fbab32face398f0"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetClipboardText(sbyte* text)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25979) | Put UTF-8 text into the clipboard. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetClipboardText @sa SDL_HasClipboardText<br>`text`：the text to store in the clipboard.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d5606449465c2c5b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetCurrentThreadPriority(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLThreadPriority priority)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18906) | Set the priority for the current thread. Note that some platforms will not let you alter the priority (or at least, promote the thread to a higher priority) at all, and some require you to be an administrator account. Be prepared for this to fail. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`priority`：the SDL_ThreadPriority to set.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6c9228a22038abd5"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetCursor(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCursor* cursor)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39739) | Set the active cursor. This function sets the currently active cursor to the specified one. If the cursor is currently visible, the change will be immediately represented on the display. SDL_SetCursor(NULL) can be used to force cursor redraw, if this is desired for any reason. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetCursor<br>`cursor`：a cursor to make active.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8a0f6164ebb8b6ab"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetDefaultTextureScaleMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScaleMode scale_mode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46748) | Set default scale mode for new textures for given renderer. When a renderer is created, scale_mode defaults to SDL_SCALEMODE_LINEAR. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_GetDefaultTextureScaleMode<br>`renderer`：the renderer to update.<br>`scale_mode`：the scale mode to change to for new textures.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-116467e5ca7428fb"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetEnvironmentVariable(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEnvironment* env, sbyte* name, sbyte* value, byte overwrite)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18046) | Set the value of a variable in the environment. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetEnvironment @sa SDL_CreateEnvironment @sa SDL_GetEnvironmentVariable @sa SDL_GetEnvironmentVariables @sa SDL_UnsetEnvironmentVariable<br>`env`：the environment to modify.<br>`name`：the name of the variable to set.<br>`value`：the value of the variable to set.<br>`overwrite`：true to overwrite the variable if it exists, false to return success without setting the variable if it already exists.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0884c23eb09a65d9"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetErrorV(sbyte* fmt, sbyte* ap)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42780) | Set the SDL error message for the current thread. Calling this function will replace any previous error message that was set. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ClearError @sa SDL_GetError @sa SDL_SetError<br>`fmt`：a printf()-style message format string.<br>`ap`：a variable argument list.<br>返回：false. |
| <a id="api-74850a187c502e7c"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetEventEnabled(uint type, byte enabled)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46060) | Set the state of processing events by type. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_EventEnabled<br>`type`：the type of event; see SDL_EventType for details.<br>`enabled`：whether to process the event or not. |
| <a id="api-d6ef0ffbcd98c930"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetEventFilter(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEvent*, byte> filter, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19765) | Set up a filter to process all events before they are added to the internal event queue. If you just want to see events without modifying them or preventing them from being queued, you should use SDL_AddEventWatch() instead. If the filter function returns true when called, then the event will be added to the internal queue. If it returns false, then the event will be dropped from the queue, but the internal state will still be updated. This allows selective filtering of dynamically arriving events. **WARNING**: Be very careful of what you do in the event filter function, as it may run in a different thread! The exception is handling of SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_EXPOSED, which is guaranteed to be sent from the OS on the main thread and you are expected to redraw your window in response to this event. On platforms that support it, if the quit event is generated by an interrupt signal (e.g. pressing Ctrl-C), it will be delivered to the application at the next event poll. Note: Disabled events never make it to the event filter function; see SDL_SetEventEnabled(). Note: Events pushed onto the queue with SDL_PushEvent() get passed through the event filter, but events pushed onto the queue with SDL_PeepEvents() do not. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AddEventWatch @sa SDL_SetEventEnabled @sa SDL_GetEventFilter @sa SDL_PeepEvents @sa SDL_PushEvent<br>`filter`：a function to call when an event happens.<br>`userdata`：a pointer that is passed to `filter`. |
| <a id="api-300419c3c53f8dde"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetFloatProperty(uint props, sbyte* name, float value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38731) | Set a floating point property in a group of properties. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetFloatProperty<br>`props`：the properties to modify.<br>`name`：the name of the property to modify.<br>`value`：the new value of the property.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a7fe5dfd9124e1ca"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGamepadEventsEnabled(byte enabled)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51007) | Set the state of gamepad event processing. If gamepad events are disabled, you must call SDL_UpdateGamepads() yourself and check the state of the gamepad when you want gamepad information. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GamepadEventsEnabled @sa SDL_UpdateGamepads<br>`enabled`：whether to process gamepad events or not. |
| <a id="api-fb6efce774537530"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGamepadLed(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, byte red, byte green, byte blue)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51531) | Update a gamepad's LED color. An example of a joystick LED is the light on the back of a PlayStation 4's DualShock 4 controller. For gamepads with a single color LED, the maximum of the RGB values will be used as the LED brightness. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad to update.<br>`red`：the intensity of the red LED.<br>`green`：the intensity of the green LED.<br>`blue`：the intensity of the blue LED.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-3c5aabf3481c4ce7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGamepadMapping(uint instance_id, sbyte* mapping)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40153) | Set the current mapping of a joystick or gamepad. Details about mappings are discussed with SDL_AddGamepadMapping(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AddGamepadMapping @sa SDL_GetGamepadMapping<br>`instance_id`：the joystick instance ID.<br>`mapping`：the mapping to use for this device, or NULL to clear the mapping.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-633237d67a095988"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGamepadPlayerIndex(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, int player_index)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20795) | Set the player index of an opened gamepad. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetGamepadPlayerIndex<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad object to adjust.<br>`player_index`：player index to assign to this gamepad, or -1 to clear the player index and turn off player LEDs.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-31c0ea2e3ffaa8a7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGamepadSensorEnabled(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGamepad* gamepad, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSensorType type, byte enabled)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43235) | Set whether data reporting for a gamepad sensor is enabled. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GamepadHasSensor @sa SDL_GamepadSensorEnabled<br>`gamepad`：the gamepad to update.<br>`type`：the type of sensor to enable/disable.<br>`enabled`：whether data reporting should be enabled.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-faa582892d9a8d9b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGpuAllowedFramesInFlight(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, uint allowed_frames_in_flight)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42754) | Configures the maximum allowed number of frames in flight. The default value when the device is created is 2. This means that after you have submitted 2 frames for presentation, if the GPU has not finished working on the first frame, SDL_AcquireGPUSwapchainTexture() will fill the swapchain texture pointer with NULL, and SDL_WaitAndAcquireGPUSwapchainTexture() will block. Higher values increase throughput at the expense of visual latency. Lower values decrease visual latency at the expense of throughput. Note that calling this function will stall and flush the command queue to prevent synchronization issues. The minimum value of allowed frames in flight is 1, and the maximum is 3. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`allowed_frames_in_flight`：the maximum number of frames that can be pending on the GPU.<br>返回：true if successful, false on error; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-91c9838d3b1659a5"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGpuBlendConstants(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPURenderPass* render_pass, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFColor* blend_constants)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30495) | Sets the current blend constants on a command buffer. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GPU_BLENDFACTOR_CONSTANT_COLOR @sa SDL_GPU_BLENDFACTOR_ONE_MINUS_CONSTANT_COLOR<br>`render_pass`：a render pass handle.<br>`blend_constants`：the blend constant color. |
| <a id="api-3e7127a4cdcc6c12"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGpuBufferName(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUBuffer* buffer, sbyte* text)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45905) | Sets an arbitrary string constant to label a buffer. You should use SDL_PROP_GPU_BUFFER_CREATE_NAME_STRING with SDL_CreateGPUBuffer instead of this function to avoid thread safety issues. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe, you must make sure the buffer is not simultaneously used by any other thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateGPUBuffer<br>`device`：a GPU Context.<br>`buffer`：a buffer to attach the name to.<br>`text`：a UTF-8 string constant to mark as the name of the buffer. |
| <a id="api-74517d3d2ffc3318"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGpuRenderState(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPURenderState* state)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44869) | Set custom GPU render state. This function sets custom GPU render state for subsequent draw calls. This allows using custom shaders with the GPU renderer. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the renderer. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`renderer`：the renderer to use.<br>`state`：the state to to use, or NULL to clear custom GPU render state.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ffdd4118eeaa5a53"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGpuRenderStateFragmentUniforms(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPURenderState* state, uint slot_index, void* data, uint length)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42022) | Set fragment shader uniform variables in a custom GPU render state. The data is copied and will be pushed using SDL_PushGPUFragmentUniformData() during draw call execution. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the renderer. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`state`：the state to modify.<br>`slot_index`：the fragment uniform slot to push data to.<br>`data`：client data to write.<br>`length`：the length of the data to write.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9fd0f0b2cd491737"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGpuRenderStateSamplerBindings(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPURenderState* state, int num_sampler_bindings, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureSamplerBinding* sampler_bindings)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17320) | Set sampler bindings variables in a custom GPU render state. The data is copied and will be binded using SDL_BindGPUFragmentSamplers() during draw call execution. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the renderer. @since This function is available since SDL 3.6.0.<br>`state`：the state to modify.<br>`num_sampler_bindings`：The number of additional fragment samplers to bind.<br>`sampler_bindings`：Additional fragment samplers to bind.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ae7c89724bea6bea"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGpuRenderStateStorageBuffers(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPURenderState* state, int num_storage_buffers, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUBuffer** storage_buffers)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22263) | Set storage buffers variables in a custom GPU render state. | <a id="api-19942c4da221a0b7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetGpuSwapchainParameters(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUSwapchainComposition swapchain_composition, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUPresentMode present_mode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32772) | Changes the swapchain parameters for the given claimed window. Check if the parameters are supported via SDL_WindowSupportsGPUPresentMode / SDL_WindowSupportsGPUSwapchainComposition prior to calling this function. SDL_GPU_PRESENTMODE_VSYNC with SDL_GPU_SWAPCHAINCOMPOSITION_SDR is always supported. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_WindowSupportsGPUPresentMode @sa SDL_WindowSupportsGPUSwapchainComposition<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`window`：an SDL_Window that has been claimed.<br>`swapchain_composition`：the desired composition of the swapchain.<br>`present_mode`：the desired present mode for the swapchain.<br>返回：true if successful, false on error; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
