| <a id="api-5bd0f133f22726a4"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetHapticAutocenter(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic, int autocenter)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47697) | Set the global autocenter of the device. Autocenter should be between 0 and 100. Setting it to 0 will disable autocentering. Device must support the SDL_HAPTIC_AUTOCENTER feature. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetHapticFeatures<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to set autocentering on.<br>`autocenter`：value to set autocenter to (0-100).<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7366039861720e78"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetHapticGain(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic, int gain)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17990) | Set the global gain of the specified haptic device. Device must support the SDL_HAPTIC_GAIN feature. The user may specify the maximum gain by setting the environment variable `SDL_HAPTIC_GAIN_MAX` which should be between 0 and 100. All calls to SDL_SetHapticGain() will scale linearly using `SDL_HAPTIC_GAIN_MAX` as the maximum. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetHapticFeatures<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to set the gain on.<br>`gain`：value to set the gain to, should be between 0 and 100 (0 - 100).<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7af24fd72a8957e8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetHint(sbyte* name, sbyte* value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52342) | Set a hint with normal priority. Hints will not be set if there is an existing override hint or environment variable that takes precedence. You can use SDL_SetHintWithPriority() to set the hint with override priority instead. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetHint @sa SDL_ResetHint @sa SDL_SetHintWithPriority<br>`name`：the hint to set.<br>`value`：the value of the hint variable.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4af9619f9c82b177"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetHintWithPriority(sbyte* name, sbyte* value, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHintPriority priority)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27956) | Set a hint with a specific priority. The priority controls the behavior when setting a hint that already has a value. Hints will replace existing hints of their priority and lower. Environment variables are considered to have override priority. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetHint @sa SDL_ResetHint @sa SDL_SetHint<br>`name`：the hint to set.<br>`value`：the value of the hint variable.<br>`priority`：the SDL_HintPriority level for the hint.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-09b6d7549c359ba4"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetInitialized(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLInitState* state, byte initialized)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27703) | Finish an initialization state transition. This function sets the status of the passed in state to `SDL_INIT_STATUS_INITIALIZED` or `SDL_INIT_STATUS_UNINITIALIZED` and allows any threads waiting for the status to proceed. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ShouldInit @sa SDL_ShouldQuit<br>`state`：the initialization state to check.<br>`initialized`：the new initialization state. |

| <a id="api-0c014265edcd6b6c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetJoystickVirtualAxis(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int axis, short value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18196) | Set the state of an axis on an opened virtual joystick. Please note that values set here will not be applied until the next call to SDL_UpdateJoysticks, which can either be called directly, or can be called indirectly through various other SDL APIs, including, but not limited to the following: SDL_PollEvent, SDL_PumpEvents, SDL_WaitEventTimeout, SDL_WaitEvent. Note that when sending trigger axes, you should scale the value to the full range of Sint16. For example, a trigger at rest would have the value of `SDL_JOYSTICK_AXIS_MIN`. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualButton @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualBall @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualHat @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualTouchpad @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualSensorData<br>`joystick`：the virtual joystick on which to set state.<br>`axis`：the index of the axis on the virtual joystick to update.<br>`value`：the new value for the specified axis.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-775a25a159f7e8fa"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetJoystickVirtualBall(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int ball, short xrel, short yrel)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21970) | Generate ball motion on an opened virtual joystick. Please note that values set here will not be applied until the next call to SDL_UpdateJoysticks, which can either be called directly, or can be called indirectly through various other SDL APIs, including, but not limited to the following: SDL_PollEvent, SDL_PumpEvents, SDL_WaitEventTimeout, SDL_WaitEvent. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualAxis @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualButton @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualHat @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualTouchpad @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualSensorData<br>`joystick`：the virtual joystick on which to set state.<br>`ball`：the index of the ball on the virtual joystick to update.<br>`xrel`：the relative motion on the X axis.<br>`yrel`：the relative motion on the Y axis.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5eeda94256d94b10"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetJoystickVirtualButton(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int button, byte down)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44928) | Set the state of a button on an opened virtual joystick. Please note that values set here will not be applied until the next call to SDL_UpdateJoysticks, which can either be called directly, or can be called indirectly through various other SDL APIs, including, but not limited to the following: SDL_PollEvent, SDL_PumpEvents, SDL_WaitEventTimeout, SDL_WaitEvent. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualAxis @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualBall @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualHat @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualTouchpad @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualSensorData<br>`joystick`：the virtual joystick on which to set state.<br>`button`：the index of the button on the virtual joystick to update.<br>`down`：true if the button is pressed, false otherwise.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4053e5f205d0f680"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetJoystickVirtualHat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int hat, byte value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38417) | Set the state of a hat on an opened virtual joystick. Please note that values set here will not be applied until the next call to SDL_UpdateJoysticks, which can either be called directly, or can be called indirectly through various other SDL APIs, including, but not limited to the following: SDL_PollEvent, SDL_PumpEvents, SDL_WaitEventTimeout, SDL_WaitEvent. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualAxis @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualButton @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualBall @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualTouchpad @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualSensorData<br>`joystick`：the virtual joystick on which to set state.<br>`hat`：the index of the hat on the virtual joystick to update.<br>`value`：the new value for the specified hat.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e8c4a4ab3de6a215"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetJoystickVirtualTouchpad(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLJoystick* joystick, int touchpad, int finger, byte down, float x, float y, float pressure)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24346) | Set touchpad finger state on an opened virtual joystick. Please note that values set here will not be applied until the next call to SDL_UpdateJoysticks, which can either be called directly, or can be called indirectly through various other SDL APIs, including, but not limited to the following: SDL_PollEvent, SDL_PumpEvents, SDL_WaitEventTimeout, SDL_WaitEvent. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualAxis @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualButton @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualBall @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualHat @sa SDL_SetJoystickVirtualSensorData<br>`joystick`：the virtual joystick on which to set state.<br>`touchpad`：the index of the touchpad on the virtual joystick to update.<br>`finger`：the index of the finger on the touchpad to set.<br>`down`：true if the finger is pressed, false if the finger is released.<br>`x`：the x coordinate of the finger on the touchpad, normalized 0 to 1, with the origin in the upper left.<br>`y`：the y coordinate of the finger on the touchpad, normalized 0 to 1, with the origin in the upper left.<br>`pressure`：the pressure of the finger.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a32618b77cd9844e"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetLogOutputFunction(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, int, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLLogPriority, sbyte*, void> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30763) | Replace the default log output function with one of your own. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetDefaultLogOutputFunction @sa SDL_GetLogOutputFunction<br>`callback`：an SDL_LogOutputFunction to call instead of the default.<br>`userdata`：a pointer that is passed to `callback`. |
| <a id="api-cf8391c502f77e9e"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetLogPriorities(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLLogPriority priority)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21357) | Set the priority of all log categories. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ResetLogPriorities @sa SDL_SetLogPriority<br>`priority`：the SDL_LogPriority to assign. |
| <a id="api-b3bc04a23150f02e"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetLogPriority(int category, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLLogPriority priority)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41400) | Set the priority of a particular log category. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetLogPriority @sa SDL_ResetLogPriorities @sa SDL_SetLogPriorities<br>`category`：the category to assign a priority to.<br>`priority`：the SDL_LogPriority to assign. |
| <a id="api-705e0f510ed26131"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetLogPriorityPrefix(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLLogPriority priority, sbyte* prefix)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22870) | Set the text prepended to log messages of a given priority. By default SDL_LOG_PRIORITY_INFO and below have no prefix, and SDL_LOG_PRIORITY_WARN and higher have a prefix showing their priority, e.g. "WARNING: ". This function makes a copy of its string argument, **prefix**, so it is not necessary to keep the value of **prefix** alive after the call returns. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetLogPriorities @sa SDL_SetLogPriority<br>`priority`：the SDL_LogPriority to modify.<br>`prefix`：the prefix to use for that log priority, or NULL to use no prefix.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-5041d8239776b76c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetMemoryFunctions(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<ulong, void*> malloc_func, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<ulong, ulong, void*> calloc_func, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, ulong, void*> realloc_func, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, void> free_func)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19334) | Replace SDL's memory allocation functions with a custom set. It is not safe to call this function once any allocations have been made, as future calls to SDL_free will use the new allocator, even if they came from an SDL_malloc made with the old one! If used, usually this needs to be the first call made into the SDL library, if not the very first thing done at program startup time. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, but one should not replace the memory functions once any allocations are made! @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetMemoryFunctions @sa SDL_GetOriginalMemoryFunctions<br>`malloc_func`：custom malloc function.<br>`calloc_func`：custom calloc function.<br>`realloc_func`：custom realloc function.<br>`free_func`：custom free function.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a7ebfbc17a0b4923"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetModState(ushort modstate)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44280) | Set the current key modifier state for the keyboard. The inverse of SDL_GetModState(), SDL_SetModState() allows you to impose modifier key states on your application. Simply pass your desired modifier states into `modstate`. This value may be a bitwise, OR'd combination of SDL_Keymod values. This does not change the keyboard state, only the key modifier flags that SDL reports. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetModState<br>`modstate`：the desired SDL_Keymod for the keyboard. |
| <a id="api-5de0d7a70bce7d10"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetNumberProperty(uint props, sbyte* name, long value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18933) | Set an integer property in a group of properties. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetNumberProperty<br>`props`：the properties to modify.<br>`name`：the name of the property to modify.<br>`value`：the new value of the property.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-812208cd4afd9d43"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetPaletteColors(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPalette* palette, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLColor* colors, int firstcolor, int ncolors)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26362) | Set a range of colors in a palette. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, as long as the palette is not modified or destroyed in another thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`palette`：the SDL_Palette structure to modify.<br>`colors`：an array of SDL_Color structures to copy into the palette.<br>`firstcolor`：the index of the first palette entry to modify.<br>`ncolors`：the number of entries to modify.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a72117761eea1467"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetPointerProperty(uint props, sbyte* name, void* value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50559) | Set a pointer property in a group of properties. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPointerProperty @sa SDL_HasProperty @sa SDL_SetBooleanProperty @sa SDL_SetFloatProperty @sa SDL_SetNumberProperty @sa SDL_SetPointerPropertyWithCleanup @sa SDL_SetStringProperty<br>`props`：the properties to modify.<br>`name`：the name of the property to modify.<br>`value`：the new value of the property, or NULL to delete the property.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-04cd94b864bad15c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetPointerPropertyWithCleanup(uint props, sbyte* name, void* value, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, void*, void> cleanup, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44528) | Set a pointer property in a group of properties with a cleanup function that is called when the property is deleted. The cleanup function is also called if setting the property fails for any reason. For simply setting basic data types, like numbers, bools, or strings, use SDL_SetNumberProperty, SDL_SetBooleanProperty, or SDL_SetStringProperty instead, as those functions will handle cleanup on your behalf. This function is only for more complex, custom data. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPointerProperty @sa SDL_SetPointerProperty @sa SDL_CleanupPropertyCallback<br>`props`：the properties to modify.<br>`name`：the name of the property to modify.<br>`value`：the new value of the property, or NULL to delete the property.<br>`cleanup`：the function to call when this property is deleted, or NULL if no cleanup is necessary.<br>`userdata`：a pointer that is passed to the cleanup function.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c181817752b2ecdb"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetPrimarySelectionText(sbyte* text)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29865) | Put UTF-8 text into the primary selection. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetPrimarySelectionText @sa SDL_HasPrimarySelectionText<br>`text`：the text to store in the primary selection.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-eeb9138a730a005f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRelativeMouseTransform(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, ulong, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow*, uint, float*, float*, void> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20542) | Set a user-defined function by which to transform relative mouse inputs. This overrides the relative system scale and relative speed scale hints. Should be called prior to enabling relative mouse mode, fails otherwise. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`callback`：a callback used to transform relative mouse motion, or NULL for default behavior.<br>`userdata`：a pointer that will be passed to `callback`.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f64be9e4c830a2f0"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderClipRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52391) | Set the clip rectangle for rendering on the specified target. Each render target has its own clip rectangle. This function sets the cliprect for the current render target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderClipRect @sa SDL_RenderClipEnabled<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`rect`：an SDL_Rect structure representing the clip area, relative to the viewport, or NULL to disable clipping.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f851bf2097a21bd7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderColorScale(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float scale)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25012) | Set the color scale used for render operations. The color scale is an additional scale multiplied into the pixel color value while rendering. This can be used to adjust the brightness of colors during HDR rendering, or changing HDR video brightness when playing on an SDR display. The color scale does not affect the alpha channel, only the color brightness. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderColorScale<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`scale`：the color scale value.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8e68110f479135b2"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderDrawBlendMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, uint blendMode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27556) | Set the blend mode used for drawing operations (Fill and Line). If the blend mode is not supported, the closest supported mode is chosen. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderDrawBlendMode<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`blendMode`：the SDL_BlendMode to use for blending.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b0c1bf55760d9ce5"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderDrawColor(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, byte r, byte g, byte b, byte a)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46786) | Set the color used for drawing operations. Set the color for drawing or filling rectangles, lines, and points, and for SDL_RenderClear(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderDrawColor @sa SDL_SetRenderDrawColorFloat<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`r`：the red value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`g`：the green value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`b`：the blue value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`a`：the alpha value used to draw on the rendering target; usually `SDL_ALPHA_OPAQUE` (255). Use SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode to specify how the alpha channel is used.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9a9ce4078547e0be"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderDrawColorFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float r, float g, float b, float a)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25876) | Set the color used for drawing operations (Rect, Line and Clear). Set the color for drawing or filling rectangles, lines, and points, and for SDL_RenderClear(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderDrawColorFloat @sa SDL_SetRenderDrawColor<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`r`：the red value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`g`：the green value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`b`：the blue value used to draw on the rendering target.<br>`a`：the alpha value used to draw on the rendering target. Use SDL_SetRenderDrawBlendMode to specify how the alpha channel is used.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b65385ae27333ef3"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderLogicalPresentation(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, int w, int h, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRendererLogicalPresentation mode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46012) | Set a device-independent resolution and presentation mode for rendering. This function sets the width and height of the logical rendering output. The renderer will act as if the current render target is always the requested dimensions, scaling to the actual resolution as necessary. This can be useful for games that expect a fixed size, but would like to scale the output to whatever is available, regardless of how a user resizes a window, or if the display is high DPI. Logical presentation can be used with both render target textures and the renderer's window; the state is unique to each render target, and this function sets the state for the current render target. It might be useful to draw to a texture that matches the window dimensions with logical presentation enabled, and then draw that texture across the entire window with logical presentation disabled. Be careful not to render both with logical presentation enabled, however, as this could produce double-letterboxing, etc. You can disable logical coordinates by setting the mode to SDL_LOGICAL_PRESENTATION_DISABLED, and in that case you get the full pixel resolution of the render target; it is safe to toggle logical presentation during the rendering of a frame: perhaps most of the rendering is done to specific dimensions but to make fonts look sharp, the app turns off logical presentation while drawing text, for example. You can convert coordinates in an event into rendering coordinates using SDL_ConvertEventToRenderCoordinates(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ConvertEventToRenderCoordinates @sa SDL_GetRenderLogicalPresentation @sa SDL_GetRenderLogicalPresentationRect<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`w`：the width of the logical resolution.<br>`h`：the height of the logical resolution.<br>`mode`：the presentation mode used.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6acea9ed7b9e34af"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderScale(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, float scaleX, float scaleY)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19017) | Set the drawing scale for rendering on the current target. The drawing coordinates are scaled by the x/y scaling factors before they are used by the renderer. This allows resolution independent drawing with a single coordinate system. If this results in scaling or subpixel drawing by the rendering backend, it will be handled using the appropriate quality hints. For best results use integer scaling factors. Each render target has its own scale. This function sets the scale for the current render target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderScale<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`scaleX`：the horizontal scaling factor.<br>`scaleY`：the vertical scaling factor.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-96ee5fc2e89ca77b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderTarget(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19690) | Set a texture as the current rendering target. The default render target is the window for which the renderer was created. To stop rendering to a texture and render to the window again, call this function with a NULL `texture`. Viewport, cliprect, scale, and logical presentation are unique to each render target. Get and set functions for these states apply to the current render target set by this function, and those states persist on each target when the current render target changes. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderTarget<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`texture`：the targeted texture, which must be created with the `SDL_TEXTUREACCESS_TARGET` flag, or NULL to render to the window instead of a texture.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-53057e56c74e191a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderTextureAddressMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTextureAddressMode u_mode, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTextureAddressMode v_mode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16956) | Set the texture addressing mode used in SDL_RenderGeometry(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_RenderGeometry @sa SDL_RenderGeometryRaw @sa SDL_GetRenderTextureAddressMode<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`u_mode`：the SDL_TextureAddressMode to use for horizontal texture coordinates in SDL_RenderGeometry().<br>`v_mode`：the SDL_TextureAddressMode to use for vertical texture coordinates in SDL_RenderGeometry().<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-2258b42fcabe2d06"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderVSync(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, int vsync)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23156) | Toggle VSync of the given renderer. When a renderer is created, vsync defaults to SDL_RENDERER_VSYNC_DISABLED. The `vsync` parameter can be 1 to synchronize present with every vertical refresh, 2 to synchronize present with every second vertical refresh, etc., SDL_RENDERER_VSYNC_ADAPTIVE for late swap tearing (adaptive vsync), or SDL_RENDERER_VSYNC_DISABLED to disable. Not every value is supported by every driver, so you should check the return value to see whether the requested setting is supported. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderVSync<br>`renderer`：the renderer to toggle.<br>`vsync`：the vertical refresh sync interval.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-93843c1a8022773e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetRenderViewport(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRenderer* renderer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40961) | Set the drawing area for rendering on the current target. Drawing will clip to this area (separately from any clipping done with SDL_SetRenderClipRect), and the top left of the area will become coordinate (0, 0) for future drawing commands. The area's width and height must be >= 0. Each render target has its own viewport. This function sets the viewport for the current render target. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetRenderViewport @sa SDL_RenderViewportSet<br>`renderer`：the rendering context.<br>`rect`：the SDL_Rect structure representing the drawing area, or NULL to set the viewport to the entire target.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ae1f3c4ac2c5ccad"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetScancodeName(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScancode scancode, sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22151) | Set a human-readable name for a scancode. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetScancodeName<br>`scancode`：the desired SDL_Scancode.<br>`name`：the name to use for the scancode, encoded as UTF-8. The string is not copied, so the pointer given to this function must stay valid while SDL is being used.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-25b5d34de8dd991a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetStringProperty(uint props, sbyte* name, sbyte* value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36621) | Set a string property in a group of properties. This function makes a copy of the string; the caller does not have to preserve the data after this call completes. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetStringProperty<br>`props`：the properties to modify.<br>`name`：the name of the property to modify.<br>`value`：the new value of the property, or NULL to delete the property.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9e9e51b0a1896e34"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetSurfaceAlphaMod(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, byte alpha)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41702) | Set an additional alpha value used in blit operations. When this surface is blitted, during the blit operation the source alpha value is modulated by this alpha value according to the following formula: `srcA = srcA * (alpha / 255)` @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetSurfaceAlphaMod @sa SDL_SetSurfaceColorMod<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to update.<br>`alpha`：the alpha value multiplied into blit operations.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d3e173276fccc0ea"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetSurfaceBlendMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, uint blendMode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27584) | Set the blend mode used for blit operations. To copy a surface to another surface (or texture) without blending with the existing data, the blendmode of the SOURCE surface should be set to `SDL_BLENDMODE_NONE`. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetSurfaceBlendMode<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to update.<br>`blendMode`：the SDL_BlendMode to use for blit blending.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f91ed32cc6e627f6"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetSurfaceClipRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30942) | Set the clipping rectangle for a surface. When `surface` is the destination of a blit, only the area within the clip rectangle is drawn into. Note that blits are automatically clipped to the edges of the source and destination surfaces. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetSurfaceClipRect<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to be clipped.<br>`rect`：the SDL_Rect structure representing the clipping rectangle, or NULL to disable clipping.<br>返回：true if the rectangle intersects the surface, otherwise false and blits will be completely clipped. |
| <a id="api-3aa5ae7c2e91db5f"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetSurfaceColorKey(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, byte enabled, uint key)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37574) | Set the color key (transparent pixel) in a surface. The color key defines a pixel value that will be treated as transparent in a blit. For example, one can use this to specify that cyan pixels should be considered transparent, and therefore not rendered. It is a pixel of the format used by the surface, as generated by SDL_MapRGB(). @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetSurfaceColorKey @sa SDL_SetSurfaceRLE @sa SDL_SurfaceHasColorKey<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to update.<br>`enabled`：true to enable color key, false to disable color key.<br>`key`：the transparent pixel.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6a79cc3de7988878"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetSurfaceColorMod(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, byte r, byte g, byte b)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51660) | Set an additional color value multiplied into blit operations. When this surface is blitted, during the blit operation each source color channel is modulated by the appropriate color value according to the following formula: `srcC = srcC * (color / 255)` @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetSurfaceColorMod @sa SDL_SetSurfaceAlphaMod<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to update.<br>`r`：the red color value multiplied into blit operations.<br>`g`：the green color value multiplied into blit operations.<br>`b`：the blue color value multiplied into blit operations.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ca7549cfa7500e0a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetSurfaceColorspace(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLColorspace colorspace)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52019) | Set the colorspace used by a surface. Setting the colorspace doesn't change the pixels, only how they are interpreted in color operations. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetSurfaceColorspace<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to update.<br>`colorspace`：an SDL_Colorspace value describing the surface colorspace.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-bdc650c0234ab679"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetSurfacePalette(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPalette* palette)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29670) | Set the palette used by a surface. Setting the palette keeps an internal reference to the palette, which can be safely destroyed afterwards. A single palette can be shared with many surfaces. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreatePalette @sa SDL_GetSurfacePalette<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to update.<br>`palette`：the SDL_Palette structure to use.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6d6ae690a880169c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetSurfaceRle(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface, byte enabled)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17604) | Set the RLE acceleration hint for a surface. If RLE is enabled, color key and alpha blending blits are much faster, but the surface must be locked before directly accessing the pixels. @threadsafety This function can be called on different threads with different surfaces. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_BlitSurface @sa SDL_LockSurface @sa SDL_UnlockSurface<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to optimize.<br>`enabled`：true to enable RLE acceleration, false to disable it.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ddb48f61900390aa"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTextInputArea(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect, int cursor)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47533) | Set the area used to type Unicode text input. Native input methods may place a window with word suggestions near the cursor, without covering the text being entered. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextInputArea @sa SDL_StartTextInput<br>`window`：the window for which to set the text input area.<br>`rect`：the SDL_Rect representing the text input area, in window coordinates, or NULL to clear it.<br>`cursor`：the offset of the current cursor location relative to `rect->x`, in window coordinates.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f8c119649b4b62e6"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTextureAlphaMod(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, byte alpha)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45182) | Set an additional alpha value multiplied into render copy operations. When this texture is rendered, during the copy operation the source alpha value is modulated by this alpha value according to the following formula: `srcA = srcA * (alpha / 255)` Alpha modulation is not always supported by the renderer; it will return false if alpha modulation is not supported. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextureAlphaMod @sa SDL_SetTextureAlphaModFloat @sa SDL_SetTextureColorMod<br>`texture`：the texture to update.<br>`alpha`：the source alpha value multiplied into copy operations.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-6cdca1237d4a6315"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTextureAlphaModFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, float alpha)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36885) | Set an additional alpha value multiplied into render copy operations. When this texture is rendered, during the copy operation the source alpha value is modulated by this alpha value according to the following formula: `srcA = srcA * alpha` Alpha modulation is not always supported by the renderer; it will return false if alpha modulation is not supported. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextureAlphaModFloat @sa SDL_SetTextureAlphaMod @sa SDL_SetTextureColorModFloat<br>`texture`：the texture to update.<br>`alpha`：the source alpha value multiplied into copy operations.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c7c8116fe39bb568"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTextureBlendMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, uint blendMode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23319) | Set the blend mode for a texture, used by SDL_RenderTexture(). If the blend mode is not supported, the closest supported mode is chosen and this function returns false. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextureBlendMode<br>`texture`：the texture to update.<br>`blendMode`：the SDL_BlendMode to use for texture blending.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1be89016efaa06b4"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTextureColorMod(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, byte r, byte g, byte b)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41492) | Set an additional color value multiplied into render copy operations. When this texture is rendered, during the copy operation each source color channel is modulated by the appropriate color value according to the following formula: `srcC = srcC * (color / 255)` Color modulation is not always supported by the renderer; it will return false if color modulation is not supported. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextureColorMod @sa SDL_SetTextureAlphaMod @sa SDL_SetTextureColorModFloat<br>`texture`：the texture to update.<br>`r`：the red color value multiplied into copy operations.<br>`g`：the green color value multiplied into copy operations.<br>`b`：the blue color value multiplied into copy operations.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e3bd8d24ad0aa691"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTextureColorModFloat(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, float r, float g, float b)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39642) | Set an additional color value multiplied into render copy operations. When this texture is rendered, during the copy operation each source color channel is modulated by the appropriate color value according to the following formula: `srcC = srcC * color` Color modulation is not always supported by the renderer; it will return false if color modulation is not supported. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextureColorModFloat @sa SDL_SetTextureAlphaModFloat @sa SDL_SetTextureColorMod<br>`texture`：the texture to update.<br>`r`：the red color value multiplied into copy operations.<br>`g`：the green color value multiplied into copy operations.<br>`b`：the blue color value multiplied into copy operations.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-750ace1298f07752"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTexturePalette(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPalette* palette)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25040) | Set the palette used by a texture. Setting the palette keeps an internal reference to the palette, which can be safely destroyed afterwards. A single palette can be shared with many textures. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_CreatePalette @sa SDL_GetTexturePalette<br>`texture`：the texture to update.<br>`palette`：the SDL_Palette structure to use.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0f684418589fb39c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTextureScaleMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScaleMode scaleMode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50726) | Set the scale mode used for texture scale operations. The default texture scale mode is SDL_SCALEMODE_LINEAR. If the scale mode is not supported, the closest supported mode is chosen. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTextureScaleMode<br>`texture`：the texture to update.<br>`scaleMode`：the SDL_ScaleMode to use for texture scaling.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-4f2969e279ed80e6"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTls(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAtomicInt* id, void* value, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, void> destructor)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35617) | Set the current thread's value associated with a thread local storage ID. If the thread local storage ID is not initialized (the value is 0), a new ID will be created in a thread-safe way, so all calls using a pointer to the same ID will refer to the same local storage. Note that replacing a value from a previous call to this function on the same thread does _not_ call the previous value's destructor! `destructor` can be NULL; it is assumed that `value` does not need to be cleaned up if so. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTLS<br>`id`：a pointer to the thread local storage ID, may not be NULL.<br>`value`：the value to associate with the ID for the current thread.<br>`destructor`：a function called when the thread exits, to free the value, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-791548ee76a237ab"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTrayEntryCallback(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* entry, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry*, void> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29543) | Sets a callback to be invoked when the entry is selected. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTrayEntries @sa SDL_InsertTrayEntryAt<br>`entry`：the entry to be updated.<br>`callback`：a callback to be invoked when the entry is selected.<br>`userdata`：an optional pointer to pass extra data to the callback when it will be invoked. |
| <a id="api-dd4ee503c0aca70f"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTrayEntryChecked(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* entry, byte @checked)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18633) | Sets whether or not an entry is checked. The entry must have been created with the SDL_TRAYENTRY_CHECKBOX flag. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTrayEntries @sa SDL_InsertTrayEntryAt @sa SDL_GetTrayEntryChecked<br>`entry`：the entry to be updated.<br>`checked`：true if the entry should be checked; false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-8ab1d847524b230c"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTrayEntryEnabled(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* entry, byte enabled)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17003) | Sets whether or not an entry is enabled. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTrayEntries @sa SDL_InsertTrayEntryAt @sa SDL_GetTrayEntryEnabled<br>`entry`：the entry to be updated.<br>`enabled`：true if the entry should be enabled; false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-497c8bc3ea73cb04"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTrayEntryLabel(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTrayEntry* entry, sbyte* label)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46438) | Sets the label of an entry. An entry cannot change between a separator and an ordinary entry; that is, it is not possible to set a non-NULL label on an entry that has a NULL label (separators), or to set a NULL label to an entry that has a non-NULL label. The function will silently fail if that happens. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetTrayEntries @sa SDL_InsertTrayEntryAt @sa SDL_GetTrayEntryLabel<br>`entry`：the entry to be updated.<br>`label`：the new label for the entry in UTF-8 encoding. |
| <a id="api-ab5d2ae9b3e36788"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTrayIcon(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTray* tray, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* icon)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17139) | Updates the system tray icon's icon. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateTray<br>`tray`：the tray icon to be updated.<br>`icon`：the new icon. May be NULL. |
| <a id="api-28607289d7ad13af"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetTrayTooltip(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTray* tray, sbyte* tooltip)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39967) | Updates the system tray icon's tooltip. @threadsafety This function should be called on the thread that created the tray. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateTray<br>`tray`：the tray icon to be updated.<br>`tooltip`：the new tooltip in UTF-8 encoding. May be NULL. |
| <a id="api-6039c96fbf1532f0"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowAlwaysOnTop(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, byte on_top)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42606) | Set the window to always be above the others. This will add or remove the window's `SDL_WINDOW_ALWAYS_ON_TOP` flag. This will bring the window to the front and keep the window above the rest. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowFlags<br>`window`：the window of which to change the always on top state.<br>`on_top`：true to set the window always on top, false to disable.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1dad864cb1398a5e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowAspectRatio(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, float min_aspect, float max_aspect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34360) | Request that the aspect ratio of a window's client area be set. The aspect ratio is the ratio of width divided by height, e.g. 2560x1600 would be 1.6. Larger aspect ratios are wider and smaller aspect ratios are narrower. If, at the time of this request, the window in a fixed-size state, such as maximized or fullscreen, the request will be deferred until the window exits this state and becomes resizable again. On some windowing systems, this request is asynchronous and the new window aspect ratio may not have have been applied immediately upon the return of this function. If an immediate change is required, call SDL_SyncWindow() to block until the changes have taken effect. When the window size changes, an SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_RESIZED event will be emitted with the new window dimensions. Note that the new dimensions may not match the exact aspect ratio requested, as some windowing systems can restrict the window size in certain scenarios (e.g. constraining the size of the content area to remain within the usable desktop bounds). Additionally, as this is just a request, it can be denied by the windowing system. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowAspectRatio @sa SDL_SyncWindow<br>`window`：the window to change.<br>`min_aspect`：the minimum aspect ratio of the window, or 0.0f for no limit.<br>`max_aspect`：the maximum aspect ratio of the window, or 0.0f for no limit.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0849f4dcf0c592f0"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowBordered(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, byte bordered)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37960) | Set the border state of a window. This will add or remove the window's `SDL_WINDOW_BORDERLESS` flag and add or remove the border from the actual window. This is a no-op if the window's border already matches the requested state. You can't change the border state of a fullscreen window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowFlags<br>`window`：the window of which to change the border state.<br>`bordered`：false to remove border, true to add border.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-bd550ceab3438a60"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowFillDocument(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, byte fill)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22065) | Set the window to fill the current document space (Emscripten only). This will add or remove the window's `SDL_WINDOW_FILL_DOCUMENT` flag. Currently this flag only applies to the Emscripten target. When enabled, the canvas element fills the entire document. Resize events will be generated as the browser window is resized, as that will adjust the canvas size as well. The canvas will cover anything else on the page, including any controls provided by Emscripten in its generated HTML file (in fact, any elements on the page that aren't the canvas will be moved into a hidden `div` element). Often times this is desirable for a browser-based game, but it means several things that we expect of an SDL window on other platforms might not work as expected, such as minimum window sizes and aspect ratios. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowFlags<br>`window`：the window of which to change the fill-document state.<br>`fill`：true to set the window to fill the document, false to disable.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0bfcd2a506ecdbc8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowFocusable(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, byte focusable)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49823) | Set whether the window may have input focus. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to set focusable state.<br>`focusable`：true to allow input focus, false to not allow input focus.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ff38d6fb916d8b27"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowFullscreen(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, byte fullscreen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30400) | Request that the window's fullscreen state be changed. By default a window in fullscreen state uses borderless fullscreen desktop mode, but a specific exclusive display mode can be set using SDL_SetWindowFullscreenMode(). On some windowing systems this request is asynchronous and the new fullscreen state may not have have been applied immediately upon the return of this function. If an immediate change is required, call SDL_SyncWindow() to block until the changes have taken effect. When the window state changes, an SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_ENTER_FULLSCREEN or SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_LEAVE_FULLSCREEN event will be emitted. Note that, as this is just a request, it can be denied by the windowing system. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowFullscreenMode @sa SDL_SetWindowFullscreenMode @sa SDL_SyncWindow @sa SDL_WINDOW_FULLSCREEN<br>`window`：the window to change.<br>`fullscreen`：true for fullscreen mode, false for windowed mode.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-52b2170eb3b9a6a5"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowFullscreenMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDisplayMode* mode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50942) | Set the display mode to use when a window is visible and fullscreen. This only affects the display mode used when the window is fullscreen. To change the window size when the window is not fullscreen, use SDL_SetWindowSize(). If the window is currently in the fullscreen state, this request is asynchronous on some windowing systems and the new mode dimensions may not be applied immediately upon the return of this function. If an immediate change is required, call SDL_SyncWindow() to block until the changes have taken effect. When the new mode takes effect, an SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_RESIZED and/or an SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED event will be emitted with the new mode dimensions. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowFullscreenMode @sa SDL_SetWindowFullscreen @sa SDL_SyncWindow<br>`window`：the window to affect.<br>`mode`：a pointer to the display mode to use, which can be NULL for borderless fullscreen desktop mode, or one of the fullscreen modes returned by SDL_GetFullscreenDisplayModes() to set an exclusive fullscreen mode.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-dcafa001e2d4e37e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowHitTest(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLPoint*, void*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHitTestResult> callback, void* callback_data)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37051) | Provide a callback that decides if a window region has special properties. Normally windows are dragged and resized by decorations provided by the system window manager (a title bar, borders, etc), but for some apps, it makes sense to drag them from somewhere else inside the window itself; for example, one might have a borderless window that wants to be draggable from any part, or simulate its own title bar, etc. This function lets the app provide a callback that designates pieces of a given window as special. This callback is run during event processing if we need to tell the OS to treat a region of the window specially; the use of this callback is known as "hit testing." Mouse input may not be delivered to your application if it is within a special area; the OS will often apply that input to moving the window or resizing the window and not deliver it to the application. Specifying NULL for a callback disables hit-testing. Hit-testing is disabled by default. Platforms that don't support this functionality will return false unconditionally, even if you're attempting to disable hit-testing. Your callback may fire at any time, and its firing does not indicate any specific behavior (for example, on Windows, this certainly might fire when the OS is deciding whether to drag your window, but it fires for lots of other reasons, too, some unrelated to anything you probably care about _and when the mouse isn't actually at the location it is testing_). Since this can fire at any time, you should try to keep your callback efficient, devoid of allocations, etc. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window to set hit-testing on.<br>`callback`：the function to call when doing a hit-test.<br>`callback_data`：an app-defined void pointer passed to **callback**.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-97ceef1562a11137"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowIcon(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* icon)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28785) | Set the icon for a window. If this function is passed a surface with alternate representations added using SDL_AddSurfaceAlternateImage(), the surface will be interpreted as the content to be used for 100% display scale, and the alternate representations will be used for high DPI situations. For example, if the original surface is 32x32, then on a 2x macOS display or 200% display scale on Windows, a 64x64 version of the image will be used, if available. If a matching version of the image isn't available, the closest larger size image will be downscaled to the appropriate size and be used instead, if available. Otherwise, the closest smaller image will be upscaled and be used instead. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AddSurfaceAlternateImage<br>`window`：the window to change.<br>`icon`：an SDL_Surface structure containing the icon for the window.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-56051b71d2d3e45e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowKeyboardGrab(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, byte grabbed)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23796) | Set a window's keyboard grab mode. Keyboard grab enables capture of system keyboard shortcuts like Alt+Tab or the Meta/Super key. Note that not all system keyboard shortcuts can be captured by applications (one example is Ctrl+Alt+Del on Windows). This is primarily intended for specialized applications such as VNC clients or VM frontends. Normal games should not use keyboard grab. When keyboard grab is enabled, SDL will continue to handle Alt+Tab when the window is full-screen to ensure the user is not trapped in your application. If you have a custom keyboard shortcut to exit fullscreen mode, you may suppress this behavior with `SDL_HINT_ALLOW_ALT_TAB_WHILE_GRABBED`. If the caller enables a grab while another window is currently grabbed, the other window loses its grab in favor of the caller's window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowKeyboardGrab @sa SDL_SetWindowMouseGrab<br>`window`：the window for which the keyboard grab mode should be set.<br>`grabbed`：this is true to grab keyboard, and false to release.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7a95ca754de3eb4b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowMaximumSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int max_w, int max_h)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26454) | Set the maximum size of a window's client area. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowMaximumSize @sa SDL_SetWindowMinimumSize<br>`window`：the window to change.<br>`max_w`：the maximum width of the window, or 0 for no limit.<br>`max_h`：the maximum height of the window, or 0 for no limit.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b202257d3dff98b8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowMinimumSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int min_w, int min_h)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39689) | Set the minimum size of a window's client area. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowMinimumSize @sa SDL_SetWindowMaximumSize<br>`window`：the window to change.<br>`min_w`：the minimum width of the window, or 0 for no limit.<br>`min_h`：the minimum height of the window, or 0 for no limit.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-648c098ce3b85464"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowModal(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, byte modal)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21408) | Toggle the state of the window as modal. To enable modal status on a window, the window must currently be the child window of a parent, or toggling modal status on will fail. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowParent @sa SDL_WINDOW_MODAL<br>`window`：the window on which to set the modal state.<br>`modal`：true to toggle modal status on, false to toggle it off.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-dc22cec467195b29"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowMouseGrab(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, byte grabbed)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41009) | Set a window's mouse grab mode. Mouse grab confines the mouse cursor to the window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowMouseRect @sa SDL_SetWindowMouseRect @sa SDL_SetWindowKeyboardGrab<br>`window`：the window for which the mouse grab mode should be set.<br>`grabbed`：this is true to grab mouse, and false to release.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-f0729c45858fa87e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowMouseRect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32615) | Confines the cursor to the specified area of a window. Note that this does NOT grab the cursor, it only defines the area a cursor is restricted to when the window has mouse focus. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowMouseRect @sa SDL_GetWindowMouseGrab @sa SDL_SetWindowMouseGrab<br>`window`：the window that will be associated with the barrier.<br>`rect`：a rectangle area in window-relative coordinates. If NULL the barrier for the specified window will be destroyed.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8ee6951078359b9c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowOpacity(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, float opacity)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25622) | Set the opacity for a window. The parameter `opacity` will be clamped internally between 0.0f (transparent) and 1.0f (opaque). This function also returns false if setting the opacity isn't supported. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowOpacity<br>`window`：the window which will be made transparent or opaque.<br>`opacity`：the opacity value (0.0f - transparent, 1.0f - opaque).<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-73b1e8b17a3eca0b"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowParent(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* parent)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51471) | Set the window as a child of a parent window. If the window is already the child of an existing window, it will be reparented to the new owner. Setting the parent window to NULL unparents the window and removes child window status. If a parent window is hidden or destroyed, the operation will be recursively applied to child windows. Child windows hidden with the parent that did not have their hidden status explicitly set will be restored when the parent is shown. Attempting to set the parent of a window that is currently in the modal state will fail. Use SDL_SetWindowModal() to cancel the modal status before attempting to change the parent. Popup windows cannot change parents and attempts to do so will fail. Setting a parent window that is currently the sibling or descendent of the child window results in undefined behavior. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowModal<br>`window`：the window that should become the child of a parent.<br>`parent`：the new parent window for the child window.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-8aad299c9ad23fdf"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowPosition(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int x, int y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41932) | Request that the window's position be set. If the window is in an exclusive fullscreen or maximized state, this request has no effect. This can be used to reposition fullscreen-desktop windows onto a different display, however, as exclusive fullscreen windows are locked to a specific display, they can only be repositioned programmatically via SDL_SetWindowFullscreenMode(). On some windowing systems this request is asynchronous and the new coordinates may not have have been applied immediately upon the return of this function. If an immediate change is required, call SDL_SyncWindow() to block until the changes have taken effect. When the window position changes, an SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_MOVED event will be emitted with the window's new coordinates. Note that the new coordinates may not match the exact coordinates requested, as some windowing systems can restrict the position of the window in certain scenarios (e.g. constraining the position so the window is always within desktop bounds). Additionally, as this is just a request, it can be denied by the windowing system. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowPosition @sa SDL_SyncWindow<br>`window`：the window to reposition.<br>`x`：the x coordinate of the window, or `SDL_WINDOWPOS_CENTERED` or `SDL_WINDOWPOS_UNDEFINED`.<br>`y`：the y coordinate of the window, or `SDL_WINDOWPOS_CENTERED` or `SDL_WINDOWPOS_UNDEFINED`.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e0eece02d192abbb"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowProgressState(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLProgressState state)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46215) | Sets the state of the progress bar for the given window’s taskbar icon. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`window`：the window whose progress state is to be modified.<br>`state`：the progress state. `SDL_PROGRESS_STATE_NONE` stops displaying the progress bar.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-342ae3e75d5f569a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowProgressValue(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, float value)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26925) | Sets the value of the progress bar for the given window’s taskbar icon. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0.<br>`window`：the window whose progress value is to be modified.<br>`value`：the progress value in the range of [0.0f - 1.0f]. If the value is outside the valid range, it gets clamped.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-978ec5774a6cfcbc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowRelativeMouseMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, byte enabled)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23728) | Set relative mouse mode for a window. While the window has focus and relative mouse mode is enabled, the cursor is hidden, the mouse position is constrained to the window, and SDL will report continuous relative mouse motion even if the mouse is at the edge of the window. If you'd like to keep the mouse position fixed while in relative mode you can use SDL_SetWindowMouseRect(). If you'd like the cursor to be at a specific location when relative mode ends, you should use SDL_WarpMouseInWindow() before disabling relative mode. This function will flush any pending mouse motion for this window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowRelativeMouseMode<br>`window`：the window to change.<br>`enabled`：true to enable relative mode, false to disable.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-32509f7e52c62520"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowResizable(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, byte resizable)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30039) | Set the user-resizable state of a window. This will add or remove the window's `SDL_WINDOW_RESIZABLE` flag and allow/disallow user resizing of the window. This is a no-op if the window's resizable state already matches the requested state. You can't change the resizable state of a fullscreen window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowFlags<br>`window`：the window of which to change the resizable state.<br>`resizable`：true to allow resizing, false to disallow.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-1654f3df7bf64a65"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowShape(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* shape)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36114) | Set the shape of a transparent window. This sets the alpha channel of a transparent window and any fully transparent areas are also transparent to mouse clicks. If you are using something besides the SDL render API, then you are responsible for drawing the alpha channel of the window to match the shape alpha channel to get consistent cross-platform results. The shape is copied inside this function, so you can free it afterwards. If your shape surface changes, you should call SDL_SetWindowShape() again to update the window. This is an expensive operation, so should be done sparingly. The window must have been created with the SDL_WINDOW_TRANSPARENT flag. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window.<br>`shape`：the surface representing the shape of the window, or NULL to remove any current shape.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
