| <a id="api-968ad19399880425"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowSize(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int w, int h)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47247) | Request that the size of a window's client area be set. If the window is in a fullscreen or maximized state, this request has no effect. To change the exclusive fullscreen mode of a window, use SDL_SetWindowFullscreenMode(). On some windowing systems, this request is asynchronous and the new window size may not have have been applied immediately upon the return of this function. If an immediate change is required, call SDL_SyncWindow() to block until the changes have taken effect. When the window size changes, an SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_RESIZED event will be emitted with the new window dimensions. Note that the new dimensions may not match the exact size requested, as some windowing systems can restrict the window size in certain scenarios (e.g. constraining the size of the content area to remain within the usable desktop bounds). Additionally, as this is just a request, it can be denied by the windowing system. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowSize @sa SDL_SetWindowFullscreenMode @sa SDL_SyncWindow<br>`window`：the window to change.<br>`w`：the width of the window, must be > 0.<br>`h`：the height of the window, must be > 0.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-dce46c229bbd658e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowSurfaceVSync(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int vsync)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25574) | Toggle VSync for the window surface. When a window surface is created, vsync defaults to SDL_WINDOW_SURFACE_VSYNC_DISABLED. The `vsync` parameter can be 1 to synchronize present with every vertical refresh, 2 to synchronize present with every second vertical refresh, etc., SDL_WINDOW_SURFACE_VSYNC_ADAPTIVE for late swap tearing (adaptive vsync), or SDL_WINDOW_SURFACE_VSYNC_DISABLED to disable. Not every value is supported by every driver, so you should check the return value to see whether the requested setting is supported. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowSurfaceVSync<br>`window`：the window.<br>`vsync`：the vertical refresh sync interval.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-e4c22e2e78241785"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowTitle(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, sbyte* title)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34088) | Set the title of a window. This string is expected to be in UTF-8 encoding. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowTitle<br>`window`：the window to change.<br>`title`：the desired window title in UTF-8 format.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-82ea68355c683ab3"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetWindowsMessageHook(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.TagMSG*, byte> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31681) | Set a callback for every Windows message, run before TranslateMessage(). The callback may modify the message, and should return true if the message should continue to be processed, or false to prevent further processing. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_WindowsMessageHook @sa SDL_HINT_WINDOWS_ENABLE_MESSAGELOOP<br>`callback`：the SDL_WindowsMessageHook function to call.<br>`userdata`：a pointer to pass to every iteration of `callback`. |
| <a id="api-4a6530b2636cd515"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetX11EventHook(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, Inno.Native.Sdl3.XEvent*, byte> callback, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37833) | Set a callback for every X11 event. The callback may modify the event, and should return true if the event should continue to be processed, or false to prevent further processing. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`callback`：the SDL_X11EventHook function to call.<br>`userdata`：a pointer to pass to every iteration of `callback`. |
| <a id="api-75aac5e65c0ceaeb"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SetenvUnsafe(sbyte* name, sbyte* value, int overwrite)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46384) | Set the value of a variable in the environment. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe, consider using SDL_SetEnvironmentVariable() instead. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetEnvironmentVariable<br>`name`：the name of the variable to set.<br>`value`：the value of the variable to set.<br>`overwrite`：1 to overwrite the variable if it exists, 0 to return success without setting the variable if it already exists.<br>返回：0 on success, -1 on error. |
| <a id="api-8d4f2bfe29867e19"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShouldInit(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLInitState* state)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41313) | Return whether initialization should be done. This function checks the passed in state and if initialization should be done, sets the status to `SDL_INIT_STATUS_INITIALIZING` and returns true. If another thread is already modifying this state, it will wait until that's done before returning. If this function returns true, the calling code must call SDL_SetInitialized() to complete the initialization. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetInitialized @sa SDL_ShouldQuit<br>`state`：the initialization state to check.<br>返回：true if initialization needs to be done, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-9b650f84dfd8f258"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShouldQuit(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLInitState* state)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41286) | Return whether cleanup should be done. This function checks the passed in state and if cleanup should be done, sets the status to `SDL_INIT_STATUS_UNINITIALIZING` and returns true. If this function returns true, the calling code must call SDL_SetInitialized() to complete the cleanup. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetInitialized @sa SDL_ShouldInit<br>`state`：the initialization state to check.<br>返回：true if cleanup needs to be done, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-86fa370e2217435e"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShowCursor()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25426) | Show the cursor. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CursorVisible @sa SDL_HideCursor<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ef53924fd3eb0e08"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShowFileDialogWithProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLFileDialogType type, delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, sbyte**, int, void> callback, void* userdata, uint props)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43057) | Create and launch a file dialog with the specified properties. These are the supported properties: - `SDL_PROP_FILE_DIALOG_FILTERS_POINTER`: a pointer to a list of SDL_DialogFileFilter structs, which will be used as filters for file-based selections. Ignored if the dialog is an "Open Folder" dialog. If non-NULL, the array of filters must remain valid at least until the callback is invoked. - `SDL_PROP_FILE_DIALOG_NFILTERS_NUMBER`: the number of filters in the array of filters, if it exists. - `SDL_PROP_FILE_DIALOG_WINDOW_POINTER`: the window that the dialog should be modal for. - `SDL_PROP_FILE_DIALOG_LOCATION_STRING`: the default folder or file to start the dialog at. - `SDL_PROP_FILE_DIALOG_MANY_BOOLEAN`: true to allow the user to select more than one entry. - `SDL_PROP_FILE_DIALOG_TITLE_STRING`: the title for the dialog. - `SDL_PROP_FILE_DIALOG_ACCEPT_STRING`: the label that the accept button should have. - `SDL_PROP_FILE_DIALOG_CANCEL_STRING`: the label that the cancel button should have. Note that each platform may or may not support any of the properties. @threadsafety This function should be called only from the main thread. The callback may be invoked from the same thread or from a different one, depending on the OS's constraints. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_FileDialogType @sa SDL_DialogFileCallback @sa SDL_DialogFileFilter @sa SDL_ShowOpenFileDialog @sa SDL_ShowSaveFileDialog @sa SDL_ShowOpenFolderDialog<br>`type`：the type of file dialog.<br>`callback`：a function pointer to be invoked when the user selects a file and accepts, or cancels the dialog, or an error occurs.<br>`userdata`：an optional pointer to pass extra data to the callback when it will be invoked.<br>`props`：the properties to use. |
| <a id="api-ec8ee165b2ae5440"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShowMessageBox(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLMessageBoxData* messageboxdata, int* buttonid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17819) | Create a modal message box. If your needs aren't complex, it might be easier to use SDL_ShowSimpleMessageBox. This function should be called on the thread that created the parent window, or on the main thread if the messagebox has no parent. It will block execution of that thread until the user clicks a button or closes the messagebox. This function may be called at any time, even before SDL_Init(). This makes it useful for reporting errors like a failure to create a renderer or OpenGL context. On X11, SDL rolls its own dialog box with X11 primitives instead of a formal toolkit like GTK+ or Qt. Note that if SDL_Init() would fail because there isn't any available video target, this function is likely to fail for the same reasons. If this is a concern, check the return value from this function and fall back to writing to stderr if you can. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ShowSimpleMessageBox<br>`messageboxdata`：the SDL_MessageBoxData structure with title, text and other options.<br>`buttonid`：the pointer to which user id of hit button should be copied.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-26543f4b67a06274"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShowOpenFileDialog(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, sbyte**, int, void> callback, void* userdata, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDialogFileFilter* filters, int nfilters, sbyte* default_location, byte allow_many)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30141) | Displays a dialog that lets the user select a file on their filesystem. This is an asynchronous function; it will return immediately, and the result will be passed to the callback. The callback will be invoked with a null-terminated list of files the user chose. The list will be empty if the user canceled the dialog, and it will be NULL if an error occurred. Note that the callback may be called from a different thread than the one the function was invoked on. Depending on the platform, the user may be allowed to input paths that don't yet exist. On Linux, dialogs may require XDG Portals, which requires DBus, which requires an event-handling loop. Apps that do not use SDL to handle events should add a call to SDL_PumpEvents in their main loop. @threadsafety This function should be called only from the main thread. The callback may be invoked from the same thread or from a different one, depending on the OS's constraints. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_DialogFileCallback @sa SDL_DialogFileFilter @sa SDL_ShowSaveFileDialog @sa SDL_ShowOpenFolderDialog @sa SDL_ShowFileDialogWithProperties<br>`callback`：a function pointer to be invoked when the user selects a file and accepts, or cancels the dialog, or an error occurs.<br>`userdata`：an optional pointer to pass extra data to the callback when it will be invoked.<br>`window`：the window that the dialog should be modal for, may be NULL. Not all platforms support this option.<br>`filters`：a list of filters, may be NULL. Not all platforms support this option, and platforms that do support it may allow the user to ignore the filters. If non-NULL, it must remain valid at least until the callback is invoked.<br>`nfilters`：the number of filters. Ignored if filters is NULL.<br>`default_location`：the default folder or file to start the dialog at, may be NULL. Not all platforms support this option.<br>`allow_many`：if non-zero, the user will be allowed to select multiple entries. Not all platforms support this option. |
| <a id="api-ae4b944da2cd8bf5"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShowOpenFolderDialog(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, sbyte**, int, void> callback, void* userdata, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, sbyte* default_location, byte allow_many)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48255) | Displays a dialog that lets the user select a folder on their filesystem. This is an asynchronous function; it will return immediately, and the result will be passed to the callback. The callback will be invoked with a null-terminated list of files the user chose. The list will be empty if the user canceled the dialog, and it will be NULL if an error occurred. Note that the callback may be called from a different thread than the one the function was invoked on. Depending on the platform, the user may be allowed to input paths that don't yet exist. On Linux, dialogs may require XDG Portals, which requires DBus, which requires an event-handling loop. Apps that do not use SDL to handle events should add a call to SDL_PumpEvents in their main loop. @threadsafety This function should be called only from the main thread. The callback may be invoked from the same thread or from a different one, depending on the OS's constraints. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_DialogFileCallback @sa SDL_ShowOpenFileDialog @sa SDL_ShowSaveFileDialog @sa SDL_ShowFileDialogWithProperties<br>`callback`：a function pointer to be invoked when the user selects a file and accepts, or cancels the dialog, or an error occurs.<br>`userdata`：an optional pointer to pass extra data to the callback when it will be invoked.<br>`window`：the window that the dialog should be modal for, may be NULL. Not all platforms support this option.<br>`default_location`：the default folder or file to start the dialog at, may be NULL. Not all platforms support this option.<br>`allow_many`：if non-zero, the user will be allowed to select multiple entries. Not all platforms support this option. |
| <a id="api-e8e828bbb4ce5276"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShowSaveFileDialog(delegate* unmanaged[Cdecl]<void*, sbyte**, int, void> callback, void* userdata, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDialogFileFilter* filters, int nfilters, sbyte* default_location)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48424) | Displays a dialog that lets the user choose a new or existing file on their filesystem. This is an asynchronous function; it will return immediately, and the result will be passed to the callback. The callback will be invoked with a null-terminated list of files the user chose. The list will be empty if the user canceled the dialog, and it will be NULL if an error occurred. Note that the callback may be called from a different thread than the one the function was invoked on. The chosen file may or may not already exist. On Linux, dialogs may require XDG Portals, which requires DBus, which requires an event-handling loop. Apps that do not use SDL to handle events should add a call to SDL_PumpEvents in their main loop. @threadsafety This function should be called only from the main thread. The callback may be invoked from the same thread or from a different one, depending on the OS's constraints. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_DialogFileCallback @sa SDL_DialogFileFilter @sa SDL_ShowOpenFileDialog @sa SDL_ShowOpenFolderDialog @sa SDL_ShowFileDialogWithProperties<br>`callback`：a function pointer to be invoked when the user selects a file and accepts, or cancels the dialog, or an error occurs.<br>`userdata`：an optional pointer to pass extra data to the callback when it will be invoked.<br>`window`：the window that the dialog should be modal for, may be NULL. Not all platforms support this option.<br>`filters`：a list of filters, may be NULL. Not all platforms support this option, and platforms that do support it may allow the user to ignore the filters. If non-NULL, it must remain valid at least until the callback is invoked.<br>`nfilters`：the number of filters. Ignored if filters is NULL.<br>`default_location`：the default folder or file to start the dialog at, may be NULL. Not all platforms support this option. |
| <a id="api-bf51307ab13da8af"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShowSimpleMessageBox(uint flags, sbyte* title, sbyte* message, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35353) | Display a simple modal message box. If your needs aren't complex, this function is preferred over SDL_ShowMessageBox. `flags` may be any of the following: - `SDL_MESSAGEBOX_ERROR`: error dialog - `SDL_MESSAGEBOX_WARNING`: warning dialog - `SDL_MESSAGEBOX_INFORMATION`: informational dialog This function should be called on the thread that created the parent window, or on the main thread if the messagebox has no parent. It will block execution of that thread until the user clicks a button or closes the messagebox. This function may be called at any time, even before SDL_Init(). This makes it useful for reporting errors like a failure to create a renderer or OpenGL context. On X11, SDL rolls its own dialog box with X11 primitives instead of a formal toolkit like GTK+ or Qt. Note that if SDL_Init() would fail because there isn't any available video target, this function is likely to fail for the same reasons. If this is a concern, check the return value from this function and fall back to writing to stderr if you can. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ShowMessageBox<br>`flags`：an SDL_MessageBoxFlags value.<br>`title`：UTF-8 title text.<br>`message`：UTF-8 message text.<br>`window`：the parent window, or NULL for no parent.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c6d43b4185a67f4a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShowWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26679) | Show a window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_HideWindow @sa SDL_RaiseWindow<br>`window`：the window to show.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-51d87b12d005ed1a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.ShowWindowSystemMenu(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, int x, int y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40394) | Display the system-level window menu. This default window menu is provided by the system and on some platforms provides functionality for setting or changing privileged state on the window, such as moving it between workspaces or displays, or toggling the always-on-top property. On platforms or desktops where this is unsupported, this function does nothing. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`window`：the window for which the menu will be displayed.<br>`x`：the x coordinate of the menu, relative to the origin (top-left) of the client area.<br>`y`：the y coordinate of the menu, relative to the origin (top-left) of the client area.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ba4ef150de9fee81"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SignalAsyncIoQueue(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIOQueue* queue)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38084) | Wake up any threads that are blocking in SDL_WaitAsyncIOResult(). This will unblock any threads that are sleeping in a call to SDL_WaitAsyncIOResult for the specified queue, and cause them to return from that function. This can be useful when destroying a queue to make sure nothing is touching it indefinitely. In this case, once this call completes, the caller should take measures to make sure any previously-blocked threads have returned from their wait and will not touch the queue again (perhaps by setting a flag to tell the threads to terminate and then using SDL_WaitThread() to make sure they've done so). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_WaitAsyncIOResult<br>`queue`：the async I/O task queue to signal. |
| <a id="api-a700373532d57039"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SignalCondition(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCondition* cond)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18755) | Restart one of the threads that are waiting on the condition variable. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_BroadcastCondition @sa SDL_WaitCondition @sa SDL_WaitConditionTimeout<br>`cond`：the condition variable to signal. |
| <a id="api-5c625efecf64a3db"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SignalSemaphore(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSemaphore* sem)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26837) | Atomically increment a semaphore's value and wake waiting threads. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_TryWaitSemaphore @sa SDL_WaitSemaphore @sa SDL_WaitSemaphoreTimeout<br>`sem`：the semaphore to increment. |
| <a id="api-aa1b0ba0a4f53660"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Sin(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50677) | Compute the sine of `x`. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-1 < = y < = 1` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_sinf for single-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_sinf @sa SDL_asin @sa SDL_cos<br>`x`：floating point value, in radians.<br>返回：sine of `x`. |
| <a id="api-38db53cf05d47837"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Sinf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37089) | Compute the sine of `x`. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-1 < = y < = 1` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_sin for double-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_sin @sa SDL_asinf @sa SDL_cosf<br>`x`：floating point value, in radians.<br>返回：sine of `x`. |
| <a id="api-a8c7d769473670f4"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Sqrt(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44805) | Compute the square root of `x`. Domain: `0 < = x < = INF` Range: `0 < = y < = INF` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_sqrtf for single-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_sqrtf<br>`x`：floating point value. Must be greater than or equal to 0.<br>返回：square root of `x`. |
| <a id="api-7452624cb87e6198"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Sqrtf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36283) | Compute the square root of `x`. Domain: `0 < = x < = INF` Range: `0 < = y < = INF` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_sqrt for double-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_sqrt<br>`x`：floating point value. Must be greater than or equal to 0.<br>返回：square root of `x`. |
| <a id="api-1771b33bf7d13942"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Srand(ulong seed)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45046) | Seeds the pseudo-random number generator. Reusing the seed number will cause SDL_rand() to repeat the same stream of 'random' numbers. @threadsafety This should be called on the same thread that calls SDL_rand() @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_rand @sa SDL_rand_bits @sa SDL_randf<br>`seed`：the value to use as a random number seed, or 0 to use SDL_GetPerformanceCounter(). |
| <a id="api-6d0202c205ce6075"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StartTextInput(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41733) | Start accepting Unicode text input events in a window. This function will enable text input (SDL_EVENT_TEXT_INPUT and SDL_EVENT_TEXT_EDITING events) in the specified window. Please use this function paired with SDL_StopTextInput(). Text input events are not received by default. On some platforms using this function shows the screen keyboard and/or activates an IME, which can prevent some key press events from being passed through. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetTextInputArea @sa SDL_StartTextInputWithProperties @sa SDL_StopTextInput @sa SDL_TextInputActive<br>`window`：the window to enable text input.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a08f95ab0d9e77a9"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StartTextInputWithProperties(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, uint props)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33890) | Start accepting Unicode text input events in a window, with properties describing the input. This function will enable text input (SDL_EVENT_TEXT_INPUT and SDL_EVENT_TEXT_EDITING events) in the specified window. Please use this function paired with SDL_StopTextInput(). Text input events are not received by default. On some platforms using this function shows the screen keyboard and/or activates an IME, which can prevent some key press events from being passed through. These are the supported properties: - `SDL_PROP_TEXTINPUT_TYPE_NUMBER` - an SDL_TextInputType value that describes text being input, defaults to SDL_TEXTINPUT_TYPE_TEXT. - `SDL_PROP_TEXTINPUT_CAPITALIZATION_NUMBER` - an SDL_Capitalization value that describes how text should be capitalized, defaults to SDL_CAPITALIZE_SENTENCES for normal text entry, SDL_CAPITALIZE_WORDS for SDL_TEXTINPUT_TYPE_TEXT_NAME, and SDL_CAPITALIZE_NONE for e-mail addresses, usernames, and passwords. - `SDL_PROP_TEXTINPUT_AUTOCORRECT_BOOLEAN` - true to enable auto completion and auto correction, defaults to true. - `SDL_PROP_TEXTINPUT_MULTILINE_BOOLEAN` - true if multiple lines of text are allowed. This defaults to true if SDL_HINT_RETURN_KEY_HIDES_IME is "0" or is not set, and defaults to false if SDL_HINT_RETURN_KEY_HIDES_IME is "1". On Android you can directly specify the input type: - `SDL_PROP_TEXTINPUT_ANDROID_INPUTTYPE_NUMBER` - the text input type to use, overriding other properties. This is documented at https://developer.android.com/reference/android/text/InputType @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetTextInputArea @sa SDL_StartTextInput @sa SDL_StopTextInput @sa SDL_TextInputActive<br>`window`：the window to enable text input.<br>`props`：the properties to use.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-77295a4f80c1521a"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StepBackUtf8(sbyte* start, sbyte** pstr)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39806) | Decode a UTF-8 string in reverse, one Unicode codepoint at a time. This will go to the start of the previous Unicode codepoint in the string, move `*pstr` to that location and return that codepoint. If `*pstr` is already at the start of the string), it will not advance `*pstr` at all. Generally this function is called in a loop until it returns zero, adjusting its parameter each iteration. If an invalid UTF-8 sequence is encountered, this function returns SDL_INVALID_UNICODE_CODEPOINT. Several things can generate invalid UTF-8 sequences, including overlong encodings, the use of UTF-16 surrogate values, and truncated data. Please refer to [RFC3629](https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc3629.txt) for details. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`start`：a pointer to the beginning of the UTF-8 string.<br>`pstr`：a pointer to a UTF-8 string pointer to be read and adjusted.<br>返回：the previous Unicode codepoint in the string. |
| <a id="api-19a735db0c9ff5c4"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StepUtf8(sbyte** pstr, ulong* pslen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45777) | Decode a UTF-8 string, one Unicode codepoint at a time. This will return the first Unicode codepoint in the UTF-8 encoded string in `*pstr`, and then advance `*pstr` past any consumed bytes before returning. It will not access more than `*pslen` bytes from the string. `*pslen` will be adjusted, as well, subtracting the number of bytes consumed. `pslen` is allowed to be NULL, in which case the string _must_ be NULL-terminated, as the function will blindly read until it sees the NULL char. if `*pslen` is zero, it assumes the end of string is reached and returns a zero codepoint regardless of the contents of the string buffer. If the resulting codepoint is zero (a NULL terminator), or `*pslen` is zero, it will not advance `*pstr` or `*pslen` at all. Generally this function is called in a loop until it returns zero, adjusting its parameters each iteration. If an invalid UTF-8 sequence is encountered, this function returns SDL_INVALID_UNICODE_CODEPOINT and advances the string/length by one byte (which is to say, a multibyte sequence might produce several SDL_INVALID_UNICODE_CODEPOINT returns before it syncs to the next valid UTF-8 sequence). Several things can generate invalid UTF-8 sequences, including overlong encodings, the use of UTF-16 surrogate values, and truncated data. Please refer to [RFC3629](https://www.ietf.org/rfc/rfc3629.txt) for details. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`pstr`：a pointer to a UTF-8 string pointer to be read and adjusted.<br>`pslen`：a pointer to the number of bytes in the string, to be read and adjusted. NULL is allowed.<br>返回：the first Unicode codepoint in the string. |
| <a id="api-c6b84b77fd781d64"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StopHapticEffect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic, int effect)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46356) | Stop the haptic effect on its associated haptic device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RunHapticEffect @sa SDL_StopHapticEffects<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to stop the effect on.<br>`effect`：the ID of the haptic effect to stop.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9468484c469ad12a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StopHapticEffects(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34911) | Stop all the currently playing effects on a haptic device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_RunHapticEffect @sa SDL_StopHapticEffect<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device to stop.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-19b7c23e7d5af000"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StopHapticRumble(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39447) | Stop the simple rumble on a haptic device. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PlayHapticRumble<br>`haptic`：the haptic device to stop the rumble effect on.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-43fec27f453acadd"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StopTextInput(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17646) | Stop receiving any text input events in a window. If SDL_StartTextInput() showed the screen keyboard, this function will hide it. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_StartTextInput<br>`window`：the window to disable text input.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-69ec0b4b2f861209"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StorageReady(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLStorage* storage)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39554) | Checks if the storage container is ready to use. This function should be called in regular intervals until it returns true - however, it is not recommended to spinwait on this call, as the backend may depend on a synchronous message loop. You might instead poll this in your game's main loop while processing events and drawing a loading screen. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`storage`：a storage container to query.<br>返回：true if the container is ready, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-06dea99b607fae69"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strcasecmp(sbyte* str1, sbyte* str2)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43503) | Compare two null-terminated UTF-8 strings, case-insensitively. This will work with Unicode strings, using a technique called "case-folding" to handle the vast majority of case-sensitive human languages regardless of system locale. It can deal with expanding values: a German Eszett character can compare against two ASCII 's' chars and be considered a match, for example. A notable exception: it does not handle the Turkish 'i' character; human language is complicated! Since this handles Unicode, it expects the string to be well-formed UTF-8 and not a null-terminated string of arbitrary bytes. Bytes that are not valid UTF-8 are treated as Unicode character U+FFFD (REPLACEMENT CHARACTER), which is to say two strings of random bits may turn out to match if they convert to the same amount of replacement characters. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str1`：the first string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`str2`：the second string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>返回：less than zero if str1 is "less than" str2, greater than zero if str1 is "greater than" str2, and zero if the strings match exactly. |
| <a id="api-814bcc334b72b384"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strcasestr(sbyte* haystack, sbyte* needle)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41593) | Search a UTF-8 string for the first instance of a specific substring, case-insensitively. This will work with Unicode strings, using a technique called "case-folding" to handle the vast majority of case-sensitive human languages regardless of system locale. It can deal with expanding values: a German Eszett character can compare against two ASCII 's' chars and be considered a match, for example. A notable exception: it does not handle the Turkish 'i' character; human language is complicated! Since this handles Unicode, it expects the strings to be well-formed UTF-8 and not a null-terminated string of arbitrary bytes. Bytes that are not valid UTF-8 are treated as Unicode character U+FFFD (REPLACEMENT CHARACTER), which is to say two strings of random bits may turn out to match if they convert to the same amount of replacement characters. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`haystack`：the string to search. Must not be NULL.<br>`needle`：the string to search for. Must not be NULL.<br>返回：a pointer to the first instance of `needle` in the string, or NULL if not found. |
| <a id="api-592492bf2f2e6f02"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strchr(sbyte* str, int c)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43914) | Search a string for the first instance of a specific byte. The search ends once it finds the requested byte value, or a null terminator byte to end the string. Note that this looks for _bytes_, not _characters_, so you cannot match against a Unicode codepoint > 255, regardless of character encoding. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str`：the string to search. Must not be NULL.<br>`c`：the byte value to search for.<br>返回：a pointer to the first instance of `c` in the string, or NULL if not found. |
| <a id="api-596c53604c0b2f94"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strcmp(sbyte* str1, sbyte* str2)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37988) | Compare two null-terminated UTF-8 strings. Due to the nature of UTF-8 encoding, this will work with Unicode strings, since effectively this function just compares bytes until it hits a null-terminating character. Also due to the nature of UTF-8, this can be used with SDL_qsort() to put strings in (roughly) alphabetical order. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str1`：the first string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`str2`：the second string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>返回：less than zero if str1 is "less than" str2, greater than zero if str1 is "greater than" str2, and zero if the strings match exactly. |
| <a id="api-4f2407424282965f"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strdup(sbyte* str)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39859) | Allocate a copy of a string. This allocates enough space for a null-terminated copy of `str`, using SDL_malloc, and then makes a copy of the string into this space. The returned string is owned by the caller, and should be passed to SDL_free when no longer needed. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str`：the string to copy.<br>返回：a pointer to the newly-allocated string. |
| <a id="api-bf968ea08aca9202"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StretchSurface(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* src, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* srcrect, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* dst, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* dstrect, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLScaleMode scaleMode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49694) | Perform a stretched pixel copy from one surface to another. @threadsafety Only one thread should be using the `src` and `dst` surfaces at any given time. @since This function is available since SDL 3.4.0. @sa SDL_BlitSurfaceScaled<br>`src`：the SDL_Surface structure to be copied from.<br>`srcrect`：the SDL_Rect structure representing the rectangle to be copied, or NULL to copy the entire surface.<br>`dst`：the SDL_Surface structure that is the blit target.<br>`dstrect`：the SDL_Rect structure representing the target rectangle in the destination surface, or NULL to fill the entire destination surface.<br>`scaleMode`：the SDL_ScaleMode to be used.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-366c0d4bb039b210"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StringToGuid(sbyte* pchGUID, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGUID* bgcs_result)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28731) | Convert a GUID string into a SDL_GUID structure. Performs no error checking. If this function is given a string containing an invalid GUID, the function will silently succeed, but the GUID generated will not be useful. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GUIDToString<br>`pchGUID`：string containing an ASCII representation of a GUID.<br>`bgcs_result`：Native parameter.<br>约束：Native result: a SDL_GUID structure. |
| <a id="api-fdacde4c7e958c0b"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strlcat(sbyte* dst, sbyte* src, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39357) | Concatenate strings. This function appends up to `maxlen` - SDL_strlen(dst) - 1 characters from `src` to the end of the string in `dst`, then appends a null terminator. `src` and `dst` must not overlap. If `maxlen` - SDL_strlen(dst) - 1 is less than or equal to 0, then `dst` is unmodified. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_strlcpy<br>`dst`：The destination buffer already containing the first null-terminated string. Must not be NULL and must not overlap with `src`.<br>`src`：The second null-terminated string. Must not be NULL, and must not overlap with `dst`.<br>`maxlen`：The length (in characters) of the destination buffer.<br>返回：the length (in characters, excluding the null terminator) of the string in `dst` plus the length of `src`. |
| <a id="api-46a0ac76816c895c"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strlcpy(sbyte* dst, sbyte* src, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42721) | Copy a string. This function copies up to `maxlen` - 1 characters from `src` to `dst`, then appends a null terminator. If `maxlen` is 0, no characters are copied and no null terminator is written. If you want to copy an UTF-8 string but need to ensure that multi-byte sequences are not truncated, consider using SDL_utf8strlcpy(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_strlcat @sa SDL_utf8strlcpy<br>`dst`：The destination buffer. Must not be NULL, and must not overlap with `src`.<br>`src`：The null-terminated string to copy. Must not be NULL, and must not overlap with `dst`.<br>`maxlen`：The length (in characters) of the destination buffer.<br>返回：the length (in characters, excluding the null terminator) of `src`. |
| <a id="api-a68aaeb18a1a82bb"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strlen(sbyte* str)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27481) | This works exactly like strlen() but doesn't require access to a C runtime. Counts the bytes in `str`, excluding the null terminator. If you need the length of a UTF-8 string, consider using SDL_utf8strlen(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_strnlen @sa SDL_utf8strlen @sa SDL_utf8strnlen<br>`str`：The null-terminated string to read. Must not be NULL.<br>返回：the length (in bytes, excluding the null terminator) of `str`. |
| <a id="api-d27585d9b9f1046d"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strlwr(sbyte* str)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47805) | Convert a string to lowercase. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only convert ASCII values 'A' through 'Z' to lowercase. This function operates on a null-terminated string of bytes--even if it is malformed UTF-8!--and converts ASCII characters 'A' through 'Z' to their lowercase equivalents in-place, returning the original `str` pointer. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_strupr<br>`str`：the string to convert in-place. Can not be NULL.<br>返回：the `str` pointer passed into this function. |
| <a id="api-fd8a23fb2533ea71"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strncasecmp(sbyte* str1, sbyte* str2, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44998) | Compare two UTF-8 strings, case-insensitively, up to a number of bytes. This will work with Unicode strings, using a technique called "case-folding" to handle the vast majority of case-sensitive human languages regardless of system locale. It can deal with expanding values: a German Eszett character can compare against two ASCII 's' chars and be considered a match, for example. A notable exception: it does not handle the Turkish 'i' character; human language is complicated! Since this handles Unicode, it expects the string to be well-formed UTF-8 and not a null-terminated string of arbitrary bytes. Bytes that are not valid UTF-8 are treated as Unicode character U+FFFD (REPLACEMENT CHARACTER), which is to say two strings of random bits may turn out to match if they convert to the same amount of replacement characters. Note that while this function is intended to be used with UTF-8, `maxlen` specifies a _byte_ limit! If the limit lands in the middle of a multi-byte UTF-8 sequence, it may convert a portion of the final character to one or more Unicode character U+FFFD (REPLACEMENT CHARACTER) so as not to overflow a buffer. `maxlen` specifies a maximum number of bytes to compare; if the strings match to this number of bytes (or both have matched to a null-terminator character before this number of bytes), they will be considered equal. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str1`：the first string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`str2`：the second string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`maxlen`：the maximum number of bytes to compare.<br>返回：less than zero if str1 is "less than" str2, greater than zero if str1 is "greater than" str2, and zero if the strings match exactly. |
| <a id="api-7420446a55858cb6"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strncmp(sbyte* str1, sbyte* str2, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17916) | Compare two UTF-8 strings up to a number of bytes. Due to the nature of UTF-8 encoding, this will work with Unicode strings, since effectively this function just compares bytes until it hits a null-terminating character. Also due to the nature of UTF-8, this can be used with SDL_qsort() to put strings in (roughly) alphabetical order. Note that while this function is intended to be used with UTF-8, it is doing a bytewise comparison, and `maxlen` specifies a _byte_ limit! If the limit lands in the middle of a multi-byte UTF-8 sequence, it will only compare a portion of the final character. `maxlen` specifies a maximum number of bytes to compare; if the strings match to this number of bytes (or both have matched to a null-terminator character before this number of bytes), they will be considered equal. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str1`：the first string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`str2`：the second string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`maxlen`：the maximum number of _bytes_ to compare.<br>返回：less than zero if str1 is "less than" str2, greater than zero if str1 is "greater than" str2, and zero if the strings match exactly. |
| <a id="api-e6794bf2afc9de2d"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strndup(sbyte* str, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52190) | Allocate a copy of a string, up to n characters. This allocates enough space for a null-terminated copy of `str`, up to `maxlen` bytes, using SDL_malloc, and then makes a copy of the string into this space. If the string is longer than `maxlen` bytes, the returned string will be `maxlen` bytes long, plus a null-terminator character that isn't included in the count. The returned string is owned by the caller, and should be passed to SDL_free when no longer needed. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str`：the string to copy.<br>`maxlen`：the maximum length of the copied string, not counting the null-terminator character.<br>返回：a pointer to the newly-allocated string. |
| <a id="api-16d072604c846523"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strnlen(sbyte* str, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30793) | This works exactly like strnlen() but doesn't require access to a C runtime. Counts up to a maximum of `maxlen` bytes in `str`, excluding the null terminator. If you need the length of a UTF-8 string, consider using SDL_utf8strnlen(). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_strlen @sa SDL_utf8strlen @sa SDL_utf8strnlen<br>`str`：The null-terminated string to read. Must not be NULL.<br>`maxlen`：The maximum amount of bytes to count.<br>返回：the length (in bytes, excluding the null terminator) of `src` but never more than `maxlen`. |
| <a id="api-ce7015caaaea5926"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strnstr(sbyte* haystack, sbyte* needle, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31305) | Search a string, up to n bytes, for the first instance of a specific substring. The search ends once it finds the requested substring, or a null terminator byte to end the string, or `maxlen` bytes have been examined. It is possible to use this function on a string without a null terminator. Note that this looks for strings of _bytes_, not _characters_, so it's legal to search for malformed and incomplete UTF-8 sequences. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`haystack`：the string to search. Must not be NULL.<br>`needle`：the string to search for. Must not be NULL.<br>`maxlen`：the maximum number of bytes to search in `haystack`.<br>返回：a pointer to the first instance of `needle` in the string, or NULL if not found. |
| <a id="api-d1085f0eba7b7ac3"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strpbrk(sbyte* str, sbyte* breakset)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17192) | Searches a string for the first occurrence of any character contained in a breakset, and returns a pointer from the string to that character. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str`：The null-terminated string to be searched. Must not be NULL, and must not overlap with `breakset`.<br>`breakset`：A null-terminated string containing the list of characters to look for. Must not be NULL, and must not overlap with `str`.<br>返回：A pointer to the location, in str, of the first occurrence of a character present in the breakset, or NULL if none is found. |
| <a id="api-11b28db497319e26"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strrchr(sbyte* str, int c)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32424) | Search a string for the last instance of a specific byte. The search must go until it finds a null terminator byte to end the string. Note that this looks for _bytes_, not _characters_, so you cannot match against a Unicode codepoint > 255, regardless of character encoding. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str`：the string to search. Must not be NULL.<br>`c`：the byte value to search for.<br>返回：a pointer to the last instance of `c` in the string, or NULL if not found. |
| <a id="api-cd973a6da578b7ff"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strrev(sbyte* str)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41235) | Reverse a string's contents. This reverses a null-terminated string in-place. Only the content of the string is reversed; the null-terminator character remains at the end of the reversed string. **WARNING**: This function reverses the _bytes_ of the string, not the codepoints. If `str` is a UTF-8 string with Unicode codepoints > 127, this will ruin the string data. You should only use this function on strings that are completely comprised of low ASCII characters. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str`：the string to reverse.<br>返回：`str`. |
| <a id="api-b5dd778b27d28a40"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strstr(sbyte* haystack, sbyte* needle)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L50023) | Search a string for the first instance of a specific substring. The search ends once it finds the requested substring, or a null terminator byte to end the string. Note that this looks for strings of _bytes_, not _characters_, so it's legal to search for malformed and incomplete UTF-8 sequences. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`haystack`：the string to search. Must not be NULL.<br>`needle`：the string to search for. Must not be NULL.<br>返回：a pointer to the first instance of `needle` in the string, or NULL if not found. |
| <a id="api-735a369196ac74e0"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strtod(sbyte* str, sbyte** endp)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40788) | Parse a `double` from a string. This function makes fewer guarantees than the C runtime `strtod`: - Only decimal notation is guaranteed to be supported. The handling of scientific and hexadecimal notation is unspecified. - Whether or not INF and NAN can be parsed is unspecified. - The precision of the result is unspecified. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_atoi @sa SDL_atof @sa SDL_strtol @sa SDL_strtoll @sa SDL_strtoul @sa SDL_strtoull<br>`str`：the null-terminated string to read. Must not be NULL.<br>`endp`：if not NULL, the address of the first invalid character (i.e. the next character after the parsed number) will be written to this pointer.<br>返回：the parsed `double`, or 0 if no number could be parsed. |
| <a id="api-cc3c61eccbd794b1"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.StrtokR(sbyte* str, sbyte* delim, sbyte** saveptr)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19370) | This works exactly like strtok_r() but doesn't require access to a C runtime. Break a string up into a series of tokens. To start tokenizing a new string, `str` should be the non-NULL address of the string to start tokenizing. Future calls to get the next token from the same string should specify a NULL. Note that this function will overwrite pieces of `str` with null chars to split it into tokens. This function cannot be used with const/read-only strings! `saveptr` just needs to point to a `char *` that can be overwritten; SDL will use this to save tokenizing state between calls. It is initialized if `str` is non-NULL, and used to resume tokenizing when `str` is NULL. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str`：the string to tokenize, or NULL to continue tokenizing.<br>`delim`：the delimiter string that separates tokens.<br>`saveptr`：pointer to a char *, used for ongoing state.<br>返回：A pointer to the next token, or NULL if no tokens remain. |
| <a id="api-f2474f32f2ee238a"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strtol(sbyte* str, sbyte** endp, int @base)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25298) | Parse a `long` from a string. If `str` starts with whitespace, then those whitespace characters are skipped before attempting to parse the number. If the parsed number does not fit inside a `long`, the result is clamped to the minimum and maximum representable `long` values. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_atoi @sa SDL_atof @sa SDL_strtoul @sa SDL_strtoll @sa SDL_strtoull @sa SDL_strtod @sa SDL_ltoa @sa SDL_wcstol<br>`str`：The null-terminated string to read. Must not be NULL.<br>`endp`：If not NULL, the address of the first invalid character (i.e. the next character after the parsed number) will be written to this pointer.<br>`base`：The base of the integer to read. Supported values are 0 and 2 to 36 inclusive. If 0, the base will be inferred from the number's prefix (0x for hexadecimal, 0 for octal, decimal otherwise).<br>返回：the parsed `long`, or 0 if no number could be parsed. |
| <a id="api-307c33b574e3a3f2"></a>[`public long Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Strtoll(sbyte* str, sbyte** endp, int @base)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25125) | Parse a `long long` from a string. Supported values are 0 and 2 to 36 inclusive. If 0, the base will be inferred from the number's prefix (0x for hexadecimal, 0 for octal, decimal otherwise).<br>返回：the parsed `long long`, or 0 if no number could be parsed. |

| <a id="api-8fcf8aa7d6e013cb"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SubmitGpuCommandBuffer(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCommandBuffer* command_buffer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25542) | Submits a command buffer so its commands can be processed on the GPU. It is invalid to use the command buffer after this is called. This must be called from the thread the command buffer was acquired on. All commands in the submission are guaranteed to begin executing before any command in a subsequent submission begins executing. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AcquireGPUCommandBuffer @sa SDL_WaitAndAcquireGPUSwapchainTexture @sa SDL_AcquireGPUSwapchainTexture @sa SDL_SubmitGPUCommandBufferAndAcquireFence<br>`command_buffer`：a command buffer.<br>返回：true on success, false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0c3625d747414710"></a>[`public Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUFence* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SubmitGpuCommandBufferAndAcquireFence(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCommandBuffer* command_buffer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33755) | Submits a command buffer so its commands can be processed on the GPU, and acquires a fence associated with the command buffer. This must be called from the thread the command buffer was acquired on. All commands in the submission are guaranteed to begin executing before any command in a subsequent submission begins executing. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AcquireGPUCommandBuffer @sa SDL_WaitAndAcquireGPUSwapchainTexture @sa SDL_AcquireGPUSwapchainTexture @sa SDL_SubmitGPUCommandBuffer @sa SDL_ReleaseGPUFence<br>`command_buffer`：a command buffer.<br>返回：a fence associated with the command buffer, or NULL on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9926a2b5c66422fc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SurfaceHasAlternateImages(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40083) | Return whether a surface has alternate versions available. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AddSurfaceAlternateImage @sa SDL_RemoveSurfaceAlternateImages @sa SDL_GetSurfaceImages<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>返回：true if alternate versions are available or false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-4aa57ff8b63386c7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SurfaceHasColorKey(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24480) | Returns whether the surface has a color key. It is safe to pass a NULL `surface` here; it will return false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetSurfaceColorKey @sa SDL_GetSurfaceColorKey<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>返回：true if the surface has a color key, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-db9915add5375bbc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SurfaceHasRle(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28806) | Returns whether the surface is RLE enabled. It is safe to pass a NULL `surface` here; it will return false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetSurfaceRLE<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to query.<br>返回：true if the surface is RLE enabled, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-d8ab6410e2e834b5"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.SyncWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36523) | Block until any pending window state is finalized. On asynchronous windowing systems, this acts as a synchronization barrier for pending window state. It will attempt to wait until any pending window state has been applied and is guaranteed to return within finite time. Note that for how long it can potentially block depends on the underlying window system, as window state changes may involve somewhat lengthy animations that must complete before the window is in its final requested state. On windowing systems where changes are immediate, this does nothing. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SetWindowSize @sa SDL_SetWindowPosition @sa SDL_SetWindowFullscreen @sa SDL_MinimizeWindow @sa SDL_MaximizeWindow @sa SDL_RestoreWindow @sa SDL_HINT_VIDEO_SYNC_WINDOW_OPERATIONS<br>`window`：the window for which to wait for the pending state to be applied.<br>返回：true on success or false if the operation timed out before the window was in the requested state. |
| <a id="api-eff1ace08fca19f3"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Tan(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L16830) | Compute the tangent of `x`. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_tanf for single-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_tanf @sa SDL_sin @sa SDL_cos @sa SDL_atan @sa SDL_atan2<br>`x`：floating point value, in radians.<br>返回：tangent of `x`. |
| <a id="api-be465e4ffb7f3ad1"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Tanf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30725) | Compute the tangent of `x`. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_tan for double-precision floats. This function may use a different approximation across different versions, platforms and configurations. i.e, it can return a different value given the same input on different machines or operating systems, or if SDL is updated. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_tan @sa SDL_sinf @sa SDL_cosf @sa SDL_atanf @sa SDL_atan2f<br>`x`：floating point value, in radians.<br>返回：tangent of `x`. |
| <a id="api-bf74c19b39d03b47"></a>[`public long Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TellIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* context)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40038) | Determine the current read/write offset in an SDL_IOStream data stream. SDL_TellIO is actually a wrapper function that calls the SDL_IOStream's `seek` method, with an offset of 0 bytes from `SDL_IO_SEEK_CUR`, to simplify application development. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SeekIO<br>`context`：an SDL_IOStream data stream object from which to get the current offset.<br>返回：the current offset in the stream, or -1 if the information can not be determined. |
| <a id="api-1ea7080e939332a4"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TextInputActive(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26882) | Check whether or not Unicode text input events are enabled for a window. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_StartTextInput<br>`window`：the window to check.<br>返回：true if text input events are enabled else false. |
| <a id="api-c91c6221ac9a4049"></a>[`public long Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TimeFromWindows(uint dwLowDateTime, uint dwHighDateTime)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22125) | Converts a Windows FILETIME (100-nanosecond intervals since January 1, 1601) to an SDL time. This function takes the two 32-bit values of the FILETIME structure as parameters. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dwLowDateTime`：the low portion of the Windows FILETIME value.<br>`dwHighDateTime`：the high portion of the Windows FILETIME value.<br>返回：the converted SDL time. |
| <a id="api-599c2d6115337ee8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TimeToDateTime(long ticks, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLDateTime* dt, byte localTime)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35487) | Converts an SDL_Time in nanoseconds since the epoch to a calendar time in the SDL_DateTime format. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`ticks`：the SDL_Time to be converted.<br>`dt`：the resulting SDL_DateTime.<br>`localTime`：the resulting SDL_DateTime will be expressed in local time if true, otherwise it will be in Universal Coordinated Time (UTC).<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ae6b16e858aa4f7b"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TimeToWindows(long ticks, uint* dwLowDateTime, uint* dwHighDateTime)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24506) | Converts an SDL time into a Windows FILETIME (100-nanosecond intervals since January 1, 1601). This function fills in the two 32-bit values of the FILETIME structure. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`ticks`：the time to convert.<br>`dwLowDateTime`：a pointer filled in with the low portion of the Windows FILETIME value.<br>`dwHighDateTime`：a pointer filled in with the high portion of the Windows FILETIME value. |
| <a id="api-9391029d76a4cdc0"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Tolower(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48517) | Convert low-ASCII English letters to lowercase. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only convert ASCII values 'A' through 'Z' to lowercase. This function returns the lowercase equivalent of `x`. If a character cannot be converted, or is already lowercase, this function returns `x`. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：lowercase version of x, or x if no conversion available. |
| <a id="api-2375fce7775e6c58"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Toupper(int x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43775) | Convert low-ASCII English letters to uppercase. **WARNING**: Regardless of system locale, this will only convert ASCII values 'a' through 'z' to uppercase. This function returns the uppercase equivalent of `x`. If a character cannot be converted, or is already uppercase, this function returns `x`. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`x`：character value to check.<br>返回：capitalized version of x, or x if no conversion available. |
| <a id="api-3e564d4a1057f04d"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Trunc(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19804) | Truncate `x` to an integer. Rounds `x` to the next closest integer to 0. This is equivalent to removing the fractional part of `x`, leaving only the integer part. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_truncf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_truncf @sa SDL_fmod @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lround<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：`x` truncated to an integer. |
| <a id="api-be8ec2d8efe47f79"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Truncf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L27623) | Truncate `x` to an integer. Rounds `x` to the next closest integer to 0. This is equivalent to removing the fractional part of `x`, leaving only the integer part. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_trunc for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_trunc @sa SDL_fmodf @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lroundf<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：`x` truncated to an integer. |
| <a id="api-5214741654990edc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TryLockJoysticks()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34313) | Locking for atomic access to the joystick API. The SDL joystick functions are thread-safe, however you can lock the joysticks while processing to guarantee that the joystick list won't change and joystick and gamepad events will not be delivered. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.6.0.<br>返回：true if the joysticks were successfully locked, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-0420bb37d82be233"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TryLockMutex(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLMutex* mutex)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23027) | Try to lock a mutex without blocking. This works just like SDL_LockMutex(), but if the mutex is not available, this function returns false immediately. This technique is useful if you need exclusive access to a resource but don't want to wait for it, and will return to it to try again later. This function returns true if passed a NULL mutex. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockMutex @sa SDL_UnlockMutex<br>`mutex`：the mutex to try to lock.<br>返回：true on success, false if the mutex would block. |
| <a id="api-8cd04e55c77c0937"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TryLockRwLockForReading(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRWLock* rwlock)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33784) | Try to lock a read/write lock _for reading_ without blocking. This works just like SDL_LockRWLockForReading(), but if the rwlock is not available, then this function returns false immediately. This technique is useful if you need access to a resource but don't want to wait for it, and will return to it to try again later. Trying to lock for read-only access can succeed if other threads are holding read-only locks, as this won't prevent access. This function returns true if passed a NULL rwlock. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockRWLockForReading @sa SDL_TryLockRWLockForWriting @sa SDL_UnlockRWLock<br>`rwlock`：the rwlock to try to lock.<br>返回：true on success, false if the lock would block. |
| <a id="api-4757d3a3900c4ac5"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TryLockRwLockForWriting(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRWLock* rwlock)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L37330) | Try to lock a read/write lock _for writing_ without blocking. This works just like SDL_LockRWLockForWriting(), but if the rwlock is not available, then this function returns false immediately. This technique is useful if you need exclusive access to a resource but don't want to wait for it, and will return to it to try again later. It is illegal for the owning thread to lock an already-locked rwlock for writing (read-only may be locked recursively, writing can not). Doing so results in undefined behavior. It is illegal to request a write lock from a thread that already holds a read-only lock. Doing so results in undefined behavior. Unlock the read-only lock before requesting a write lock. This function returns true if passed a NULL rwlock. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockRWLockForWriting @sa SDL_TryLockRWLockForReading @sa SDL_UnlockRWLock<br>`rwlock`：the rwlock to try to lock.<br>返回：true on success, false if the lock would block. |
| <a id="api-cd2d847b65e3d894"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TryLockSpinlock(int* @lock)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L25368) | Try to lock a spin lock by setting it to a non-zero value. ***Please note that spinlocks are dangerous if you don't know what you're doing. Please be careful using any sort of spinlock!*** @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockSpinlock @sa SDL_UnlockSpinlock<br>`lock`：a pointer to a lock variable.<br>返回：true if the lock succeeded, false if the lock is already held. |
| <a id="api-15d6057d3ba1fb0d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.TryWaitSemaphore(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSemaphore* sem)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51991) | See if a semaphore has a positive value and decrement it if it does. This function checks to see if the semaphore pointed to by `sem` has a positive value and atomically decrements the semaphore value if it does. If the semaphore doesn't have a positive value, the function immediately returns false. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SignalSemaphore @sa SDL_WaitSemaphore @sa SDL_WaitSemaphoreTimeout<br>`sem`：the semaphore to wait on.<br>返回：true if the wait succeeds, false if the wait would block. |
| <a id="api-6ed99e094f91533e"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Ucs4ToUtf8(uint codepoint, sbyte* dst)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L26993) | Convert a single Unicode codepoint to UTF-8. The buffer pointed to by `dst` must be at least 4 bytes long, as this function may generate between 1 and 4 bytes of output. This function returns the first byte _after_ the newly-written UTF-8 sequence, which is useful for encoding multiple codepoints in a loop, or knowing where to write a NULL-terminator character to end the string (in either case, plan to have a buffer of _more_ than 4 bytes!). If `codepoint` is an invalid value (outside the Unicode range, or a UTF-16 surrogate value, etc), this will use U+FFFD (REPLACEMENT CHARACTER) for the codepoint instead, and not set an error. If `dst` is NULL, this returns NULL immediately without writing to the pointer and without setting an error. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`codepoint`：a Unicode codepoint to convert to UTF-8.<br>`dst`：the location to write the encoded UTF-8. Must point to at least 4 bytes!<br>返回：the first byte past the newly-written UTF-8 sequence. |
