| <a id="api-ae3201376585d5d6"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Uitoa(uint value, sbyte* str, int radix)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43836) | Convert an unsigned integer into a string. This requires a radix to specified for string format. Specifying 10 produces a decimal number, 16 hexadecimal, etc. Must be in the range of 2 to 36. Note that this function will overflow a buffer if `str` is not large enough to hold the output! It may be safer to use SDL_snprintf to clamp output, or SDL_asprintf to allocate a buffer. Otherwise, it doesn't hurt to allocate much more space than you expect to use (and don't forget null terminator bytes, etc). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_itoa @sa SDL_ultoa @sa SDL_ulltoa<br>`value`：the unsigned integer to convert.<br>`str`：the buffer to write the string into.<br>`radix`：the radix to use for string generation.<br>返回：`str`. |
| <a id="api-b5fa1525e4a7b689"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Ulltoa(ulong value, sbyte* str, int radix)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46191) | Convert an unsigned long long integer into a string. This requires a radix to specified for string format. Specifying 10 produces a decimal number, 16 hexadecimal, etc. Must be in the range of 2 to 36. Note that this function will overflow a buffer if `str` is not large enough to hold the output! It may be safer to use SDL_snprintf to clamp output, or SDL_asprintf to allocate a buffer. Otherwise, it doesn't hurt to allocate much more space than you expect to use (and don't forget null terminator bytes, etc). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_lltoa @sa SDL_uitoa @sa SDL_ultoa<br>`value`：the unsigned long long integer to convert.<br>`str`：the buffer to write the string into.<br>`radix`：the radix to use for string generation.<br>返回：`str`. |
| <a id="api-c64b635d76dbd495"></a>[`public sbyte* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Ultoa(uint value, sbyte* str, int radix)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L39895) | Convert an unsigned long integer into a string. This requires a radix to specified for string format. Specifying 10 produces a decimal number, 16 hexadecimal, etc. Must be in the range of 2 to 36. Note that this function will overflow a buffer if `str` is not large enough to hold the output! It may be safer to use SDL_snprintf to clamp output, or SDL_asprintf to allocate a buffer. Otherwise, it doesn't hurt to allocate much more space than you expect to use (and don't forget null terminator bytes, etc). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ltoa @sa SDL_uitoa @sa SDL_ulltoa<br>`value`：the unsigned long integer to convert.<br>`str`：the buffer to write the string into.<br>`radix`：the radix to use for string generation.<br>返回：`str`. |
| <a id="api-431a26fe0594a397"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnbindAudioStream(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49918) | Unbind a single audio stream from its audio device. This is a convenience function, equivalent to calling `SDL_UnbindAudioStreams( &stream , 1)`. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_BindAudioStream<br>`stream`：an audio stream to unbind from a device. Can be NULL. |
| <a id="api-02415310a79203e9"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnbindAudioStreams(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream** streams, int num_streams)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43468) | Unbind a list of audio streams from their audio devices. The streams being unbound do not all have to be on the same device. All streams on the same device will be unbound atomically (data will stop flowing through all unbound streams on the same device at the same time). Unbinding a stream that isn't bound to a device is a legal no-op. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_BindAudioStreams<br>`streams`：an array of audio streams to unbind. Can be NULL or contain NULL.<br>`num_streams`：number streams listed in the `streams` array. |
| <a id="api-8c6978654aaf63e8"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnloadObject(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSharedObject* handle)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42933) | Unload a shared object from memory. Note that any pointers from this object looked up through SDL_LoadFunction() will no longer be valid. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LoadObject<br>`handle`：a valid shared object handle returned by SDL_LoadObject(). |
| <a id="api-f121294085a3beb8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnlockAudioStream(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAudioStream* stream)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48321) | Unlock an audio stream for serialized access. This unlocks an audio stream after a call to SDL_LockAudioStream. @threadsafety You should only call this from the same thread that previously called SDL_LockAudioStream. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockAudioStream<br>`stream`：the audio stream to unlock.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-062967c2c3c7f9a9"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnlockJoysticks()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40219) | Unlocking for atomic access to the joystick API. @threadsafety This should be called from the same thread that called SDL_LockJoysticks(). @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. |
| <a id="api-fda730c80c3a853f"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnlockMutex(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLMutex* mutex)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34662) | Unlock the mutex. It is legal for the owning thread to lock an already-locked mutex. It must unlock it the same number of times before it is actually made available for other threads in the system (this is known as a "recursive mutex"). It is illegal to unlock a mutex that has not been locked by the current thread, and doing so results in undefined behavior. @threadsafety This call must be paired with a previous locking call on the same thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockMutex @sa SDL_TryLockMutex<br>`mutex`：the mutex to unlock. |
| <a id="api-1b094288373537ee"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnlockProperties(uint props)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23632) | Unlock a group of properties. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockProperties<br>`props`：the properties to unlock. |
| <a id="api-72886fc1fa46a3da"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnlockRwLock(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRWLock* rwlock)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45934) | Unlock the read/write lock. Use this function to unlock the rwlock, whether it was locked for read-only or write operations. It is legal for the owning thread to lock an already-locked read-only lock. It must unlock it the same number of times before it is actually made available for other threads in the system (this is known as a "recursive rwlock"). It is illegal to unlock a rwlock that has not been locked by the current thread, and doing so results in undefined behavior. @threadsafety This call must be paired with a previous locking call on the same thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockRWLockForReading @sa SDL_LockRWLockForWriting @sa SDL_TryLockRWLockForReading @sa SDL_TryLockRWLockForWriting<br>`rwlock`：the rwlock to unlock. |
| <a id="api-ef4fc75511c805cf"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnlockSpinlock(int* @lock)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49595) | Unlock a spin lock by setting it to 0. Always returns immediately. ***Please note that spinlocks are dangerous if you don't know what you're doing. Please be careful using any sort of spinlock!*** @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockSpinlock @sa SDL_TryLockSpinlock<br>`lock`：a pointer to a lock variable. |
| <a id="api-d8bdeb1556192a98"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnlockSurface(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSurface* surface)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L30912) | Release a surface after directly accessing the pixels. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. The locking referred to by this function is making the pixels available for direct access, not thread-safe locking. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockSurface<br>`surface`：the SDL_Surface structure to be unlocked. |
| <a id="api-30766e4a796e4235"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnlockTexture(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29267) | Unlock a texture, uploading the changes to video memory, if needed. **Warning**: Please note that SDL_LockTexture() is intended to be write-only; it will not guarantee the previous contents of the texture will be provided. You must fully initialize any area of a texture that you lock before unlocking it, as the pixels might otherwise be uninitialized memory. Which is to say: locking and immediately unlocking a texture can result in corrupted textures, depending on the renderer in use. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockTexture<br>`texture`：a texture locked by SDL_LockTexture(). |
| <a id="api-1d67299e3729cf8a"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnmapGpuTransferBuffer(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTransferBuffer* transfer_buffer)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19083) | Unmaps a previously mapped transfer buffer. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`transfer_buffer`：a previously mapped transfer buffer. |
| <a id="api-c10a8a6fda51191d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnsetEnvironmentVariable(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEnvironment* env, sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42471) | Clear a variable from the environment. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetEnvironment @sa SDL_CreateEnvironment @sa SDL_GetEnvironmentVariable @sa SDL_GetEnvironmentVariables @sa SDL_SetEnvironmentVariable @sa SDL_UnsetEnvironmentVariable<br>`env`：the environment to modify.<br>`name`：the name of the variable to unset.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b1ce18af131e4d89"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UnsetenvUnsafe(sbyte* name)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L41421) | Clear a variable from the environment. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe, consider using SDL_UnsetEnvironmentVariable() instead. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_UnsetEnvironmentVariable<br>`name`：the name of the variable to unset.<br>返回：0 on success, -1 on error. |
| <a id="api-35b84764ddde2dd2"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UpdateGamepads()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21698) | Manually pump gamepad updates if not using the loop. This function is called automatically by the event loop if events are enabled. Under such circumstances, it will not be necessary to call this function. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. |
| <a id="api-68f13c90b5a18595"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UpdateHapticEffect(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHaptic* haptic, int effect, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLHapticEffect* data)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47437) | Update the properties of an effect. Can be used dynamically, although behavior when dynamically changing direction may be strange. Specifically the effect may re-upload itself and start playing from the start. You also cannot change the type either when running SDL_UpdateHapticEffect(). @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateHapticEffect @sa SDL_RunHapticEffect<br>`haptic`：the SDL_Haptic device that has the effect.<br>`effect`：the identifier of the effect to update.<br>`data`：an SDL_HapticEffect structure containing the new effect properties to use.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b39cc3980752a0b6"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UpdateJoysticks()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51352) | Update the current state of the open joysticks. This is called automatically by the event loop if any joystick events are enabled and SDL_HINT_AUTO_UPDATE_JOYSTICKS hasn't been set to "0". @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. |
| <a id="api-11995c7fb83a7f89"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UpdateNvTexture(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect, byte* Yplane, int Ypitch, byte* UVplane, int UVpitch)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31761) | Update a rectangle within a planar NV12 or NV21 texture with new pixels. You can use SDL_UpdateTexture() as long as your pixel data is a contiguous block of NV12/21 planes in the proper order, but this function is available if your pixel data is not contiguous. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_UpdateTexture @sa SDL_UpdateYUVTexture<br>`texture`：the texture to update.<br>`rect`：a pointer to the rectangle of pixels to update, or NULL to update the entire texture.<br>`Yplane`：the raw pixel data for the Y plane.<br>`Ypitch`：the number of bytes between rows of pixel data for the Y plane.<br>`UVplane`：the raw pixel data for the UV plane.<br>`UVpitch`：the number of bytes between rows of pixel data for the UV plane.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-ff7c5bf609b362f6"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UpdateSensors()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L21886) | Update the current state of the open sensors. This is called automatically by the event loop if sensor events are enabled. This needs to be called from the thread that initialized the sensor subsystem. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. |
| <a id="api-0079a29898aff153"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UpdateTexture(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect, void* pixels, int pitch)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40126) | Update the given texture rectangle with new pixel data. The pixel data must be in the pixel format of the texture, which can be queried using the SDL_PROP_TEXTURE_FORMAT_NUMBER property. This is a fairly slow function, intended for use with static textures that do not change often. If the texture is intended to be updated often, it is preferred to create the texture as streaming and use the locking functions referenced below. While this function will work with streaming textures, for optimization reasons you may not get the pixels back if you lock the texture afterward. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_LockTexture @sa SDL_UnlockTexture @sa SDL_UpdateNVTexture @sa SDL_UpdateYUVTexture<br>`texture`：the texture to update.<br>`rect`：an SDL_Rect structure representing the area to update, or NULL to update the entire texture.<br>`pixels`：the raw pixel data in the format of the texture.<br>`pitch`：the number of bytes in a row of pixel data, including padding between lines.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-d5dc3e2579305e3e"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UpdateTrays()`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L36789) | Update the trays. This is called automatically by the event loop and is only needed if you're using trays but aren't handling SDL events. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. |
| <a id="api-d2191b4f7bad5030"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UpdateWindowSurface(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L28548) | Copy the window surface to the screen. This is the function you use to reflect any changes to the surface on the screen. This function is equivalent to the SDL 1.2 API SDL_Flip(). @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowSurface @sa SDL_UpdateWindowSurfaceRects<br>`window`：the window to update.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0bb51488108febe8"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UpdateWindowSurfaceRects(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rects, int numrects)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L20017) | Copy areas of the window surface to the screen. This is the function you use to reflect changes to portions of the surface on the screen. This function is equivalent to the SDL 1.2 API SDL_UpdateRects(). Note that this function will update _at least_ the rectangles specified, but this is only intended as an optimization; in practice, this might update more of the screen (or all of the screen!), depending on what method SDL uses to send pixels to the system. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowSurface @sa SDL_UpdateWindowSurface<br>`window`：the window to update.<br>`rects`：an array of SDL_Rect structures representing areas of the surface to copy, in pixels.<br>`numrects`：the number of rectangles.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-126ed46b51d25440"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UpdateYuvTexture(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLTexture* texture, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLRect* rect, byte* Yplane, int Ypitch, byte* Uplane, int Upitch, byte* Vplane, int Vpitch)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L24736) | Update a rectangle within a planar YV12 or IYUV texture with new pixel data. You can use SDL_UpdateTexture() as long as your pixel data is a contiguous block of Y and U/V planes in the proper order, but this function is available if your pixel data is not contiguous. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_UpdateNVTexture @sa SDL_UpdateTexture<br>`texture`：the texture to update.<br>`rect`：a pointer to the rectangle of pixels to update, or NULL to update the entire texture.<br>`Yplane`：the raw pixel data for the Y plane.<br>`Ypitch`：the number of bytes between rows of pixel data for the Y plane.<br>`Uplane`：the raw pixel data for the U plane.<br>`Upitch`：the number of bytes between rows of pixel data for the U plane.<br>`Vplane`：the raw pixel data for the V plane.<br>`Vpitch`：the number of bytes between rows of pixel data for the V plane.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-592ccc9e220eac95"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UploadToGpuBuffer(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCopyPass* copy_pass, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTransferBufferLocation* source, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUBufferRegion* destination, byte cycle)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L43730) | Uploads data from a transfer buffer to a buffer. The upload occurs on the GPU timeline. You may assume that the upload has finished in subsequent commands. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`copy_pass`：a copy pass handle.<br>`source`：the source transfer buffer with offset.<br>`destination`：the destination buffer with offset and size.<br>`cycle`：if true, cycles the buffer if it is already bound, otherwise overwrites the data. |
| <a id="api-8d57edccab55d3ae"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.UploadToGpuTexture(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCopyPass* copy_pass, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureTransferInfo* source, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTextureRegion* destination, byte cycle)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52780) | Uploads data from a transfer buffer to a texture. The upload occurs on the GPU timeline. You may assume that the upload has finished in subsequent commands. You must align the data in the transfer buffer to a multiple of the texel size of the texture format. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`copy_pass`：a copy pass handle.<br>`source`：the source transfer buffer with image layout information.<br>`destination`：the destination texture region.<br>`cycle`：if true, cycles the texture if the texture is bound, otherwise overwrites the data. |
| <a id="api-6e23c0906bd1bf62"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Utf8strlcpy(sbyte* dst, sbyte* src, ulong dst_bytes)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22100) | Copy an UTF-8 string. This function copies up to `dst_bytes` - 1 bytes from `src` to `dst` while also ensuring that the string written to `dst` does not end in a truncated multi-byte sequence. Finally, it appends a null terminator. `src` and `dst` must not overlap. Note that unlike SDL_strlcpy(), this function returns the number of bytes written, not the length of `src`. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_strlcpy<br>`dst`：The destination buffer. Must not be NULL, and must not overlap with `src`.<br>`src`：The null-terminated UTF-8 string to copy. Must not be NULL, and must not overlap with `dst`.<br>`dst_bytes`：The length (in bytes) of the destination buffer. Must not be 0.<br>返回：the number of bytes written, excluding the null terminator. |
| <a id="api-60e24b5fac74c151"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Utf8strlen(sbyte* str)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L45579) | Count the number of codepoints in a UTF-8 string. Counts the _codepoints_, not _bytes_, in `str`, excluding the null terminator. If you need to count the bytes in a string instead, consider using SDL_strlen(). Since this handles Unicode, it expects the strings to be well-formed UTF-8 and not a null-terminated string of arbitrary bytes. Bytes that are not valid UTF-8 are treated as Unicode character U+FFFD (REPLACEMENT CHARACTER), so a malformed or incomplete UTF-8 sequence might increase the count by several replacement characters. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_utf8strnlen @sa SDL_strlen<br>`str`：The null-terminated UTF-8 string to read. Must not be NULL.<br>返回：The length (in codepoints, excluding the null terminator) of `src`. |
| <a id="api-61129141c7bcb3c0"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Utf8strnlen(sbyte* str, ulong bytes)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49731) | Count the number of codepoints in a UTF-8 string, up to n bytes. Counts the _codepoints_, not _bytes_, in `str`, excluding the null terminator. If you need to count the bytes in a string instead, consider using SDL_strnlen(). The counting stops at `bytes` bytes (not codepoints!). This seems counterintuitive, but makes it easy to express the total size of the string's buffer. Since this handles Unicode, it expects the strings to be well-formed UTF-8 and not a null-terminated string of arbitrary bytes. Bytes that are not valid UTF-8 are treated as Unicode character U+FFFD (REPLACEMENT CHARACTER), so a malformed or incomplete UTF-8 sequence might increase the count by several replacement characters. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_utf8strlen @sa SDL_strnlen<br>`str`：The null-terminated UTF-8 string to read. Must not be NULL.<br>`bytes`：The maximum amount of bytes to count.<br>返回：The length (in codepoints, excluding the null terminator) of `src` but never more than `maxlen`. |
| <a id="api-2e23131dda7907d7"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Vasprintf(sbyte** strp, sbyte* fmt, sbyte* ap)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17750) | This works exactly like vasprintf() but doesn't require access to a C runtime. Functions identically to SDL_asprintf(), except it takes a `va_list` instead of using `...` variable arguments. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`strp`：on output, is set to the new string. Must not be NULL.<br>`fmt`：a printf-style format string. Must not be NULL.<br>`ap`：a `va_list` values to be used with the format string.<br>返回：the number of bytes in the newly-allocated string, not counting the null-terminator char, or a negative value on error. |
| <a id="api-7709f2bc6acaa2fa"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Vsnprintf(sbyte* text, ulong maxlen, sbyte* fmt, sbyte* ap)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23104) | This works exactly like vsnprintf() but doesn't require access to a C runtime. Functions identically to SDL_snprintf(), except it takes a `va_list` instead of using `...` variable arguments. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`text`：the buffer to write the string into. Must not be NULL.<br>`maxlen`：the maximum bytes to write, including the null-terminator.<br>`fmt`：a printf-style format string. Must not be NULL.<br>`ap`：a `va_list` values to be used with the format string.<br>返回：the number of bytes that should be written, not counting the null-terminator char, or a negative value on error. |
| <a id="api-6aa4403117382274"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Vsscanf(sbyte* text, sbyte* fmt, sbyte* ap)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35304) | This works exactly like vsscanf() but doesn't require access to a C runtime. Functions identically to SDL_sscanf(), except it takes a `va_list` instead of using `...` variable arguments. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`text`：the string to scan. Must not be NULL.<br>`fmt`：a printf-style format string. Must not be NULL.<br>`ap`：a `va_list` of pointers to values to be filled in with scanned items.<br>返回：the number of items that matched the format string. |
| <a id="api-f6edc59c281a19ca"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Vswprintf(ushort* text, ulong maxlen, ushort* fmt, sbyte* ap)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L52596) | This works exactly like vswprintf() but doesn't require access to a C runtime. Functions identically to SDL_swprintf(), except it takes a `va_list` instead of using `...` variable arguments. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`text`：the buffer to write the string into. Must not be NULL.<br>`maxlen`：the maximum wide characters to write, including the null-terminator.<br>`fmt`：a printf-style format wide string. Must not be NULL.<br>`ap`：a `va_list` values to be used with the format string.<br>返回：the number of wide characters that should be written, not counting the null-terminator char, or a negative value on error. |
| <a id="api-325e7031384d67bc"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitAndAcquireGpuSwapchainTexture(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUCommandBuffer* command_buffer, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUTexture** swapchain_texture, uint* swapchain_texture_width, uint* swapchain_texture_height)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32736) | Blocks the thread until a swapchain texture is available to be acquired, and then acquires it. When a swapchain texture is acquired on a command buffer, it will automatically be submitted for presentation when the command buffer is submitted. The swapchain texture should only be referenced by the command buffer used to acquire it. It is an error to call SDL_CancelGPUCommandBuffer() after a swapchain texture is acquired. This function can fill the swapchain texture handle with NULL in certain cases, for example if the window is minimized. This is not an error. You should always make sure to check whether the pointer is NULL before actually using it. The swapchain texture is managed by the implementation and must not be freed by the user. You MUST NOT call this function from any thread other than the one that created the window. The swapchain texture is write-only and cannot be used as a sampler or for another reading operation. @threadsafety This function should only be called from the thread that created the window. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SubmitGPUCommandBuffer @sa SDL_SubmitGPUCommandBufferAndAcquireFence @sa SDL_AcquireGPUSwapchainTexture<br>`command_buffer`：a command buffer.<br>`window`：a window that has been claimed.<br>`swapchain_texture`：a pointer filled in with a swapchain texture handle.<br>`swapchain_texture_width`：a pointer filled in with the swapchain texture width, may be NULL.<br>`swapchain_texture_height`：a pointer filled in with the swapchain texture height, may be NULL.<br>返回：true on success, false on error; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-a1884a1f73f9099d"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitAsyncIoResult(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIOQueue* queue, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIOOutcome* outcome, int timeoutMS)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44702) | Block until an async I/O task queue has a completed task. This function puts the calling thread to sleep until there a task assigned to the queue that has finished. If a task assigned to the queue has finished, this will return true and fill in `outcome` with the details of the task. If no task in the queue has finished, this function will return false. If a task has completed, this function will free its resources and the task pointer will no longer be valid. The task will be removed from the queue. It is safe for multiple threads to call this function on the same queue at once; a completed task will only go to one of the threads. Note that by the nature of various platforms, more than one waiting thread may wake to handle a single task, but only one will obtain it, so `timeoutMS` is a _maximum_ wait time, and this function may return false sooner. This function may return false if there was a system error, the OS inadvertently awoke multiple threads, or if SDL_SignalAsyncIOQueue() was called to wake up all waiting threads without a finished task. A timeout can be used to specify a maximum wait time, but rather than polling, it is possible to have a timeout of -1 to wait forever, and use SDL_SignalAsyncIOQueue() to wake up the waiting threads later. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SignalAsyncIOQueue<br>`queue`：the async I/O task queue to wait on.<br>`outcome`：details of a finished task will be written here. May not be NULL.<br>`timeoutMS`：the maximum time to wait, in milliseconds, or -1 to wait indefinitely.<br>返回：true if task has completed, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-5b5964e7793e723d"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitCondition(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCondition* cond, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLMutex* mutex)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47099) | Wait until a condition variable is signaled. This function unlocks the specified `mutex` and waits for another thread to call SDL_SignalCondition() or SDL_BroadcastCondition() on the condition variable `cond`. Once the condition variable is signaled, the mutex is re-locked and the function returns. The mutex must be locked before calling this function. Locking the mutex recursively (more than once) is not supported and leads to undefined behavior. This function is the equivalent of calling SDL_WaitConditionTimeout() with a time length of -1. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_BroadcastCondition @sa SDL_SignalCondition @sa SDL_WaitConditionTimeout<br>`cond`：the condition variable to wait on.<br>`mutex`：the mutex used to coordinate thread access. |
| <a id="api-4e020e9fccc15ecf"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitConditionTimeout(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLCondition* cond, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLMutex* mutex, int timeoutMS)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47307) | Wait until a condition variable is signaled or a certain time has passed. This function unlocks the specified `mutex` and waits for another thread to call SDL_SignalCondition() or SDL_BroadcastCondition() on the condition variable `cond`, or for the specified time to elapse. Once the condition variable is signaled or the time elapsed, the mutex is re-locked and the function returns. The mutex must be locked before calling this function. Locking the mutex recursively (more than once) is not supported and leads to undefined behavior. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_BroadcastCondition @sa SDL_SignalCondition @sa SDL_WaitCondition<br>`cond`：the condition variable to wait on.<br>`mutex`：the mutex used to coordinate thread access.<br>`timeoutMS`：the maximum time to wait, in milliseconds, or -1 to wait indefinitely.<br>返回：true if the condition variable is signaled, false if the condition is not signaled in the allotted time. |
| <a id="api-4d64dd64656f184c"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitEvent(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEvent* @event)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L23072) | Wait indefinitely for the next available event. If `event` is not NULL, the next event is removed from the queue and stored in the SDL_Event structure pointed to by `event`. As this function may implicitly call SDL_PumpEvents(), you can only call this function in the thread that initialized the video subsystem. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PollEvent @sa SDL_PushEvent @sa SDL_WaitEventTimeout<br>`event`：the SDL_Event structure to be filled in with the next event from the queue, or NULL.<br>返回：true on success or false if there was an error while waiting for events; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-b512ee28c4add8f4"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitEventTimeout(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLEvent* @event, int timeoutMS)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L38942) | Wait until the specified timeout (in milliseconds) for the next available event. If `event` is not NULL, the next event is removed from the queue and stored in the SDL_Event structure pointed to by `event`. As this function may implicitly call SDL_PumpEvents(), you can only call this function in the thread that initialized the video subsystem. The timeout is not guaranteed, the actual wait time could be longer due to system scheduling. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_PollEvent @sa SDL_PushEvent @sa SDL_WaitEvent<br>`event`：the SDL_Event structure to be filled in with the next event from the queue, or NULL.<br>`timeoutMS`：the maximum number of milliseconds to wait for the next available event.<br>返回：true if this got an event or false if the timeout elapsed without any events available. |
| <a id="api-34d42eac933c42a6"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitForGpuFences(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, byte wait_all, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUFence** fences, uint num_fences)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44348) | Blocks the thread until the given fences are signaled. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SubmitGPUCommandBufferAndAcquireFence @sa SDL_WaitForGPUIdle<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`wait_all`：if 0, wait for any fence to be signaled, if 1, wait for all fences to be signaled.<br>`fences`：an array of fences to wait on.<br>`num_fences`：the number of fences in the fences array.<br>返回：true on success, false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-08fac544fec1c612"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitForGpuIdle(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17936) | Blocks the thread until the GPU is completely idle. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_WaitForGPUFences<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>返回：true on success, false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-9a84d95202bf83c7"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitForGpuSwapchain(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L42807) | Blocks the thread until a swapchain texture is available to be acquired. @threadsafety This function should only be called from the thread that created the window. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AcquireGPUSwapchainTexture @sa SDL_WaitAndAcquireGPUSwapchainTexture @sa SDL_SetGPUAllowedFramesInFlight<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`window`：a window that has been claimed.<br>返回：true on success, false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-c43ba76de414d7f3"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitProcess(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLProcess* process, byte block, int* exitcode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L33638) | Wait for a process to finish. This can be called multiple times to get the status of a process. The exit code will be the exit code of the process if it terminates normally, a negative signal if it terminated due to a signal, or -255 otherwise. It will not be changed if the process is still running. If you create a process with standard output piped to the application (`pipe_stdio` being true) then you should read all of the process output before calling SDL_WaitProcess(). If you don't do this the process might be blocked indefinitely waiting for output to be read and SDL_WaitProcess() will never return true; @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateProcess @sa SDL_CreateProcessWithProperties @sa SDL_KillProcess @sa SDL_DestroyProcess<br>`process`：The process to wait for.<br>`block`：If true, block until the process finishes; otherwise, report on the process' status.<br>`exitcode`：a pointer filled in with the process exit code if the process has exited, may be NULL.<br>返回：true if the process exited, false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-87d8a5f7ea932af0"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitSemaphore(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSemaphore* sem)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L34606) | Wait until a semaphore has a positive value and then decrements it. This function suspends the calling thread until the semaphore pointed to by `sem` has a positive value, and then atomically decrement the semaphore value. This function is the equivalent of calling SDL_WaitSemaphoreTimeout() with a time length of -1. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SignalSemaphore @sa SDL_TryWaitSemaphore @sa SDL_WaitSemaphoreTimeout<br>`sem`：the semaphore wait on. |
| <a id="api-250458c819479df6"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitSemaphoreTimeout(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLSemaphore* sem, int timeoutMS)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22474) | Wait until a semaphore has a positive value and then decrements it. This function suspends the calling thread until either the semaphore pointed to by `sem` has a positive value or the specified time has elapsed. If the call is successful it will atomically decrement the semaphore value. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_SignalSemaphore @sa SDL_TryWaitSemaphore @sa SDL_WaitSemaphore<br>`sem`：the semaphore to wait on.<br>`timeoutMS`：the length of the timeout, in milliseconds, or -1 to wait indefinitely.<br>返回：true if the wait succeeds or false if the wait times out. |
| <a id="api-d45b05a194db88ae"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WaitThread(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLThread* thread, int* status)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22030) | Wait for a thread to finish. Threads that haven't been detached will remain until this function cleans them up. Not doing so is a resource leak. Once a thread has been cleaned up through this function, the SDL_Thread that references it becomes invalid and should not be referenced again. As such, only one thread may call SDL_WaitThread() on another. The return code from the thread function is placed in the area pointed to by `status`, if `status` is not NULL. You may not wait on a thread that has been used in a call to SDL_DetachThread(). Use either that function or this one, but not both, or behavior is undefined. It is safe to pass a NULL thread to this function; it is a no-op. Note that the thread pointer is freed by this function and is not valid afterward. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread, but only a single thread can wait any specific thread to finish. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_CreateThread @sa SDL_DetachThread<br>`thread`：the SDL_Thread pointer that was returned from the SDL_CreateThread() call that started this thread.<br>`status`：a pointer filled in with the value returned from the thread function by its 'return', or -1 if the thread has been detached or isn't valid, may be NULL. |
| <a id="api-555a6227f7f22e55"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WarpMouseGlobal(float x, float y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19893) | Move the mouse to the given position in global screen space. This function generates a mouse motion event. A failure of this function usually means that it is unsupported by a platform. Note that this function will appear to succeed, but not actually move the mouse when used over Microsoft Remote Desktop. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_WarpMouseInWindow<br>`x`：the x coordinate.<br>`y`：the y coordinate.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-651121128f576b8a"></a>[`public void Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WarpMouseInWindow(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, float x, float y)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19981) | Move the mouse cursor to the given position within the window. This function generates a mouse motion event if relative mode is not enabled. If relative mode is enabled, you can force mouse events for the warp by setting the SDL_HINT_MOUSE_RELATIVE_WARP_MOTION hint. Note that this function will appear to succeed, but not actually move the mouse when used over Microsoft Remote Desktop. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_WarpMouseGlobal<br>`window`：the window to move the mouse into, or NULL for the current mouse focus.<br>`x`：the x coordinate within the window.<br>`y`：the y coordinate within the window. |
| <a id="api-6fcc0e3c51ae6999"></a>[`public uint Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WasInit(uint flags)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L19199) | Get a mask of the specified subsystems which are currently initialized. @threadsafety This function is not thread safe. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_Init @sa SDL_InitSubSystem<br>`flags`：any of the flags used by SDL_Init(); see SDL_Init for details.<br>返回：a mask of all initialized subsystems if `flags` is 0, otherwise it returns the initialization status of the specified subsystems. |
| <a id="api-576ca78ba6b5e91c"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcscasecmp(ushort* str1, ushort* str2)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L44317) | Compare two null-terminated wide strings, case-insensitively. This will work with Unicode strings, using a technique called "case-folding" to handle the vast majority of case-sensitive human languages regardless of system locale. It can deal with expanding values: a German Eszett character can compare against two ASCII 's' chars and be considered a match, for example. A notable exception: it does not handle the Turkish 'i' character; human language is complicated! Depending on your platform, "wchar_t" might be 2 bytes, and expected to be UTF-16 encoded (like Windows), or 4 bytes in UTF-32 format. Since this handles Unicode, it expects the string to be well-formed and not a null-terminated string of arbitrary bytes. Characters that are not valid UTF-16 (or UTF-32) are treated as Unicode character U+FFFD (REPLACEMENT CHARACTER), which is to say two strings of random bits may turn out to match if they convert to the same amount of replacement characters. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str1`：the first string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`str2`：the second string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>返回：less than zero if str1 is "less than" str2, greater than zero if str1 is "greater than" str2, and zero if the strings match exactly. |
| <a id="api-bef328a8934ea588"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcscmp(ushort* str1, ushort* str2)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L31947) | Compare two null-terminated wide strings. This only compares wchar_t values until it hits a null-terminating character; it does not care if the string is well-formed UTF-16 (or UTF-32, depending on your platform's wchar_t size), or uses valid Unicode values. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str1`：the first string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`str2`：the second string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>返回：less than zero if str1 is "less than" str2, greater than zero if str1 is "greater than" str2, and zero if the strings match exactly. |
| <a id="api-d0337f623ea95330"></a>[`public ushort* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcsdup(ushort* wstr)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L18465) | Allocate a copy of a wide string. This allocates enough space for a null-terminated copy of `wstr`, using SDL_malloc, and then makes a copy of the string into this space. The returned string is owned by the caller, and should be passed to SDL_free when no longer needed. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`wstr`：the string to copy.<br>返回：a pointer to the newly-allocated wide string. |
| <a id="api-549babc533406e9e"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcslcat(ushort* dst, ushort* src, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L32159) | Concatenate wide strings. This function appends up to `maxlen` - SDL_wcslen(dst) - 1 wide characters from `src` to the end of the wide string in `dst`, then appends a null terminator. `src` and `dst` must not overlap. If `maxlen` - SDL_wcslen(dst) - 1 is less than or equal to 0, then `dst` is unmodified. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_wcslcpy<br>`dst`：The destination buffer already containing the first null-terminated wide string. Must not be NULL and must not overlap with `src`.<br>`src`：The second null-terminated wide string. Must not be NULL, and must not overlap with `dst`.<br>`maxlen`：The length (in wide characters) of the destination buffer.<br>返回：the length (in wide characters, excluding the null terminator) of the string in `dst` plus the length of `src`. |
| <a id="api-194e45f02731882b"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcslcpy(ushort* dst, ushort* src, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L46889) | Copy a wide string. This function copies `maxlen` - 1 wide characters from `src` to `dst`, then appends a null terminator. `src` and `dst` must not overlap. If `maxlen` is 0, no wide characters are copied and no null terminator is written. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_wcslcat<br>`dst`：The destination buffer. Must not be NULL, and must not overlap with `src`.<br>`src`：The null-terminated wide string to copy. Must not be NULL, and must not overlap with `dst`.<br>`maxlen`：The length (in wide characters) of the destination buffer.<br>返回：the length (in wide characters, excluding the null terminator) of `src`. |
| <a id="api-41fce161d726a6f5"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcslen(ushort* wstr)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L49223) | This works exactly like wcslen() but doesn't require access to a C runtime. Counts the number of wchar_t values in `wstr`, excluding the null terminator. Like SDL_strlen only counts bytes and not codepoints in a UTF-8 string, this counts wchar_t values in a string, even if the string's encoding is of variable width, like UTF-16. Also be aware that wchar_t is different sizes on different platforms (4 bytes on Linux, 2 on Windows, etc). @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_wcsnlen @sa SDL_utf8strlen @sa SDL_utf8strnlen<br>`wstr`：The null-terminated wide string to read. Must not be NULL.<br>返回：the length (in wchar_t values, excluding the null terminator) of `wstr`. |
| <a id="api-6e5cff440636d437"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcsncasecmp(ushort* str1, ushort* str2, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L35582) | Compare two wide strings, case-insensitively, up to a number of wchar_t. This will work with Unicode strings, using a technique called "case-folding" to handle the vast majority of case-sensitive human languages regardless of system locale. It can deal with expanding values: a German Eszett character can compare against two ASCII 's' chars and be considered a match, for example. A notable exception: it does not handle the Turkish 'i' character; human language is complicated! Depending on your platform, "wchar_t" might be 2 bytes, and expected to be UTF-16 encoded (like Windows), or 4 bytes in UTF-32 format. Since this handles Unicode, it expects the string to be well-formed and not a null-terminated string of arbitrary bytes. Characters that are not valid UTF-16 (or UTF-32) are treated as Unicode character U+FFFD (REPLACEMENT CHARACTER), which is to say two strings of random bits may turn out to match if they convert to the same amount of replacement characters. Note that while this function might deal with variable-sized characters, `maxlen` specifies a _wchar_ limit! If the limit lands in the middle of a multi-byte UTF-16 sequence, it may convert a portion of the final character to one or more Unicode character U+FFFD (REPLACEMENT CHARACTER) so as not to overflow a buffer. `maxlen` specifies a maximum number of wchar_t values to compare; if the strings match to this number of wchar_t (or both have matched to a null-terminator character before this number of bytes), they will be considered equal. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str1`：the first string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`str2`：the second string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`maxlen`：the maximum number of wchar_t values to compare.<br>返回：less than zero if str1 is "less than" str2, greater than zero if str1 is "greater than" str2, and zero if the strings match exactly. |
| <a id="api-b517dc47e6f48170"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcsncmp(ushort* str1, ushort* str2, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L22720) | Compare two wide strings up to a number of wchar_t values. This only compares wchar_t values; it does not care if the string is well-formed UTF-16 (or UTF-32, depending on your platform's wchar_t size), or uses valid Unicode values. Note that while this function is intended to be used with UTF-16 (or UTF-32, depending on your platform's definition of wchar_t), it is comparing raw wchar_t values and not Unicode codepoints: `maxlen` specifies a wchar_t limit! If the limit lands in the middle of a multi-wchar UTF-16 sequence, it will only compare a portion of the final character. `maxlen` specifies a maximum number of wchar_t to compare; if the strings match to this number of wide chars (or both have matched to a null-terminator character before this count), they will be considered equal. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`str1`：the first string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`str2`：the second string to compare. NULL is not permitted!<br>`maxlen`：the maximum number of wchar_t to compare.<br>返回：less than zero if str1 is "less than" str2, greater than zero if str1 is "greater than" str2, and zero if the strings match exactly. |
| <a id="api-53c08d83608ce2e4"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcsnlen(ushort* wstr, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L17854) | This works exactly like wcsnlen() but doesn't require access to a C runtime. Counts up to a maximum of `maxlen` wchar_t values in `wstr`, excluding the null terminator. Like SDL_strnlen only counts bytes and not codepoints in a UTF-8 string, this counts wchar_t values in a string, even if the string's encoding is of variable width, like UTF-16. Also be aware that wchar_t is different sizes on different platforms (4 bytes on Linux, 2 on Windows, etc). Also, `maxlen` is a count of wide characters, not bytes! @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_wcslen @sa SDL_utf8strlen @sa SDL_utf8strnlen<br>`wstr`：The null-terminated wide string to read. Must not be NULL.<br>`maxlen`：The maximum amount of wide characters to count.<br>返回：the length (in wide characters, excluding the null terminator) of `wstr` but never more than `maxlen`. |
| <a id="api-3b754d4a69a1dcc2"></a>[`public ushort* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcsnstr(ushort* haystack, ushort* needle, ulong maxlen)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40450) | Search a wide string, up to n wide chars, for the first instance of a specific substring. The search ends once it finds the requested substring, or a null terminator value to end the string, or `maxlen` wide character have been examined. It is possible to use this function on a wide string without a null terminator. Note that this looks for strings of _wide characters_, not _codepoints_, so it's legal to search for malformed and incomplete UTF-16 sequences. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`haystack`：the wide string to search. Must not be NULL.<br>`needle`：the wide string to search for. Must not be NULL.<br>`maxlen`：the maximum number of wide characters to search in `haystack`.<br>返回：a pointer to the first instance of `needle` in the string, or NULL if not found. |
| <a id="api-ff3b6b03fd332bd7"></a>[`public ushort* Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcsstr(ushort* haystack, ushort* needle)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47625) | Search a wide string for the first instance of a specific substring. The search ends once it finds the requested substring, or a null terminator byte to end the string. Note that this looks for strings of _wide characters_, not _codepoints_, so it's legal to search for malformed and incomplete UTF-16 sequences. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`haystack`：the wide string to search. Must not be NULL.<br>`needle`：the wide string to search for. Must not be NULL.<br>返回：a pointer to the first instance of `needle` in the string, or NULL if not found. |
| <a id="api-d2075b457c86cdef"></a>[`public int Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.Wcstol(ushort* str, ushort** endp, int @base)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L47501) | Parse a `long` from a wide string. If `str` starts with whitespace, then those whitespace characters are skipped before attempting to parse the number. If the parsed number does not fit inside a `long`, the result is clamped to the minimum and maximum representable `long` values. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_strtol<br>`str`：The null-terminated wide string to read. Must not be NULL.<br>`endp`：If not NULL, the address of the first invalid wide character (i.e. the next character after the parsed number) will be written to this pointer.<br>`base`：The base of the integer to read. Supported values are 0 and 2 to 36 inclusive. If 0, the base will be inferred from the number's prefix (0x for hexadecimal, 0 for octal, decimal otherwise).<br>返回：the parsed `long`, or 0 if no number could be parsed. |
| <a id="api-83e5285322a6ba94"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WindowHasSurface(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L48494) | Return whether the window has a surface associated with it. @threadsafety This function should only be called on the main thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_GetWindowSurface<br>`window`：the window to query.<br>返回：true if there is a surface associated with the window, or false otherwise. |
| <a id="api-58403a99e4f40d6a"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WindowSupportsGpuPresentMode(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUPresentMode present_mode)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L29641) | Determines whether a presentation mode is supported by the window. The window must be claimed before calling this function. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ClaimWindowForGPUDevice<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`window`：an SDL_Window.<br>`present_mode`：the presentation mode to check.<br>返回：true if supported, false if unsupported. |
| <a id="api-cc47beb6fa532fbe"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WindowSupportsGpuSwapchainComposition(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUDevice* device, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLWindow* window, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLGPUSwapchainComposition swapchain_composition)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L40527) | Determines whether a swapchain composition is supported by the window. The window must be claimed before calling this function. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ClaimWindowForGPUDevice<br>`device`：a GPU context.<br>`window`：an SDL_Window.<br>`swapchain_composition`：the swapchain composition to check.<br>返回：true if supported, false if unsupported. |
| <a id="api-9b80629f4fded0d1"></a>[`public byte Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WriteAsyncIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIO* asyncio, void* ptr, ulong offset, ulong size, Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLAsyncIOQueue* queue, void* userdata)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51083) | Start an async write. This function writes `size` bytes from `offset` position in the data source to the area pointed at by `ptr`. This function returns as quickly as possible; it does not wait for the write to complete. On a successful return, this work will continue in the background. If the work begins, even failure is asynchronous: a failing return value from this function only means the work couldn't start at all. `ptr` must remain available until the work is done, and may be accessed by the system at any time until then. Do not allocate it on the stack, as this might take longer than the life of the calling function to complete! An SDL_AsyncIOQueue must be specified. The newly-created task will be added to it when it completes its work. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_ReadAsyncIO @sa SDL_CreateAsyncIOQueue<br>`asyncio`：a pointer to an SDL_AsyncIO structure.<br>`ptr`：a pointer to a buffer to write data from.<br>`offset`：the position to start writing to the data source.<br>`size`：the number of bytes to write to the data source.<br>`queue`：a queue to add the new SDL_AsyncIO to.<br>`userdata`：an app-defined pointer that will be provided with the task results.<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-0d191f12b30caf7f"></a>[`public ulong Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.RawApi.WriteIo(Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLIOStream* context, void* ptr, ulong size)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L51944) | Write to an SDL_IOStream data stream. This function writes exactly `size` bytes from the area pointed at by `ptr` to the stream. If this fails for any reason, it'll return less than `size` to demonstrate how far the write progressed. On success, it returns `size`. On error, this function still attempts to write as much as possible, so it might return a positive value less than the requested write size. The caller can use SDL_GetIOStatus() to determine if the problem is recoverable, such as a non-blocking write that can simply be retried later, or a fatal error. A request for zero bytes on a valid stream will return zero immediately without accessing the stream, so the stream status (EOF, err, etc) will not change. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_IOprintf @sa SDL_ReadIO @sa SDL_SeekIO @sa SDL_FlushIO @sa SDL_GetIOStatus<br>`context`：a pointer to an SDL_IOStream structure.<br>`ptr`：a pointer to a buffer containing data to write.<br>`size`：the number of bytes to write.<br>返回：the number of bytes written, which will be less than `size` on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |

SDL byteswaps the data only if necessary, so the application always specifies native format, and the data written will be in big-endian format. @threadsafety Do not use the same SDL_IOStream from two threads at once. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0.<br>`dst`：the stream to which data will be written.<br>`value`：the data to be written, in native format.<br>返回：true on successful write or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |

| <a id="api-6e35d37876957ad8"></a>[`public bool Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.ResumeAudioDevice(uint devid)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L12910) | Use this function to unpause audio playback on a specified device. This function unpauses audio processing for a given device that has previously been paused with SDL_PauseAudioDevice(). Once unpaused, any bound audio streams will begin to progress again, and audio can be generated. Unlike in SDL2, audio devices start in an _unpaused_ state, since an app has to bind a stream before any audio will flow. Unpausing an unpaused device is a legal no-op. Physical devices can not be paused or unpaused, only logical devices created through SDL_OpenAudioDevice() can be. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_AudioDevicePaused @sa SDL_PauseAudioDevice<br>`devid`：a device opened by SDL_OpenAudioDevice().<br>返回：true on success or false on failure; call SDL_GetError() for more information. |
| <a id="api-7ecbff1779200dce"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Round(double x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L13965) | Round `x` to the nearest integer. Rounds `x` to the nearest integer. Values halfway between integers will be rounded away from zero. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_roundf for single-precision floats. To get the result as an integer type, use SDL_lround. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_roundf @sa SDL_lround @sa SDL_floor @sa SDL_ceil @sa SDL_trunc<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：the nearest integer to `x`. |
| <a id="api-df65f10c105d1382"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Roundf(float x)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L11643) | Round `x` to the nearest integer. Rounds `x` to the nearest integer. Values halfway between integers will be rounded away from zero. Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF` Range: `-INF < = y < = INF`, y integer This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_round for double-precision floats. To get the result as an integer type, use SDL_lroundf. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_round @sa SDL_lroundf @sa SDL_floorf @sa SDL_ceilf @sa SDL_truncf<br>`x`：floating point value.<br>返回：the nearest integer to `x`. |
| <a id="api-729a58616aaaa229"></a>[`public double Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Scalbn(double x, int n)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L15773) | Scale `x` by an integer power of two. Multiplies `x` by the `n`th power of the floating point radix (always 2). Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `n` integer Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` This function operates on double-precision floating point values, use SDL_scalbnf for single-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_scalbnf @sa SDL_pow<br>`x`：floating point value to be scaled.<br>`n`：integer exponent.<br>返回：`x * 2^n`. |
| <a id="api-ec3dc1b3f1a5bf9a"></a>[`public float Inno.Native.Sdl3.SDLContext.Scalbnf(float x, int n)`](../../../../backends/Sdl3/native/Inno.Native.Sdl3/Generated/Bindings.cs#L14459) | Scale `x` by an integer power of two. Multiplies `x` by the `n`th power of the floating point radix (always 2). Domain: `-INF < = x < = INF`, `n` integer Range: `-INF < = y < = INF` This function operates on single-precision floating point values, use SDL_scalbn for double-precision floats. @threadsafety It is safe to call this function from any thread. @since This function is available since SDL 3.2.0. @sa SDL_scalbn @sa SDL_powf<br>`x`：floating point value to be scaled.<br>`n`：integer exponent.<br>返回：`x * 2^n`. |
